执行记录（2026-11-25）：按计划落地，相对计划有五处调整（前两处登记在 §0 执行偏差表）：
- 插话队列：`take_queued_interventions` 只取出、不改状态；`attach_intervention_turn`（原计划在任务 B6）挪到这里，由它标为已注入并记下对应的发言。
- 找不到行时抛 `LookupError`，不再静默返回（`get_meeting`、`update_meeting`、`set_state`、`finish_turn`、`update_participant`、`update_decision_point`、`attach_intervention_turn`）。
- 计划的测试里 `add_participant(...)` 漏了必填的 `mode`，照原样跑会报 `TypeError`；已补。
- 去掉了计划测试里一句拿发言 id 和序号比较的断言（`add_turn` 返回的是行 id，不是序号），改为断言读回的序号是 1、2。
- 另补了几处断言：没说完的发言不算数；被移出名单的参会者默认不列出；`finished_at=True` 会记下时间；表态矩阵的键是参会者 id 的字符串。最后一点要特别注意：`stances_json` 是 JSON 列，整数键读出来会变成字符串，后面的代码一律按 `str(参会者 id)` 存取。

四类行为做过变异检查。本任务 6 passed。

### Task B3: 协议基类、会话、运行器入口与会议内确认点

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/app/meeting/protocols/__init__.py`
- Create: `backend/app/meeting/protocols/base.py`
- Create: `backend/app/meeting/checkpoints.py`
- Create: `backend/app/meeting/session.py`
- Create: `backend/app/meeting/runner.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/engine/gates.py`（`gate_payload`）
- Modify: `backend/app/engine/runners.py`（`execute_node` 加一个分支）
- Modify: `backend/app/engine/orchestrator.py`（`_run_attempt` 末尾加"挂起"分支）
- Test: `backend/tests/test_meeting_runner.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 任务 B2 的存取函数
- Produces: `StepResult(action, next=None, checkpoint=None, reroute=None, summary="", extra=None)`；辅助构造 `advance(next=None)`、`suspend(checkpoint)`、`reroute(targets, feedback, self_feedback=None, summary="", extra_round=False)`、`done(summary, **extra)`
- Produces: `async reroute_or_escalate(mtg, targets, feedback, *, self_feedback=None, title, description, summary="") -> StepResult | None`（回流轮次没用完就回流；用完了先开人工确认点）
- Produces: `MeetingProtocol`：类属性 `name: str`、`steps: tuple[str, ...]`；每个步骤是 `async step_<名字>(mtg) -> StepResult`；`async run(mtg) -> StepResult`；可覆写的 `async on_enter(mtg) -> None`（带着反馈续开时在这里取走反馈）
- Produces: `register_protocol(cls)`（装饰器）、`get_protocol(name) -> MeetingProtocol`
- Produces: `MeetingSession(meeting_id, ctx)`，方法见 Step 5；其中 `max_rounds()` 是节点级的回流轮次上限
- Produces: `async open_checkpoint(mtg, kind, *, title, description, payload) -> dict`；`get_decision(gate_id) -> dict | None`；`async record_auto_decision(mtg, kind, *, decided_by, decision, title="", payload=None) -> int`
- Produces: `async run_meeting(ctx: NodeContext) -> dict`，返回三种之一：`{"summary", "done": True, **extra}`、`{"suspended": True, "meeting_id", "checkpoint"}`、`{"reroute": {...}, "meeting_id"}`
- Produces: 门的载荷多三个字段：`kind`、`payload`、`decision`

- [x] **Step 1: 写失败的测试**

`backend/tests/test_meeting_runner.py`：

```python
"""会议运行器：步进循环、挂起与恢复、续开与重开的判定、会议内确认点、回流轮次用尽转人工。不调模型。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio

from app.db.base import init_db, session_scope
from app.db.models import Gate, Node, Run, Workflow
from app.engine.gates import get_gate
from app.engine.runners import NodeContext
from app.engine.templates import seed_defaults
from app.meeting import store
from app.meeting.protocols.base import (MeetingProtocol, advance, done, register_protocol,
                                        reroute_or_escalate, suspend)
from app.meeting.runner import run_meeting

_counter = iter(range(1, 1000))


@register_protocol
class ToyProtocol(MeetingProtocol):
    name = "toy"
    steps = ("first", "ask", "last")

    async def on_enter(self, mtg):
        feedback = mtg.consume_feedback()
        if feedback:
            seen = list(mtg.state().get("feedbacks") or [])
            mtg.set_state(feedbacks=seen + [feedback])

    async def step_first(self, mtg):
        mtg.set_state(ran_first=True)
        return advance()

    async def step_ask(self, mtg):
        decision = mtg.decision()
        if decision is None:
            return suspend(await mtg.open_checkpoint(
                "consensus", title="请确认", description="玩具确认点", payload={"q": 1}))
        mtg.set_state(answer=decision["action"])
        return advance()

    async def step_last(self, mtg):
        return done("玩具协议完成", artifact={"kind": "toy"})


@register_protocol
class LoopProtocol(MeetingProtocol):
    """一进来就要把上游定点重开的协议：用来验证回流轮次用尽时转人工。"""

    name = "loop"
    steps = ("check",)

    async def step_check(self, mtg):
        result = await reroute_or_escalate(
            mtg, ["upstream"], "还有未解决的问题", title="回流轮次已用尽",
            description="请选择：接受现状，或写明意见再评一轮")
        if result is None:
            return done("人接受了现状")
        return result


def _run(protocol: str = "toy", **config) -> tuple[str, int]:
    init_db()
    seed_defaults()
    index = next(_counter)
    with session_scope() as s:
        workflow_id = s.query(Workflow).filter(Workflow.template_key == "sdlc_v1").one().id
        run_id = f"runner-run-{index}"
        s.add(Run(id=run_id, workflow_id=workflow_id, status="running"))
        s.flush()
        node = Node(node_key=protocol, type="MEETING", name="玩具", run_id=run_id, status="pending",
                    config_json={"protocol": protocol, **config})
        s.add(node)
        s.flush()
        return run_id, node.id


def _ctx(run_id: str, node_id: int, protocol: str = "toy", **config) -> NodeContext:
    with session_scope() as s:
        node = s.get(Node, node_id)
        return NodeContext(run_id=run_id, node_id=node_id, node_key=protocol, type="MEETING",
                           name=node.name, phase="", config={**node.config_json, **config},
                           assignee={}, attempt_id=0, attempt_no=1)


def _decide(run_id: str, action: str, feedback: str = "") -> None:
    """模拟确认点已有决定（门上带着决定），节点回到待调度。"""
    with session_scope() as s:
        gate = s.query(Gate).filter(Gate.run_id == run_id, Gate.status == "open").one()
        gate.status = "approved" if action == "approve" else "rejected"
        gate.decision_json = {"action": action, "feedback": feedback, "data": {}}
        s.query(Node).filter(Node.run_id == run_id).one().status = "pending"


def test_suspends_then_resumes_from_decision() -> None:
    run_id, node_id = _run()
    first = asyncio.run(run_meeting(_ctx(run_id, node_id)))
    assert first["suspended"] is True
    meeting_id = first["meeting_id"]
    assert store.get_state(meeting_id)["ran_first"] is True
    assert store.get_meeting(meeting_id)["phase"] == "ask"
    assert store.get_meeting(meeting_id)["status"] == "suspended"
    gate = get_gate(store.get_state(meeting_id)["awaiting_gate_id"])
    assert gate["kind"] == "consensus" and gate["payload"] == {"title": "请确认",
                                                              "description": "玩具确认点", "q": 1}
    with session_scope() as s:
        assert s.get(Node, node_id).status == "waiting_human"

    _decide(run_id, "reject", feedback="继续")
    second = asyncio.run(run_meeting(_ctx(run_id, node_id)))
    assert second["done"] is True and second["summary"] == "玩具协议完成"
    assert second["artifact"] == {"kind": "toy"}
    assert store.get_state(meeting_id)["answer"] == "reject"
    assert store.get_meeting(meeting_id)["status"] == "completed"


def test_completed_meeting_without_feedback_is_restarted() -> None:
    run_id, node_id = _run()
    first = asyncio.run(run_meeting(_ctx(run_id, node_id)))
    _decide(run_id, "approve")
    asyncio.run(run_meeting(_ctx(run_id, node_id)))
    assert store.get_meeting(first["meeting_id"])["status"] == "completed"

    third = asyncio.run(run_meeting(_ctx(run_id, node_id)))      # 不带反馈：重开
    assert third["suspended"] is True
    assert third["meeting_id"] != first["meeting_id"]
    assert store.get_meeting(first["meeting_id"])["status"] == "abandoned"


def test_reopen_feedback_is_processed_once() -> None:
    """带着驳回意见续开的会议又在确认点挂起：恢复时同一条意见还留在节点配置里，不再处理第二遍。"""
    run_id, node_id = _run()
    first = asyncio.run(run_meeting(_ctx(run_id, node_id)))
    _decide(run_id, "approve")
    asyncio.run(run_meeting(_ctx(run_id, node_id)))
    store.update_meeting(first["meeting_id"], phase="ask")
    handled = dict(feedback="驳回：范围太大", feedback_kind="reject", feedback_seq=3)

    asyncio.run(run_meeting(_ctx(run_id, node_id, **handled)))       # 带着意见续开，又在确认点挂起
    _decide(run_id, "approve")
    again = asyncio.run(run_meeting(_ctx(run_id, node_id, **handled)))   # 恢复

    assert again["meeting_id"] == first["meeting_id"] and again["done"] is True
    assert store.get_state(first["meeting_id"])["feedbacks"] == ["驳回：范围太大"]


def test_resume_in_place_after_interruption() -> None:
    run_id, node_id = _run()
    first = asyncio.run(run_meeting(_ctx(run_id, node_id)))
    # 模拟中断后恢复：会议还在进行中，停在某一步
    store.update_meeting(first["meeting_id"], status="active")
    _decide(run_id, "approve")
    again = asyncio.run(run_meeting(_ctx(run_id, node_id)))
    assert again["meeting_id"] == first["meeting_id"]
    assert again["done"] is True
    assert store.get_meeting(first["meeting_id"])["status"] == "completed"


def test_reset_closed_checkpoint_restarts() -> None:
    """确认点被节点重置关掉、没有留下决定：归档旧会议，新建一场。"""
    run_id, node_id = _run()
    first = asyncio.run(run_meeting(_ctx(run_id, node_id)))
    with session_scope() as s:
        gate = s.query(Gate).filter(Gate.run_id == run_id).one()
        gate.status = "approved"                  # 被重置关闭：状态变了，但没有决定
        s.query(Node).filter(Node.run_id == run_id).one().status = "pending"

    again = asyncio.run(run_meeting(_ctx(run_id, node_id)))
    assert again["meeting_id"] != first["meeting_id"]
    assert store.get_meeting(first["meeting_id"])["status"] == "abandoned"


def test_auto_retry_feedback_resumes_in_place() -> None:
    """模型输出不合格后的自动重试也带反馈，但那不是复议：不触发入口处的反馈处理，原地续开。"""
    run_id, node_id = _run()
    first = asyncio.run(run_meeting(_ctx(run_id, node_id)))
    with session_scope() as s:       # 模拟：确认点被重置关闭（没有留下决定），节点因失败重试
        s.query(Gate).filter(Gate.run_id == run_id).update({Gate.status: "approved"})
    store.update_meeting(first["meeting_id"], status="active")

    again = asyncio.run(run_meeting(_ctx(run_id, node_id, feedback="模型输出不符合要求",
                                         feedback_kind="auto_retry", feedback_seq=2)))
    assert again["meeting_id"] == first["meeting_id"]
    assert again["suspended"] is True                    # 停在同一步，重新问
    assert "feedbacks" not in store.get_state(first["meeting_id"])


def test_escalation_checkpoint_when_rounds_are_used_up() -> None:
    run_id, node_id = _run("loop", round=2, max_rounds=2)
    first = asyncio.run(run_meeting(_ctx(run_id, node_id, "loop")))
    assert first["suspended"] is True
    assert first["checkpoint"]["kind"] == "escalation"
    assert first["checkpoint"]["payload"]["targets"] == ["upstream"]

    _decide(run_id, "approve")
    second = asyncio.run(run_meeting(_ctx(run_id, node_id, "loop")))
    assert second["done"] is True and second["summary"] == "人接受了现状"


def test_escalation_reject_grants_one_more_round() -> None:
    run_id, node_id = _run("loop", round=2, max_rounds=2)
    asyncio.run(run_meeting(_ctx(run_id, node_id, "loop")))
    _decide(run_id, "reject", feedback="再查一次成本")

    again = asyncio.run(run_meeting(_ctx(run_id, node_id, "loop")))
    assert again["reroute"] == {
        "targets": ["upstream"],
        "feedback": "还有未解决的问题\n\n用户在确认点上的意见：再查一次成本",
        "self_feedback": None, "extra_round": True}
    assert store.get_meeting(again["meeting_id"])["status"] == "waiting_rework"


def test_reroute_before_rounds_run_out() -> None:
    run_id, node_id = _run("loop", round=1, max_rounds=3)
    result = asyncio.run(run_meeting(_ctx(run_id, node_id, "loop")))
    assert result["reroute"]["extra_round"] is False
    assert "用户在确认点上的意见" not in result["reroute"]["feedback"]
```

- [x] **Step 3: 写协议基类**

`backend/app/meeting/protocols/__init__.py`：

```python
"""会议协议：每种协议是一串有序的步骤。"""
```

`backend/app/meeting/protocols/base.py`：

```python
"""协议基类：步进循环与步骤的返回值。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from app.meeting.session import MeetingSession


@dataclass
class StepResult:
    action: str                                   # advance | suspend | reroute | done
    next: str | None = None                       # advance 时可指定下一步
    checkpoint: dict | None = None                # suspend：已创建的确认点
    reroute: dict | None = None                   # reroute：定点回流指令
    summary: str = ""
    extra: dict = field(default_factory=dict)     # done：随节点结果带回的内容，如工件记录


def advance(next: str | None = None) -> StepResult:
    return StepResult(action="advance", next=next)


def suspend(checkpoint: dict, summary: str = "") -> StepResult:
    return StepResult(action="suspend", checkpoint=checkpoint, summary=summary)


def reroute(targets: list[str], feedback: str, *, self_feedback: str | None = None,
            summary: str = "", extra_round: bool = False) -> StepResult:
    """发起定点回流。

    extra_round 为真表示：回流轮次已经用完，人在转人工的确认点上要求"再评一轮"，
    编排器据此把轮次上限放宽一轮。协议一般不直接调用它，而是用 reroute_or_escalate()。
    """
    return StepResult(action="reroute", summary=summary,
                      reroute={"targets": list(targets), "feedback": feedback,
                               "self_feedback": self_feedback, "extra_round": extra_round})


async def reroute_or_escalate(mtg: "MeetingSession", targets: list[str], feedback: str, *,
                              self_feedback: str | None = None, title: str,
                              description: str, summary: str = "") -> StepResult | None:
    """发起定点回流；回流轮次已用完时，先由人在一个确认点上决定怎么办。

    返回值就是调用它的步骤该返回的东西：
    - 轮次没用完：回流指令；
    - 轮次已用完、人还没决定：挂起（确认点类型是 escalation）；
    - 轮次已用完、人驳回并写了意见：带 extra_round 的回流指令，意见并入反馈，编排器放宽一轮；
    - 返回 None：人在确认点上选择通过，即接受现状、不再回流。调用它的步骤据此往下走。
    """
    if mtg.round_no() < mtg.max_rounds():
        return reroute(targets, feedback, self_feedback=self_feedback, summary=summary)
    decision = mtg.decision()
    if decision is None:
        return suspend(await mtg.open_checkpoint(
            "escalation", title=title, description=description,
            payload={"targets": list(targets), "feedback": feedback[:2000],
                     "round": mtg.round_no(), "max_rounds": mtg.max_rounds()}))
    if decision["action"] == "approve":
        return None
    note = str(decision.get("feedback") or "").strip()
    if note:
        feedback = f"{feedback}\n\n用户在确认点上的意见：{note}"
    return reroute(targets, feedback, self_feedback=self_feedback, summary=summary,
                   extra_round=True)


def done(summary: str, **extra: Any) -> StepResult:
    return StepResult(action="done", summary=summary, extra=extra)


class MeetingProtocol:
    name: str = ""
    steps: tuple[str, ...] = ()

    async def on_enter(self, mtg: "MeetingSession") -> None:
        """进入协议时调用一次。带着反馈续开的协议在这里用 mtg.consume_feedback() 取走反馈。"""

    async def run(self, mtg: "MeetingSession") -> StepResult:
        """从当前步骤跑到挂起、回流或完成。每一步返回 advance 时推进到下一步。"""
        await self.on_enter(mtg)
        while True:
            step = mtg.current_step(self.steps)
            handler = getattr(self, f"step_{step}")
            result = await handler(mtg)
            if result.action != "advance":
                return result
            await mtg.advance(result.next, self.steps)
```

- [x] **Step 4: 写确认点，并让门的载荷带上类型、载荷与决定**

`backend/app/meeting/checkpoints.py`：

```python
"""会议内的确认点：复用门的机制，挂在会议节点上。

门行上多存三样：类型（kind）、挂起时给判定者看的内容（payload_json）、做出的决定
（decision_json）。会议内确认点的通过和驳回都让会议续开，所以"决定"以 decision_json
里有没有 action 为准——节点重置也会把关着的门置为已批准，但那不是决定。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

from app.db.base import session_scope
from app.db.models import MEETING_SUSPENDED, Gate, Node, Run
from app.engine.events import emit
from app.engine.gates import gate_payload
from app.meeting import store

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from app.meeting.session import MeetingSession

CHECKPOINT_KINDS = ("clarify", "roster", "consensus", "escalation")


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)


async def open_checkpoint(mtg: "MeetingSession", kind: str, *, title: str, description: str,
                          payload: dict | None = None) -> dict:
    """建一条 open 的门并挂起，返回门的载荷。节点上还开着的旧门（如节点被重置留下的）一并作废。"""
    with session_scope() as s:
        for stale in s.query(Gate).filter(Gate.node_id == mtg.node_id, Gate.status == "open").all():
            stale.status = "superseded"
        gate = Gate(run_id=mtg.run_id, node_id=mtg.node_id, status="open", kind=kind,
                    payload_json={"title": title, "description": description, **(payload or {})})
        s.add(gate)
        s.flush()
        node = s.get(Node, mtg.node_id)
        if node is not None:
            node.status = "waiting_human"
        run = s.get(Run, mtg.run_id)
        if run is not None:
            run.status = "waiting_human"
        body = gate_payload(s, gate)
    store.update_meeting(mtg.meeting_id, status=MEETING_SUSPENDED)
    store.set_state(mtg.meeting_id, {**store.get_state(mtg.meeting_id),
                                     "awaiting_gate_id": body["id"], "checkpoint_kind": kind})
    await emit(mtg.run_id, "gate_requested", mtg.node_id, body)
    return body


def get_decision(gate_id: int) -> dict | None:
    """门已有决定时返回 {gate_id, action, feedback, data}，否则返回 None。"""
    with session_scope() as s:
        gate = s.get(Gate, gate_id)
        if gate is None or gate.status == "open":
            return None
        decision = dict(gate.decision_json or {})
        if not decision.get("action"):
            return None
        return {"gate_id": gate.id, "action": decision["action"],
                "feedback": decision.get("feedback") or gate.feedback or "",
                "data": decision.get("data") or {}}


async def record_auto_decision(mtg: "MeetingSession", kind: str, *, decided_by: str,
                               decision: dict, title: str = "",
                               payload: dict | None = None) -> int:
    """不是由人当场判定的确认点：直接落一条已决定的门留审计，并推 checkpoint_decided。"""
    action = "approve" if decision.get("action") == "approve" else "reject"
    with session_scope() as s:
        gate = Gate(run_id=mtg.run_id, node_id=mtg.node_id,
                    status="approved" if action == "approve" else "rejected", kind=kind,
                    payload_json={"title": title, **(payload or {})},
                    decision_json={**decision, "action": action}, decided_by=decided_by,
                    decided_at=_now(), feedback=decision.get("feedback"))
        s.add(gate)
        s.flush()
        gate_id = gate.id
    await emit(mtg.run_id, "checkpoint_decided", mtg.node_id,
               {"gate_id": gate_id, "kind": kind, "decided_by": decided_by, "decision": decision})
    return gate_id
```

`backend/app/engine/gates.py` 的 `gate_payload` 整个函数换成：

```python
def gate_payload(s, gate: Gate) -> dict:
    """门对外 payload：request_gate / get_gate / run 快照共用同一份字段。

    必须复用调用方 session（连接池 size=1，另开 session 会在池上死等）。
    会议内确认点（门上带类型）的标题与描述存在门的载荷里；工件确认点沿用节点配置，
    方案确认门的描述追加讨论里的未决议题。描述里已含该段落时不重复追加，保证幂等。
    """
    node = s.get(Node, gate.node_id)
    config = dict(node.config_json or {}) if node is not None else {}
    node_key = node.node_key if node is not None else ""
    payload = dict(gate.payload_json or {})
    description = str(payload.get("description") or "") if gate.kind else (config.get("description") or "")
    if node_key == "gate_proposal" and not gate.kind and PENDING_TITLE not in description:
        pending = _pending_questions(s, gate.run_id)
        if pending:
            description += f"\n\n{PENDING_TITLE}：\n" + "\n".join(pending)
    title = (payload.get("title") if gate.kind else None) or config.get("title")
    return {
        "id": gate.id,
        "run_id": gate.run_id,
        "node_id": gate.node_id,
        "node_key": node_key,
        "status": gate.status,
        "feedback": gate.feedback,
        "decided_by": gate.decided_by,
        "created_at": gate.created_at.isoformat() if gate.created_at else None,
        "decided_at": gate.decided_at.isoformat() if gate.decided_at else None,
        "title": title or (node.name if node is not None else ""),
        "description": description,
        "artifact": config.get("artifact"),
        "kind": gate.kind or "",
        "payload": payload,
        "decision": dict(gate.decision_json or {}),
    }
```

- [x] **Step 5: 写会话**

`backend/app/meeting/session.py`：

```python
"""MeetingSession：一次执行里协议用得到的全部入口。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

from app.conversation.builder import ContextBuilder
from app.engine.events import emit
from app.meeting import store
from app.meeting.checkpoints import get_decision, open_checkpoint

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from app.engine.runners import NodeContext

# 节点配置里由编排器维护的键，不属于会议的生效参数
RUNTIME_KEYS = ("feedback", "feedback_kind", "feedback_seq", "retry_at", "retry_count",
                "escalation", "escalation_reason", "round")


@dataclass
class MeetingSession:
    meeting_id: int
    ctx: "NodeContext"
    builder: ContextBuilder = field(default_factory=ContextBuilder)

    @property
    def run_id(self) -> str:
        return self.ctx.run_id

    @property
    def node_id(self) -> int:
        return self.ctx.node_id

    @property
    def node_key(self) -> str:
        return self.ctx.node_key

    def config(self) -> dict:
        return self.ctx.config or {}

    def params(self) -> dict:
        """生效参数：模板默认值与运行级配置在新建运行时已合并进节点的 params。"""
        return dict(self.config().get("params") or {})

    def round_no(self) -> int:
        """节点级轮次：回流时由编排器加一。"""
        try:
            return max(1, int(self.config().get("round") or 1))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return 1

    # ---------- 状态与步进 ----------
    def state(self) -> dict:
        return store.get_state(self.meeting_id)

    def set_state(self, **changes) -> None:
        state = self.state()
        state.update(changes)
        store.set_state(self.meeting_id, state)

    def current_step(self, steps: tuple[str, ...]) -> str:
        phase = store.get_meeting(self.meeting_id)["phase"]
        return phase if phase in steps else steps[0]

    async def advance(self, next_step: str | None, steps: tuple[str, ...]) -> None:
        index = steps.index(self.current_step(steps))
        target = next_step or steps[index + 1]
        store.update_meeting(self.meeting_id, phase=target)
        await emit(self.run_id, "meeting_phase", self.node_id,
                   {"meeting_id": self.meeting_id, "phase": target, "round": self.round_no()})

    # ---------- 参会者、反馈、确认点 ----------
    def participants(self, mode: str | None = None) -> list[dict]:
        rows = store.list_participants(self.meeting_id)
        return [row for row in rows if mode is None or row["mode"] == mode]

    def max_rounds(self) -> int:
        """节点级的回流轮次上限。用完时发起回流的协议要转人工（见 reroute_or_escalate）。"""
        try:
            return max(1, int(self.config().get("max_rounds") or 3))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return 3

    def consume_feedback(self) -> str | None:
        """取出并清空带着反馈续开时存下的反馈，并记下它的序号：同一条反馈不再当作新反馈。"""
        state = self.state()
        feedback = str(state.get("pending_feedback") or "").strip()
        if feedback:
            self.set_state(pending_feedback="", handled_feedback_seq=state.get("seen_feedback_seq"))
        return feedback or None

    def decision(self) -> dict | None:
        """读取当前等待的确认点的决定，读到后标记为已处理。"""
        state = self.state()
        gate_id = state.get("awaiting_gate_id")
        if not gate_id or state.get("handled_gate_id") == gate_id:
            return None
        decision = get_decision(gate_id)
        if decision is None:
            return None
        self.set_state(handled_gate_id=gate_id)
        return decision

    async def open_checkpoint(self, kind: str, *, title: str, description: str,
                              payload: dict | None = None) -> dict:
        return await open_checkpoint(self, kind, title=title, description=description,
                                     payload=payload)
```

- [x] **Step 6: 写运行器**

`backend/app/meeting/runner.py`：

```python
"""会议运行器的入口：取或建会议、续开或重开的判定、把协议的结果翻译成节点结果。

进入一个会议节点时（详设 §6.2）：
- 该节点还没有会议 → 新建；
- 节点带着要复议的反馈（人工驳回、定点回流、用户带反馈的重试）→ 续开，反馈存进会议状态，
  由协议在入口处取走；每条反馈只处理一次。失败后的自动重试也带反馈，但只原地续开；
- 没有反馈：会议在进行中或等待返工 → 从当前步骤续开；
  会议在挂起且确认点已有决定 → 读取决定后续开；
  其余（已完成，或挂起但确认点被重置关闭）→ 旧会议归档，新建一场。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from app.db.models import (MEETING_ABANDONED, MEETING_ACTIVE, MEETING_COMPLETED,
                           MEETING_SUSPENDED, MEETING_WAITING_REWORK)
from app.engine.events import emit
from app.engine.feedback import REOPEN_KINDS
from app.engine.runners import NodeContext, NodeFailure
from app.meeting import store
from app.meeting.checkpoints import get_decision
from app.meeting.protocols.base import MeetingProtocol
from app.meeting.session import RUNTIME_KEYS, MeetingSession

_PROTOCOLS: dict[str, type[MeetingProtocol]] = {}


def register_protocol(cls: type[MeetingProtocol]) -> type[MeetingProtocol]:
    _PROTOCOLS[cls.name] = cls
    return cls


def get_protocol(name: str) -> MeetingProtocol:
    cls = _PROTOCOLS.get(name or "")
    if cls is None:
        raise NodeFailure(f"未知的会议协议：{name}", fatal=True)
    return cls()


def _checkpoint_decided(state: dict) -> bool:
    gate_id = state.get("awaiting_gate_id")
    return bool(gate_id) and state.get("handled_gate_id") != gate_id and get_decision(gate_id) is not None


def _enter(ctx: NodeContext, protocol: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
    """返回 (会议 id, 进入原因)。进入原因取 new / feedback / resume / decided / restart。

    一条反馈算不算"新的"，要同时满足三点：来路属于 REOPEN_KINDS；节点配置里的序号
    （feedback_seq）与会议状态里已处理的序号不同；内容非空。
    """
    config = ctx.config or {}
    meeting_config = {k: v for k, v in config.items() if k not in RUNTIME_KEYS}
    existing = store.latest_meeting_for_node(ctx.node_id)

    def new() -> tuple[int, str]:
        meeting_id = store.create_meeting(
            run_id=ctx.run_id, node_id=ctx.node_id, node_key=ctx.node_key, protocol=protocol,
            charter={}, config=meeting_config)
        return meeting_id, "new"

    if existing is None:
        return new()
    feedback = str(config.get("feedback") or "").strip()
    seq = config.get("feedback_seq")
    reopen = (bool(feedback) and config.get("feedback_kind") in REOPEN_KINDS
              and seq != existing["state"].get("handled_feedback_seq"))
    if reopen:
        store.update_meeting(
            existing["id"], status=MEETING_ACTIVE,
            state_json={**existing["state"], "pending_feedback": feedback,
                        "feedback_kind": config.get("feedback_kind"),
                        "seen_feedback_seq": seq})
        return existing["id"], "feedback"
    if existing["status"] in (MEETING_ACTIVE, MEETING_WAITING_REWORK):
        return existing["id"], "resume"
    if existing["status"] == MEETING_SUSPENDED and _checkpoint_decided(existing["state"]):
        return existing["id"], "decided"
    store.update_meeting(existing["id"], status=MEETING_ABANDONED)
    return new()


async def run_meeting(ctx: NodeContext) -> dict:
    """MEETING 节点的执行入口。不改节点的最终状态，交给编排器。"""
    protocol = get_protocol((ctx.config or {}).get("protocol"))
    meeting_id, reason = _enter(ctx, protocol.name)
    mtg = MeetingSession(meeting_id=meeting_id, ctx=ctx)
    if reason == "new":
        await emit(ctx.run_id, "meeting_started", ctx.node_id,
                   {"meeting_id": meeting_id, "node_key": ctx.node_key, "protocol": protocol.name})
    else:
        await emit(ctx.run_id, "meeting_resumed", ctx.node_id,
                   {"meeting_id": meeting_id, "reason": reason})
    if reason != "new":
        store.update_meeting(meeting_id, status=MEETING_ACTIVE)
    result = await protocol.run(mtg)
    if result.action == "suspend":
        return {"suspended": True, "meeting_id": meeting_id, "checkpoint": result.checkpoint,
                "summary": result.summary}
    if result.action == "reroute":
        store.update_meeting(meeting_id, status=MEETING_WAITING_REWORK)
        return {"reroute": result.reroute, "meeting_id": meeting_id, "summary": result.summary}
    store.update_meeting(meeting_id, status=MEETING_COMPLETED, finished_at=True)
    await emit(ctx.run_id, "meeting_finished", ctx.node_id, {"meeting_id": meeting_id})
    return {"summary": result.summary, "done": True, **result.extra}
```

`backend/tests/test_meeting_checkpoints.py`：

```python
"""会议内确认点的决定路径：通过或驳回都让会议续开，决定存在门上；工件确认点的行为不变；
写进节点的反馈带上来路和序号。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio

import pytest

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.db.base import init_db, session_scope
from app.db.models import Gate, Node, Run, Workflow
from app.engine.gates import GateError, decide_gate, get_gate, request_gate
from app.engine.orchestrator import orchestrator
from app.engine.runners import NodeContext
from app.engine.templates import instantiate_nodes, seed_defaults, workflow_definition
from app.main import app
from app.meeting import store
from app.meeting.checkpoints import open_checkpoint
from app.meeting.session import MeetingSession

_counter = iter(range(1, 1000))


def _new_run(node_kwargs: dict) -> tuple[str, int]:
    init_db()
    seed_defaults()
    index = next(_counter)
    with session_scope() as s:
        workflow_id = s.query(Workflow).filter(Workflow.template_key == "sdlc_v1").one().id
        run_id = f"checkpoint-run-{index}"
        s.add(Run(id=run_id, workflow_id=workflow_id, status="running"))
        s.flush()
        node = Node(run_id=run_id, status="pending", **node_kwargs)
        s.add(node)
        s.flush()
        return run_id, node.id


def _open_checkpoint() -> MeetingSession:
    run_id, node_id = _new_run(dict(node_key="discuss", type="MEETING", name="讨论会议",
                                   config_json={"protocol": "discussion"}))
    ctx = NodeContext(run_id=run_id, node_id=node_id, node_key="discuss", type="MEETING",
                      name="讨论会议", phase="", config={"protocol": "discussion"}, assignee={},
                      attempt_id=0, attempt_no=1)
    meeting_id = store.create_meeting(run_id=run_id, node_id=node_id, node_key="discuss",
                                      protocol="discussion", charter={}, config={})
    mtg = MeetingSession(meeting_id=meeting_id, ctx=ctx)
    asyncio.run(open_checkpoint(mtg, "consensus", title="请确认", description="还有未决议题",
                                payload={"open": ["D2"]}))
    return mtg


def _template_run() -> tuple[str, int]:
    """按当前模板实例化的运行，返回 (运行 id, 方案确认门的节点 id)。"""
    init_db()
    seed_defaults()
    run_id = f"template-run-{next(_counter)}"
    with session_scope() as s:
        workflow_id = s.query(Workflow).filter(Workflow.template_key == "sdlc_v1").one().id
        s.add(Run(id=run_id, workflow_id=workflow_id, status="running"))
        s.flush()
    instantiate_nodes(run_id, workflow_definition("sdlc_v1"))
    with session_scope() as s:
        node_id = s.query(Node).filter(Node.run_id == run_id, Node.node_key == "gate_proposal").one().id
    return run_id, node_id


def test_open_checkpoint_suspends_node_and_run() -> None:
    mtg = _open_checkpoint()
    gate = get_gate(store.get_state(mtg.meeting_id)["awaiting_gate_id"])
    assert gate["kind"] == "consensus" and gate["status"] == "open"
    assert gate["title"] == "请确认"
    assert gate["payload"]["open"] == ["D2"]
    with session_scope() as s:
        assert s.get(Node, mtg.node_id).status == "waiting_human"
    assert store.get_meeting(mtg.meeting_id)["status"] == "suspended"


def test_reject_needs_no_target_and_lets_meeting_resume() -> None:
    mtg = _open_checkpoint()
    gate_id = store.get_state(mtg.meeting_id)["awaiting_gate_id"]
    result = decide_gate(gate_id=gate_id, action="reject", feedback="给 D2 再多一轮")
    assert result["checkpoint"] is True and result["status"] == "rejected"
    with session_scope() as s:
        assert s.get(Node, mtg.node_id).status == "pending"
        assert s.get(Gate, gate_id).decision_json == {
            "action": "reject", "feedback": "给 D2 再多一轮", "data": {}}
    # 会议本身的挂起状态由运行器在续开时改，决定路径不动它
    assert store.get_meeting(mtg.meeting_id)["status"] == "suspended"


def test_approve_carries_data_and_decider() -> None:
    mtg = _open_checkpoint()
    gate_id = store.get_state(mtg.meeting_id)["awaiting_gate_id"]
    decide_gate(gate_id=gate_id, action="approve", decided_by="model:mock",
                data={"option": "proceed"})
    gate = get_gate(gate_id)
    assert gate["decision"]["data"] == {"option": "proceed"}
    assert gate["decided_by"] == "model:mock"


def test_artifact_gate_reject_still_requires_target() -> None:
    run_id, gate_node_id = _template_run()
    gate = request_gate(run_id, gate_node_id)
    with pytest.raises(GateError):
        decide_gate(gate_id=gate["id"], action="reject", feedback="重做")
    assert get_gate(gate["id"])["kind"] == ""


def test_reject_marks_feedback_with_kind_and_sequence() -> None:
    run_id, gate_node_id = _template_run()
    gate = request_gate(run_id, gate_node_id)
    decide_gate(gate_id=gate["id"], action="reject", feedback="范围太大", target_node="idea")
    with session_scope() as s:
        config = s.query(Node).filter(Node.run_id == run_id, Node.node_key == "idea").one().config_json
    assert config["feedback"] == "范围太大"
    assert config["feedback_kind"] == "reject"
    assert config["feedback_seq"] == 1


def test_retry_with_feedback_is_marked_as_user_feedback(monkeypatch) -> None:
    run_id, gate_node_id = _template_run()
    with session_scope() as s:
        s.get(Node, gate_node_id).status = "failed"
    monkeypatch.setattr(orchestrator, "kick", lambda run_id: None)
    with TestClient(app) as client:
        response = client.post(f"/api/nodes/{gate_node_id}/retry", json={"feedback": "请重做"})
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    with session_scope() as s:
        config = s.get(Node, gate_node_id).config_json
    assert (config["feedback"], config["feedback_kind"], config["feedback_seq"]) == ("请重做", "user", 1)
```

- [x] **Step 3: 写反馈的小工具**

（`backend/app/engine/feedback.py` 已经在任务 B3 里按下面的内容创建了——B3 的运行器要导入它。这一步只需确认文件在，并给 `with_feedback` 补一个直接的单元测试。）

`backend/app/engine/feedback.py`：

```python
"""节点反馈的统一写法：凡是要带着反馈重开一个节点，都经过 with_feedback()。

- feedback_seq：每写一次加一。会议运行器靠它认出"这是一条还没处理过的反馈"。
  带着反馈续开的会议如果在人工确认点挂起，恢复时节点配置里还留着同一条反馈，不该再处理一遍。
- feedback_kind：反馈的来路。要复议的来路（人工驳回、定点回流、用户带反馈的重试或注入）
  会议会把它当作一条意见处理；失败后的自动重试也带反馈，但会议只原地续开。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

# 要复议的反馈来路
REOPEN_KINDS = ("reject", "reroute", "user")


def with_feedback(config: dict | None, feedback: str | None,
                  kind: str | None = None) -> dict:
    """返回写入反馈之后的新配置，不改入参。feedback 为 None 时把反馈清掉。"""
    config = dict(config or {})
    if feedback is None:
        config.pop("feedback", None)
        config.pop("feedback_kind", None)
        return config
    config["feedback"] = feedback
    config["feedback_seq"] = int(config.get("feedback_seq") or 0) + 1
    if kind:
        config["feedback_kind"] = kind
    else:
        config.pop("feedback_kind", None)
    return config
```

```python
"""定点回流：目标节点定点重开且不级联，发起节点轮次加一，轮次用尽转人工。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio

from app.db.base import init_db, session_scope
from app.db.models import Gate, Node, Run, Workflow
from app.engine.events import load_events
from app.engine.orchestrator import Orchestrator
from app.engine.templates import seed_defaults

_counter = iter(range(1, 1000))


def _graph(round_no: int) -> str:
    """三个节点：target ← child ← self。self 发现需要 target 返工。"""
    init_db()
    seed_defaults()
    index = next(_counter)
    with session_scope() as s:
        workflow_id = s.query(Workflow).filter(Workflow.template_key == "sdlc_v1").one().id
        run_id = f"reroute-run-{index}"
        s.add(Run(id=run_id, workflow_id=workflow_id, status="running"))
        s.flush()
        specs = {
            "target": dict(name="讨论会议", type="MEETING", status="done", deps_json=[]),
            "child": dict(name="下游", type="HUMAN_GATE", status="done", deps_json=["target"]),
            "self": dict(name="评审会议", type="MEETING", status="running", deps_json=["child"],
                         config_json={"round": round_no, "max_rounds": 2}),
        }
        for key, spec in specs.items():
            s.add(Node(run_id=run_id, node_key=key, **spec))
        s.flush()
        return run_id


def _self_node(orchestrator: Orchestrator, run_id: str) -> dict:
    _, nodes = orchestrator._load(run_id)
    return next(n for n in nodes if n["node_key"] == "self")


def _reroute(run_id: str, orchestrator: Orchestrator, **instruction) -> None:
    asyncio.run(orchestrator._handle_reroute(
        run_id, _self_node(orchestrator, run_id),
        {"targets": ["target"], "feedback": "D3 需要复议", **instruction}))


def test_reroute_resets_targets_without_cascade() -> None:
    run_id = _graph(round_no=1)
    orchestrator = Orchestrator()
    _reroute(run_id, orchestrator, self_feedback=None)
    _, nodes = orchestrator._load(run_id)
    by_key = {n["node_key"]: n for n in nodes}
    assert by_key["target"]["status"] == "pending"
    assert by_key["target"]["config"]["feedback"] == "D3 需要复议"
    assert by_key["target"]["config"]["feedback_kind"] == "reroute"
    assert by_key["target"]["config"]["feedback_seq"] == 1
    assert by_key["child"]["status"] == "done"                # 不级联重置下游
    assert by_key["self"]["status"] == "pending"
    assert by_key["self"]["config"]["round"] == 2
    events = load_events(run_id)
    assert [e["type"] for e in events] == ["node_rerouted"]
    assert events[0]["payload"]["targets"] == ["target"]


def test_self_feedback_replaces_stale_feedback() -> None:
    """发起回流的节点上留着一条已经处理过的驳回意见：新的自我提示把它替换掉。"""
    run_id = _graph(round_no=1)
    orchestrator = Orchestrator()
    with session_scope() as s:
        node = s.query(Node).filter(Node.run_id == run_id, Node.node_key == "self").one()
        node.config_json = {**node.config_json, "feedback": "用户的驳回意见",
                            "feedback_kind": "reject", "feedback_seq": 3}
    _reroute(run_id, orchestrator, self_feedback="下一轮复核 D3 的处理结果")
    node = _self_node(orchestrator, run_id)
    assert node["config"]["feedback"] == "下一轮复核 D3 的处理结果"
    assert node["config"]["feedback_kind"] == "reroute_self"
    assert node["config"]["feedback_seq"] == 4


def test_no_self_feedback_clears_stale_feedback() -> None:
    run_id = _graph(round_no=1)
    orchestrator = Orchestrator()
    with session_scope() as s:
        node = s.query(Node).filter(Node.run_id == run_id, Node.node_key == "self").one()
        node.config_json = {**node.config_json, "feedback": "用户的驳回意见",
                            "feedback_kind": "reject", "feedback_seq": 3}
    _reroute(run_id, orchestrator, self_feedback=None)
    node = _self_node(orchestrator, run_id)
    assert "feedback" not in node["config"]
    assert node["config"]["feedback_seq"] == 3                # 序号保留，下一次写入接着涨


def test_reroute_escalates_when_rounds_are_used_up() -> None:
    run_id = _graph(round_no=2)
    orchestrator = Orchestrator()
    _reroute(run_id, orchestrator)
    node = _self_node(orchestrator, run_id)
    assert node["status"] == "waiting_human"
    assert node["config"]["escalation"] is True
    with session_scope() as s:
        assert s.query(Gate).filter(Gate.run_id == run_id, Gate.status == "open").count() == 1
    events = load_events(run_id)
    assert [e["type"] for e in events] == ["node_rerouted", "gate_requested"]
    assert events[0]["payload"]["escalated"] is True


def test_extra_round_allows_one_more_round() -> None:
    run_id = _graph(round_no=2)
    orchestrator = Orchestrator()
    _reroute(run_id, orchestrator, feedback="再查一次成本", extra_round=True)
    node = _self_node(orchestrator, run_id)
    assert node["status"] == "pending"
    assert (node["config"]["round"], node["config"]["max_rounds"]) == (3, 3)
    assert load_events(run_id)[0]["payload"]["escalated"] is False
```

- [x] **Step 3: 实现**

`backend/app/engine/orchestrator.py` 新增方法（放在 `_handle_review_block` 之前）：

```python
    # ---------- 定点回流 ----------
    async def _handle_reroute(self, run_id: str, node: dict, instruction: dict) -> None:
        """把指令里的节点定点重开并带反馈（不级联），当前节点轮次加一；轮次用尽转人工。

        评审、确认这类循环靠它承载：评审会议依赖讨论会议，所以讨论会议重跑完成之前，
        评审会议不会被调度。extra_round 为真表示人已同意再评一轮：轮次和上限一起加一。
        """
        config = dict(node["config"] or {})
        round_no = int(config.get("round") or 1)
        max_rounds = max(1, int(config.get("max_rounds") or 3))
        targets = [str(key) for key in (instruction.get("targets") or [])]
        feedback = str(instruction.get("feedback") or "")
        extra_round = bool(instruction.get("extra_round"))

        if round_no >= max_rounds and not extra_round:
            reason = f"回流已达 {max_rounds} 轮：{feedback[:500]}"
            self._update_node(node["id"], status="waiting_human", feedback=reason,
                              drop=["retry_at", "feedback_kind"],
                              extra={"escalation": True, "escalation_reason": reason})
            from app.engine.gates import request_gate

            gate = request_gate(run_id, node["id"], feedback=reason)
            self._set_run(run_id, status="waiting_human")
            await emit(run_id, "node_rerouted", node["id"], {
                "from": node["node_key"], "targets": targets, "round": round_no,
                "max_rounds": max_rounds, "escalated": True})
            await emit(run_id, "gate_requested", node["id"],
                       {**gate, "reason": "回流轮次用尽，转人工", "escalation": True})
            return

        bump = {"round": round_no + 1}
        if extra_round:
            bump["max_rounds"] = round_no + 1
        self_feedback = str(instruction.get("self_feedback") or "").strip()
        if self_feedback:
            self._update_node(node["id"], status="pending", extra={
                **bump, "feedback": self_feedback, "feedback_kind": "reroute_self",
                "feedback_seq": int(config.get("feedback_seq") or 0) + 1}, drop=["retry_at"])
        else:
            self._update_node(node["id"], status="pending", extra=bump,
                              drop=["retry_at", "feedback", "feedback_kind"])
        _, nodes = self._load(run_id)
        reset: list[str] = []
        for other in nodes:
            if other["node_key"] in targets and other["status"] != "running":
                self._update_node(other["id"], status="pending", extra={
                    "feedback": feedback, "feedback_kind": "reroute",
                    "feedback_seq": int((other["config"] or {}).get("feedback_seq") or 0) + 1},
                    drop=["retry_at", "retry_count"])
                reset.append(other["node_key"])
        await emit(run_id, "node_rerouted", node["id"], {
            "from": node["node_key"], "targets": reset, "round": round_no + 1,
            "max_rounds": bump.get("max_rounds", max_rounds), "escalated": False})
```

```python
            return
```

### Task B6: 角色发言的调用通道

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/app/meeting/prompts.py`
- Create: `backend/app/meeting/calls.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/meeting/store.py`（末尾加三个函数）
- Modify: `backend/app/meeting/runner.py`（把结构化输出错误翻译成可重试的失败）
- Modify: `backend/app/engine/agent_loop.py`（`run_agent` 加 `extra_messages`）
- Create: `backend/tests/meeting_fakes.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_meeting_calls.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ContextBuilder`、`Segment`（任务 A1）；`MeetingSession`（任务 B3）
- Produces: `call_tag(kind: str) -> str`；`BASE_SYSTEM_PROMPT: str`（所有角色共用的系统提示）；`persona_block(participant: dict) -> str`（角色人设与模式纪律）；`MODE_RULES: dict[str, str]`；`OUTPUT_RULES: str`
- Produces: `async speak(mtg, participant, *, kind, turn_kind, segments, instruction, charter=None, structured=None, decision_point_id=None, temperature=0.4, turn_round=None, drain=True) -> dict`，返回 `{"turn_id", "content", "data"}`。`kind` 是调用标识（如 `position`）；`structured` 是校验函数，入参是解析出的字典，返回问题列表，空列表表示合格。`charter` 是整场会议不变的段，排在人设之前；`turn_round` 是这条发言所属的会议内部轮次，也是断点续跑时"这个人这一轮说过了"的依据，不给时取节点级轮次
- Produces: `async speak_many(mtg, calls: list[dict], *, resumable=True) -> list[dict]`；`calls` 的每一项是 `speak` 的关键字参数加上 `participant`
- Produces: `CallOutputError(RuntimeError)`；`parse_structured(content, validator=None) -> dict`
- Produces: `async drain_interventions(mtg) -> bool`（取出插话、写成用户发言，并让主持人回应一次）
- Produces: 发言的上下文占到压缩阈值时，追加区里较早的发言被一条摘要取代（调用标识 `summarize`），并发 `context_compacted` 事件；纪要本身不变
- Produces: `store.attach_intervention_turn(intervention_id, turn_id) -> None`；`store.count_turns(meeting_id) -> int`；`store.find_turn(meeting_id, *, phase, round, kind, speaker_id) -> dict | None`
- Produces: `run_agent(..., extra_messages: list[Message] | None = None)`
- Produces（测试替身）：`tests/meeting_fakes.py` 的 `install(monkeypatch, replies, calls=None)`、`MODEL`、`CALL_TAG`。`replies` 的值可以是字符串，也可以是"完整提示文本 → 回复"的函数

- [x] **Step 1: 写测试替身**

`backend/tests/meeting_fakes.py`：

```python
"""会议测试的替身：让模型调用变成按调用标识查表。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from types import SimpleNamespace

from app.models.types import Usage

CALL_TAG = re.compile(r"【调用类型：([a-z_]+)】")
MODEL = SimpleNamespace(id=1, model_name="mock-fast", display_name="Mock Fast",
                        context_window=32768, max_output_tokens=4096, cap_tools=True)


def install(monkeypatch, replies: dict, calls: list | None = None):
    """replies：调用标识 → 模型回复，或者一个"完整提示文本 → 回复"的函数。
    calls：若给出，记录每次调用的标识与完整提示文本（系统提示、用户消息、各段依次拼接）。"""
    from app.meeting import calls as calls_module

    def fake_run_agent(**kwargs):
        text = "\n".join([kwargs["system_prompt"], kwargs.get("user_prompt") or "",
                          *(m.content for m in (kwargs.get("extra_messages") or []))])
        match = CALL_TAG.search(text)
        kind = match.group(1) if match else ""
        if calls is not None:
            calls.append({"kind": kind, "text": text})
        if kind not in replies:
            raise AssertionError(f"测试没有为调用类型 {kind!r} 准备回复")
        reply = replies[kind]
        content = reply(text) if callable(reply) else reply
        return SimpleNamespace(content=content, usage=Usage(100, 50, 60), steps=1,
                               stopped_by="final")

    monkeypatch.setattr(calls_module, "run_agent", fake_run_agent)
    monkeypatch.setattr(calls_module, "_model_for", lambda participant: MODEL)
```

`backend/tests/test_meeting_calls.py`：

```python
"""角色发言：拼装、落盘、事件、结构化输出校验、断点续跑、插话。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio

from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from app.conversation.builder import Segment
from app.db.base import init_db, session_scope
from app.db.models import Node, Run, Workflow
from app.engine.events import load_events
from app.engine.runners import NodeContext
from app.engine.templates import seed_defaults
from app.meeting import calls, store
from app.meeting.calls import CallOutputError, speak, speak_many
from app.meeting.session import MeetingSession
from tests.meeting_fakes import install

_counter = iter(range(1, 1000))


def _meeting() -> tuple[MeetingSession, list[dict]]:
    init_db()
    seed_defaults()
    index = next(_counter)
    with session_scope() as s:
        workflow_id = s.query(Workflow).filter(Workflow.template_key == "sdlc_v1").one().id
        run_id = f"calls-run-{index}"
        s.add(Run(id=run_id, workflow_id=workflow_id, status="running"))
        s.flush()
        node = Node(node_key="discuss", type="MEETING", name="讨论会议", run_id=run_id,
                    status="running", config_json={"protocol": "discussion"})
        s.add(node)
        s.flush()
        ctx = NodeContext(run_id=run_id, node_id=node.id, node_key="discuss", type="MEETING",
                          name="讨论会议", phase="", config={"protocol": "discussion"},
                          assignee={}, attempt_id=0, attempt_no=1)
    meeting_id = store.create_meeting(run_id=run_id, node_id=node.id, node_key="discuss",
                                      protocol="discussion", charter={}, config={})
    store.update_meeting(meeting_id, phase="positions")
    store.add_participant(meeting_id, role_key="facilitator", display_name="主持人",
                          mode="facilitator", system_prompt="你是主持人")
    store.add_participant(meeting_id, role_key="architect", display_name="架构",
                          mode="participant", system_prompt="你是架构师")
    return MeetingSession(meeting_id=meeting_id, ctx=ctx), store.list_participants(meeting_id)


def _is_list_of_strings(data: dict) -> list[str]:
    must = data.get("must")
    return [] if isinstance(must, list) and all(isinstance(x, str) for x in must) else [
        "must 须是字符串数组"]


def _speak(mtg, speaker, **kwargs) -> dict:
    return asyncio.run(speak(mtg, speaker, kind="position", turn_kind="position", **kwargs))


def test_speak_writes_turn_and_emits_events(monkeypatch) -> None:
    install(monkeypatch, {"position": "我的立场是先保证可验证性。"})
    mtg, (_, speaker) = _meeting()
    result = _speak(mtg, speaker, segments=[Segment("frozen", "章程", name="章程")],
                    instruction="请写立场")
    assert result["content"].startswith("我的立场")
    assert result["data"] is None
    turns = store.list_turns(mtg.meeting_id)
    assert [(t["kind"], t["speaker_name"], t["cached_tokens"]) for t in turns] == [
        ("position", "架构", 60)]
    types = [e["type"] for e in load_events(mtg.run_id)]
    assert types[:2] == ["meeting_turn_started", "meeting_turn_finished"]
    assert "node_log" in types


def test_speak_validates_structured_output(monkeypatch) -> None:
    install(monkeypatch, {"position": '```json\n{"must": ["可验证"]}\n```'})
    mtg, (_, speaker) = _meeting()
    result = _speak(mtg, speaker, segments=[], instruction="请写立场",
                    structured=_is_list_of_strings)
    assert result["data"] == {"must": ["可验证"]}
    assert store.list_turns(mtg.meeting_id)[0]["structured"] == {"must": ["可验证"]}


def test_speak_rejects_bad_structured_output(monkeypatch) -> None:
    install(monkeypatch, {"position": '{"must": "不是数组"}'})
    mtg, (_, speaker) = _meeting()
    with pytest.raises(CallOutputError):
        _speak(mtg, speaker, segments=[], instruction="请写立场",
               structured=_is_list_of_strings)


def test_call_tag_is_appended_after_segments(monkeypatch) -> None:
    seen: list[dict] = []
    install(monkeypatch, {"position": "立场"}, seen)
    mtg, (_, speaker) = _meeting()
    _speak(mtg, speaker, segments=[Segment("append", "已有发言")], instruction="请写立场")
    text = seen[0]["text"]
    assert text.index("已有发言") < text.index("请写立场") < text.index("【调用类型：position】")


def test_frozen_segment_change_raises_warning_event(monkeypatch) -> None:
    install(monkeypatch, {"position": "立场"})
    mtg, (_, speaker) = _meeting()
    _speak(mtg, speaker, segments=[Segment("frozen", "章程 A", name="章程")], instruction="x")
    _speak(mtg, speaker, segments=[Segment("frozen", "章程 B", name="章程")], instruction="x")
    warnings = [e for e in load_events(mtg.run_id) if e["type"] == "context_prefix_changed"]
    assert len(warnings) == 1 and warnings[0]["payload"]["segments"] == ["章程"]


def test_speak_many_skips_completed_speakers(monkeypatch) -> None:
    install(monkeypatch, {"position": "立场"})
    mtg, (facilitator, speaker) = _meeting()
    other = store.add_participant(mtg.meeting_id, role_key="qa", display_name="测试",
                                  mode="participant", system_prompt="你是测试")
    _speak(mtg, speaker, segments=[], instruction="请写立场")
    call = {"kind": "position", "turn_kind": "position", "segments": [], "instruction": "请写立场"}
    results = asyncio.run(speak_many(mtg, [{**call, "participant": speaker},
                                           {**call, "participant": other}]))
    assert [r["content"] for r in results] == ["立场", "立场"]
    # 架构已有完整发言，没有再调一次
    assert [t["speaker_name"] for t in store.list_turns(mtg.meeting_id)] == ["架构", "测试"]


def test_intervention_is_injected_and_acknowledged(monkeypatch) -> None:
    install(monkeypatch, {"position": "立场", "intervention_ack": "收到，接下来先核对成本。"})
    mtg, (_, speaker) = _meeting()
    store.queue_intervention(mtg.meeting_id, content="注意成本")
    _speak(mtg, speaker, segments=[], instruction="请写立场")
    turns = store.list_turns(mtg.meeting_id)
    assert [(t["kind"], t["speaker_name"]) for t in turns][:2] == [
        ("user_intervention", "用户"), ("facilitator_note", "主持人")]
    types = [e["type"] for e in load_events(mtg.run_id)]
    assert "meeting_intervention_injected" in types

def test_shared_charter_comes_before_persona(monkeypatch) -> None:
    """章程排在人设之前：它和系统提示在全体角色之间是同一段前缀。"""
    seen: list[dict] = []
    install(monkeypatch, {"position": "立场"}, seen)
    mtg, (_, speaker) = _meeting()
    _speak(mtg, speaker, charter=[Segment("frozen", "会议章程内容", name="章程")],
           segments=[Segment("append", "已有发言")], instruction="请写立场")
    text = seen[0]["text"]
    assert text.index("会议章程内容") < text.index("## 你的角色") < text.index("已有发言")
    assert text.index("已有发言") < text.index("请写立场") < text.index("【调用类型：position】")


def test_switching_speaker_is_not_a_frozen_change(monkeypatch) -> None:
    install(monkeypatch, {"position": "立场"})
    mtg, (_, speaker) = _meeting()
    other_id = store.add_participant(mtg.meeting_id, role_key="qa", display_name="测试",
                                     mode="participant", system_prompt="你是测试")
    other = next(p for p in store.list_participants(mtg.meeting_id) if p["id"] == other_id)
    _speak(mtg, speaker, segments=[], instruction="x")
    _speak(mtg, other, segments=[], instruction="x")
    assert [e for e in load_events(mtg.run_id) if e["type"] == "context_prefix_changed"] == []


def test_turn_round_is_recorded_on_the_turn(monkeypatch) -> None:
    install(monkeypatch, {"position": "立场"})
    mtg, (_, speaker) = _meeting()
    asyncio.run(speak(mtg, speaker, kind="position", turn_kind="stance", segments=[],
                      instruction="请表态", turn_round=7))
    assert store.list_turns(mtg.meeting_id)[0]["round"] == 7


def test_speak_compacts_old_turns_at_threshold(monkeypatch) -> None:
    """追加区占到阈值：较早的发言先压成摘要再发言。纪要不变，压缩留痕。"""
    seen: list[dict] = []
    install(monkeypatch, {"position": "立场", "summarize": "较早交锋的摘要"}, seen)
    small = SimpleNamespace(id=1, model_name="mock-small", display_name="Mock Small",
                            context_window=1600, max_output_tokens=256, cap_tools=True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(calls, "_model_for", lambda participant: small)
    mtg, (_, speaker) = _meeting()
    old = [Segment("append", "字" * 150, name=f"发言{index}") for index in range(10)]

    _speak(mtg, speaker, segments=[Segment("frozen", "章程", name="章程"), *old],
           instruction="请写立场")

    assert [call["kind"] for call in seen] == ["summarize", "position"]
    assert "较早交锋的摘要" in seen[1]["text"]
    assert seen[1]["text"].count("字" * 150) == 6              # 最近六条逐字保留
    assert "context_compacted" in [e["type"] for e in load_events(mtg.run_id)]
    assert len(store.list_turns(mtg.meeting_id)) == 1          # 做摘要不算一次发言
```

- [x] **Step 4: 写提示装配**

`backend/app/meeting/prompts.py`：

```python
"""会议里模型调用的提示装配。

系统提示只含所有角色共用的部分（平台规则与输出纪律），整场会议逐字节不变。
角色人设与模式纪律单独成一段，排在会议章程之后（详设 §7.7）：章程排在人设前面，
它和系统提示才能在全体角色之间共享同一段前缀缓存。
每次调用的具体任务说明由协议给出，放在上下文尾部；调用标识由 calls.speak 追加在最后。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from app.engine.context import PLATFORM_RULES

OUTPUT_RULES = (
    "## 输出纪律\n"
    "1. 结论必须基于给你的材料；材料里没有的，写明是你的假设。\n"
    "2. 要求输出 JSON 时只输出一个 JSON 对象，不要围栏，不要解释。\n"
    "3. 引用他人的发言时注明发言者，不要改写别人的立场。\n"
    "4. 使用中文，保留必要的英文术语与代码标识符。"
)

MODE_RULES: dict[str, str] = {
    "facilitator": (
        "## 模式纪律：主持人\n"
        "你不提出内容上的立场，不替用户裁决。你做的是：组织流程、提炼议题、点名发言、"
        "要求展开或纠偏、如实归纳各方的立场与理由、把已达成的决定整合成文。"
        "归纳时必须忠实于发言原文；分歧不得抹平。"
    ),
    "participant": (
        "## 模式纪律：参会者\n"
        "你从自己的专业视角出发。表态必须有理由；改变立场时必须写明是因为哪条意见。"
        "不要因为别人都同意就附和。"
    ),
    "proxy": (
        "## 模式纪律：需求方代理\n"
        "你只回答关于用户意图、背景与约束的事实问题，不提方案，不表态。"
        "每条回答都要标明出处：引用背景材料的片段，或指向某条用户给出的回答。"
        "材料回答不了的，明确写\"材料中没有说明\"，不要猜测。"
    ),
    "reviewer": (
        "## 模式纪律：评审者\n"
        "你只评审，不改方案。每条意见必须能定位（决策点编号或章节），并给出可执行的修改建议。"
        "没有问题就是没有问题，不要为了显得认真而凑数。"
    ),
    "quality_reviewer": (
        "## 模式纪律：质量评审者\n"
        "你按需求质量特性逐项检查，每条意见定位到需求编号并给出修改建议。只评审，不改文档。"
    ),
    "chair": (
        "## 模式纪律：主席\n"
        "你组织流程、汇总归类、主持讨论、验证修订。整体结论由程序得出，你不裁决整体结论；"
        "对有分歧的条目，你定夺它的分类与级别，并记录被否的意见。"
    ),
    "author": (
        "## 模式纪律：执笔者\n"
        "你是方案的作者。回应评审意见时逐条表态：采纳，或说明不采纳的理由。"
        "修订时只改意见涉及的内容，不要顺手重写别的部分。"
    ),
}


def call_tag(kind: str) -> str:
    """调用标识。Mock 供应商按它路由；对真实模型无害。"""
    return f"【调用类型：{kind}】"


BASE_SYSTEM_PROMPT = "\n\n".join([PLATFORM_RULES, OUTPUT_RULES])


def persona_block(participant: dict) -> str:
    """角色人设与模式纪律，一段冻结的内容。人设写具体的偏好，不写标签（详设 §11.5）。"""
    parts = [f"## 你的角色\n{(participant.get('system_prompt') or '').strip()}"]
    mode_rule = MODE_RULES.get(participant.get("mode") or "", "")
    if mode_rule:
        parts.append(mode_rule)
    return "\n\n".join(parts)
```

- [x] **Step 5: 给存取层补三个函数**

（执行时注意：其中的 `attach_intervention_turn` 已经在任务 B2 里实现了，并且由它负责把插话标为已注入；这里只加另外两个。）

`backend/app/meeting/store.py` 末尾追加：

```python
def attach_intervention_turn(intervention_id: int, turn_id: int) -> None:
    """记下插话被注入成了哪一条发言。"""
    with session_scope() as s:
        row = s.get(MeetingIntervention, intervention_id)
        if row is not None:
            row.injected_turn_id = turn_id


def count_turns(meeting_id: int) -> int:
    with session_scope() as s:
        return s.query(func.count(MeetingTurn.id)).filter(
            MeetingTurn.meeting_id == meeting_id).scalar() or 0


def find_turn(meeting_id: int, *, phase: str, round: int, kind: str,
              speaker_id: int) -> dict | None:
    """取某步骤某轮里某参会者的那条发言，断点续跑时用。"""
    with session_scope() as s:
        row = (
            s.query(MeetingTurn)
            .filter(MeetingTurn.meeting_id == meeting_id, MeetingTurn.phase == phase,
                    MeetingTurn.round == round, MeetingTurn.kind == kind,
                    MeetingTurn.speaker_id == speaker_id, MeetingTurn.content != "")
            .order_by(MeetingTurn.seq.desc())
            .first()
        )
        if row is None:
            return None
        return {"turn_id": row.id, "content": row.content, "data": dict(row.structured_json or {})}
```

- [x] **Step 7: 写调用通道**

`backend/app/meeting/calls.py`：

```python
"""角色发言：拼装上下文 → 调模型 → 落盘 → 推事件。

会议里的每一次模型调用都走 speak()。上下文按 冻结 → 追加 → 尾部 排好交给装配器，
调用标识追加在最后。模型调用复用工具循环 run_agent（单步、无工具），从而沿用它的
流式输出、用量记账与预算扣减。

一次调用发出去的内容，顺序固定（详设 §7.7、§10.1）：
系统提示（全体共用）→ 会议章程（全体相同）→ 角色人设 → 账本快照（本轮不变）→
本轮的逐字发言（只追加）→ 尾部指令。前面的段在两次调用之间逐字节不变，前缀缓存才能命中。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from typing import Callable

from app.artifacts.store import strip_outer_fence
from app.conversation.builder import Segment
from app.conversation.compact import compact_messages
from app.conversation.policy import DEFAULT_POLICY
from app.conversation.store import record_compaction
from app.engine.agent_loop import _extract_json, run_agent
from app.engine.events import emit
from app.engine.runners import NodeFailure
from app.meeting import store
from app.meeting.prompts import BASE_SYSTEM_PROMPT, call_tag, persona_block
from app.meeting.session import MeetingSession
from app.models.budget import ContextBudget, estimate_tokens, truncate_to_tokens
from app.models.pool import ProviderError, pool

Validator = Callable[[dict], list[str]]


class CallOutputError(RuntimeError):
    """模型的输出不符合要求的结构。"""


def parse_structured(content: str, validator: Validator | None = None) -> dict:
    data = _extract_json(strip_outer_fence(content))
    if data is None:
        raise CallOutputError("模型没有返回合法的 JSON 对象")
    issues = validator(data) if validator else []
    if issues:
        raise CallOutputError("；".join(issues))
    return data


def _model_for(participant: dict):
    """先取参会者绑定的模型，再取角色默认模型，再退到主持人的模型。"""
    model = None
    if participant.get("model_id"):
        try:
            model = pool.load_model(int(participant["model_id"]))
        except ProviderError:
            model = None
    if model is None and participant.get("role_key"):
        model = pool.pick_role_model(participant["role_key"])
    if model is None:
        model = pool.pick_role_model("facilitator")
    if model is None:
        raise NodeFailure(
            "尚未配置可用模型：请先在「设置 → 供应商」添加供应商与模型并测试连接", fatal=True)
    return model


def _phase(mtg: MeetingSession) -> str:
    return store.get_meeting(mtg.meeting_id)["phase"]


_TAIL_RESERVE = 300          # 尾部指令很短，压缩判断时按固定值预留
_SUMMARIZER = "你是会议记录员，负责把较早的发言压成摘要。"


async def _summarize_exchanges(mtg: MeetingSession, model, text: str) -> str:
    """把较早的逐字发言压成摘要。也走工具循环并带调用标识，Mock 与测试替身按标识路由。"""
    input_budget = ContextBudget(model.context_window, model.max_output_tokens).input_budget
    prompt = (
        "把下面这些会议发言压成一段摘要。保留：已经确定的结论及其理由、还没解决的问题、"
        "谁坚持什么。不要加入原文没有的信息。只输出摘要正文，不要标题。\n\n"
        f"{truncate_to_tokens(text, int(input_budget * 0.6))}\n\n{call_tag('summarize')}")
    result = await run_agent(
        run_id=mtg.run_id, node_id=mtg.node_id, attempt_id=mtg.ctx.attempt_id, model=model,
        system_prompt=BASE_SYSTEM_PROMPT, user_prompt=prompt, toolbox=None,
        max_steps=1, temperature=0.2, quiet=True)
    return strip_outer_fence(result.content).strip() or "（摘要为空）"


async def _fit(mtg: MeetingSession, model, segments: list[Segment],
               input_budget: int) -> list[Segment]:
    """追加区占到压缩阈值时，把其中较早的发言压成一条摘要。只改这次发出的视图，不改纪要。

    压缩只动追加区：冻结区（章程、人设、账本快照）是前缀缓存命中的部分，本轮内逐字节不变。
    """
    policy = DEFAULT_POLICY
    fixed = estimate_tokens(BASE_SYSTEM_PROMPT) + sum(
        estimate_tokens(s.text) for s in segments if s.kind == "frozen")
    append = [s for s in segments if s.kind == "append"]
    size = fixed + sum(estimate_tokens(s.text) for s in append) + _TAIL_RESERVE
    if len(append) <= policy.keep_recent or size < policy.compact_at * input_budget:
        return segments
    messages = [Message(role="system", content="系统")]
    messages += [Message(role=s.role, content=s.text) for s in append]
    compacted, before, after = await compact_messages(
        messages, summarize=lambda text: _summarize_exchanges(mtg, model, text),
        input_budget=max(1, input_budget - fixed - _TAIL_RESERVE), policy=policy)
    if compacted is messages:
        return segments
    await record_compaction(
        run_id=mtg.run_id, node_id=mtg.node_id, attempt_id=mtg.ctx.attempt_id, scope="meeting",
        summary=compacted[1].content, before_tokens=before + fixed, after_tokens=after + fixed)
    return [s for s in segments if s.kind == "frozen"] + [
        Segment("append", m.content, role=m.role, name="交锋摘要" if index == 0 else "较早发言")
        for index, m in enumerate(compacted[1:])]


async def speak(mtg: MeetingSession, participant: dict, *, kind: str, turn_kind: str,
                segments: list[Segment], instruction: str,
                charter: list[Segment] | None = None,
                structured: Validator | None = None, decision_point_id: int | None = None,
                temperature: float = 0.4, turn_round: int | None = None,
                drain: bool = True) -> dict:
    """让一个参会者发言一次。返回 {"turn_id", "content", "data"}。

    发出去的上下文依次是：共用的系统提示 → 会议章程（所有角色相同，由协议经 charter 传入）→
    这个角色的人设 → segments（账本快照与本轮的逐字发言）→ 尾部的指令与调用标识。
    这样排是为了前缀缓存（详设 §10.1）：从开头到人设之前，全体角色的内容完全相同。
    """
    if drain:
        await drain_interventions(mtg)
    model = _model_for(participant)
    persona = Segment("frozen", persona_block(participant),
                      name=f"角色人设:{participant.get('display_name')}")
    segments = list(charter or []) + [persona] + list(segments)
    budget = ContextBudget(model.context_window, model.max_output_tokens)
    segments = await _fit(mtg, model, segments, budget.input_budget)
    tail = Segment("tail", f"{instruction}\n\n{call_tag(kind)}", name="instruction")
    built = mtg.builder.build(segments + [tail], system=BASE_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
                              input_budget=budget.input_budget)
    if built.frozen_changed:
        await emit(mtg.run_id, "context_prefix_changed", mtg.node_id,
                   {"meeting_id": mtg.meeting_id, "segments": built.frozen_changed})
    speaker_id = participant["id"]
    speaker_name = participant["display_name"]
    turn_id = store.add_turn(
        mtg.meeting_id, round=turn_round or mtg.round_no(), phase=_phase(mtg),
        speaker_id=speaker_id, speaker_name=speaker_name, kind=turn_kind,
        decision_point_id=decision_point_id)
    await emit(mtg.run_id, "meeting_turn_started", mtg.node_id, {
        "meeting_id": mtg.meeting_id, "turn_id": turn_id, "speaker": speaker_name,
        "speaker_id": speaker_id, "kind": turn_kind, "decision_point_id": decision_point_id,
        "call": kind})
    result = await run_agent(
        run_id=mtg.run_id, node_id=mtg.node_id, attempt_id=mtg.ctx.attempt_id, model=model,
        system_prompt=BASE_SYSTEM_PROMPT, user_prompt="", toolbox=None, max_steps=1,
        temperature=temperature, quiet=True,
        token_extra={"turn_id": turn_id, "meeting_id": mtg.meeting_id},
        extra_messages=built.messages[1:])
    data = parse_structured(result.content, structured) if structured else None
    store.finish_turn(turn_id, content=result.content, structured=data,
                      tokens_in=result.usage.input_tokens, tokens_out=result.usage.output_tokens,
                      cached_tokens=result.usage.cached_tokens, model_id=model.id)
    await emit(mtg.run_id, "meeting_turn_finished", mtg.node_id, {
        "meeting_id": mtg.meeting_id, "turn_id": turn_id, "speaker": speaker_name,
        "kind": turn_kind, "tokens_in": result.usage.input_tokens,
        "tokens_out": result.usage.output_tokens, "cached_tokens": result.usage.cached_tokens,
        "summary": result.content[:200]})
    await emit(mtg.run_id, "node_log", mtg.node_id,
               {"level": "info", "message": f"【{speaker_name}】{result.content[:200]}"})
    return {"turn_id": turn_id, "content": result.content, "data": data}


async def speak_many(mtg: MeetingSession, calls: list[dict], *,
                     resumable: bool = True) -> list[dict]:
    """多个参会者并行发言，结果顺序与 calls 一致。

    resumable 为真时，这一步这一轮里已经有完整发言的参会者不再调用，直接取回原发言。
    轮次取 calls 里的 turn_round，没给时取节点级轮次。
    """
    if not calls:
        return []
    phase, round_no = _phase(mtg), calls[0].get("turn_round") or mtg.round_no()
    kind = calls[0]["turn_kind"]
    done: set[int] = set()
    if resumable:
        done = store.completed_speakers(mtg.meeting_id, phase=phase, round=round_no, kind=kind)
    results: list[dict | None] = [None] * len(calls)
    pending: list[int] = []
    for index, call in enumerate(calls):
        speaker_id = call["participant"]["id"]
        if speaker_id in done:
            found = store.find_turn(mtg.meeting_id, phase=phase, round=round_no,
                                    kind=kind, speaker_id=speaker_id)
            if found is not None:
                results[index] = found
                continue
        pending.append(index)
    if pending:
        await drain_interventions(mtg)
    outputs = await asyncio.gather(*(
        speak(mtg, drain=False, **calls[index]) for index in pending))
    for index, output in zip(pending, outputs):
        results[index] = output
    return [r for r in results if r is not None]


async def drain_interventions(mtg: MeetingSession) -> bool:
    """取出排队中的插话，写成用户发言，并让主持人回应一次。返回是否有插话。"""
    queued = store.take_queued_interventions(mtg.meeting_id)
    for item in queued:
        turn_id = store.add_turn(
            mtg.meeting_id, round=mtg.round_no(), phase=_phase(mtg), speaker_id=None,
            speaker_name="用户", kind="user_intervention", content=item["content"])
        store.attach_intervention_turn(item["id"], turn_id)
        await emit(mtg.run_id, "meeting_intervention_injected", mtg.node_id,
                   {"meeting_id": mtg.meeting_id, "intervention_id": item["id"], "turn_id": turn_id})
    if not queued:
        return False
    facilitator = next(iter(mtg.participants("facilitator")), None)
    if facilitator is not None:
        text = "\n".join(f"- {item['content']}" for item in queued)
        await speak(mtg, facilitator, kind="intervention_ack", turn_kind="facilitator_note",
                    segments=[Segment("append", f"## 用户在会议进行中插话\n{text}", name="插话")],
                    instruction="逐条回应用户的插话：你理解它要求什么，接下来怎么安排。只写一段话。",
                    drain=False)
    return True
```

`backend/app/meeting/runner.py` 的 `run_meeting`：把 `result = await protocol.run(mtg)` 这一行换成

```python
    from app.meeting.calls import CallOutputError

    try:
        result = await protocol.run(mtg)
    except CallOutputError as exc:
        # 结构化输出不合格：按普通节点失败处理，编排器会带反馈重试；重入时从当前步骤续开
        raise NodeFailure(f"模型输出不符合要求：{exc}") from exc
```

### Task B7: 插话与会议查询接口

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/app/api/meetings.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/main.py`（注册路由）
- Test: `backend/tests/test_meetings_api.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 任务 B2、B6 的存取函数；`gate_payload`；`open_checkpoint`
- Produces:
  - `GET /api/runs/{run_id}/meetings` → `{"items": [...]}`
  - `GET /api/runs/{run_id}/meetings/{meeting_id}` → `{"meeting", "participants", "decision_points", "checkpoints", "turn_count", "node_status"}`
  - `GET /api/runs/{run_id}/meetings/{meeting_id}/turns?since_seq=0&limit=200` → `{"items", "last_seq"}`
  - `PUT /api/runs/{run_id}/meetings/{meeting_id}/participants`，请求体 `{"participants": [{"role_key", "display_name", "persona", "model_id", "mode"}]}`；只在会议停在 `prepare`、`roster` 两步时允许；只替换参会者、评审者、质量评审者，不动主持人、需求方代理、主席
  - `PATCH /api/runs/{run_id}/meetings/{meeting_id}/decision-points/{point_id}`，请求体 `{"status"?, "conclusion"?}`；只在会议挂起时允许
  - `POST /api/runs/{run_id}/meetings/{meeting_id}/intervene`，请求体 `{"content"}`

```python
"""会议的查询与介入接口。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.db.base import init_db, session_scope
from app.db.models import Node, Run, Workflow
from app.engine.runners import NodeContext
from app.engine.templates import seed_defaults
from app.main import app
from app.meeting import store
from app.meeting.checkpoints import open_checkpoint
from app.meeting.session import MeetingSession

_counter = iter(range(1, 1000))


def _meeting(phase: str = "roster", suspend: bool = True) -> MeetingSession:
    init_db()
    seed_defaults()
    index = next(_counter)
    with session_scope() as s:
        workflow_id = s.query(Workflow).filter(Workflow.template_key == "sdlc_v1").one().id
        run_id = f"api-run-{index}"
        s.add(Run(id=run_id, workflow_id=workflow_id, status="running"))
        s.flush()
        node = Node(node_key="discuss", type="MEETING", name="讨论会议", run_id=run_id,
                    status="running", config_json={"protocol": "discussion"})
        s.add(node)
        s.flush()
        ctx = NodeContext(run_id=run_id, node_id=node.id, node_key="discuss", type="MEETING",
                          name="讨论会议", phase="", config={"protocol": "discussion"},
                          assignee={}, attempt_id=0, attempt_no=1)
    meeting_id = store.create_meeting(run_id=run_id, node_id=node.id, node_key="discuss",
                                      protocol="discussion", charter={}, config={})
    store.update_meeting(meeting_id, phase=phase)
    store.add_participant(meeting_id, role_key="facilitator", display_name="主持人",
                          mode="facilitator")
    mtg = MeetingSession(meeting_id=meeting_id, ctx=ctx)
    if suspend:
        asyncio.run(open_checkpoint(mtg, "roster", title="请确认名单", description="",
                                    payload={"proposed": []}))
    return mtg


def _client(monkeypatch) -> TestClient:
    return TestClient(app)


def test_detail_lists_participants_and_checkpoint(monkeypatch) -> None:
    mtg = _meeting()
    with _client(monkeypatch) as client:
        response = client.get(f"/api/runs/{mtg.run_id}/meetings/{mtg.meeting_id}")
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    body = response.json()
    assert body["meeting"]["protocol"] == "discussion"
    assert body["meeting"]["status"] == "suspended"
    assert [p["display_name"] for p in body["participants"]] == ["主持人"]
    assert body["checkpoints"][0]["kind"] == "roster"
    assert body["node_status"] == "waiting_human"


def test_list_meetings(monkeypatch) -> None:
    mtg = _meeting()
    with _client(monkeypatch) as client:
        body = client.get(f"/api/runs/{mtg.run_id}/meetings").json()
    assert [m["id"] for m in body["items"]] == [mtg.meeting_id]


def test_replace_participants_at_roster_checkpoint(monkeypatch) -> None:
    mtg = _meeting()
    payload = {"participants": [
        {"display_name": "架构", "persona": "你关注技术可行性。", "mode": "participant"},
        {"display_name": "合规顾问", "persona": "你关注数据合规。", "mode": "participant"},
    ]}
    with _client(monkeypatch) as client:
        response = client.put(f"/api/runs/{mtg.run_id}/meetings/{mtg.meeting_id}/participants",
                              json=payload)
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    rows = store.list_participants(mtg.meeting_id)
    # 主持人与需求方代理保留，参会者被整体替换
    assert [(r["display_name"], r["mode"]) for r in rows] == [
        ("主持人", "facilitator"), ("架构", "participant"), ("合规顾问", "participant")]
    assert rows[2]["source"] == "user" and rows[2]["role_key"] is None
    assert rows[2]["system_prompt"] == "你关注数据合规。"


def test_invalid_participant_mode_changes_nothing(monkeypatch) -> None:
    mtg = _meeting()
    store.add_participant(mtg.meeting_id, role_key="qa", display_name="测试",
                          mode="participant", system_prompt="你是测试")
    payload = {"participants": [{"display_name": "冒充主持人", "mode": "facilitator"}]}
    with _client(monkeypatch) as client:
        response = client.put(f"/api/runs/{mtg.run_id}/meetings/{mtg.meeting_id}/participants",
                              json=payload)
    assert response.status_code == 400
    assert [r["display_name"] for r in store.list_participants(mtg.meeting_id)] == ["主持人", "测试"]


def test_participants_cannot_be_changed_after_roster(monkeypatch) -> None:
    mtg = _meeting(phase="stances")
    with _client(monkeypatch) as client:
        response = client.put(f"/api/runs/{mtg.run_id}/meetings/{mtg.meeting_id}/participants",
                              json={"participants": []})
    assert response.status_code == 400


def test_patch_decision_point_while_suspended(monkeypatch) -> None:
    mtg = _meeting()
    point_id = store.upsert_decision_point(mtg.meeting_id, "D1", title="先做哪个平台",
                                           status="open")
    with _client(monkeypatch) as client:
        response = client.patch(
            f"/api/runs/{mtg.run_id}/meetings/{mtg.meeting_id}/decision-points/{point_id}",
            json={"status": "deferred", "conclusion": "留待用户决定"})
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    assert store.list_decision_points(mtg.meeting_id)[0]["status"] == "deferred"


def test_intervene_queues_request(monkeypatch) -> None:
    mtg = _meeting(phase="debate", suspend=False)
    with _client(monkeypatch) as client:
        response = client.post(f"/api/runs/{mtg.run_id}/meetings/{mtg.meeting_id}/intervene",
                               json={"content": "注意成本"})
        assert response.status_code == 201, response.text
        taken = store.take_queued_interventions(mtg.meeting_id)
    assert [t["content"] for t in taken] == ["注意成本"]
```
