- [x] **Step 3: 写接口**

`backend/app/api/meetings.py`：

```python
"""会议的查询与介入接口。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from sqlalchemy import func

from app.db.base import session_scope
from app.db.models import AgentTemplate, Gate, Meeting, MeetingIntervention, Node
from app.engine.events import emit
from app.engine.gates import gate_payload
from app.meeting import store

router = APIRouter(tags=["meetings"])

EDITABLE_MODES = ("participant", "reviewer", "quality_reviewer")
ROSTER_PHASES = ("prepare", "roster")
EDITABLE_POINT_STATUSES = ("open", "agreed", "deferred")
MAX_QUEUED_INTERVENTIONS = 20


class ParticipantIn(BaseModel):
    role_key: str | None = None
    display_name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=120)
    persona: str = ""                      # 临时角色的人设；有 role_key 时忽略
    model_id: int | None = None
    mode: str = "participant"


class RosterIn(BaseModel):
    participants: list[ParticipantIn]


class PointPatch(BaseModel):
    status: str | None = None
    conclusion: str | None = None


class InterveneIn(BaseModel):
    content: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=4000)


def _meeting_or_404(run_id: str, meeting_id: int) -> dict:
    meeting = store.get_meeting(meeting_id)
    if meeting["run_id"] != run_id:
        raise HTTPException(404, "会议不存在")
    return meeting


def _summary(meeting: dict) -> dict:
    return {"id": meeting["id"], "run_id": meeting["run_id"], "node_id": meeting["node_id"],
            "node_key": meeting["node_key"], "protocol": meeting["protocol"],
            "status": meeting["status"], "phase": meeting["phase"], "round": meeting["round"]}


@router.get("/runs/{run_id}/meetings")
def list_meetings(run_id: str) -> dict:
    with session_scope() as s:
        ids = [row.id for row in s.query(Meeting).filter(Meeting.run_id == run_id)
               .order_by(Meeting.id.asc()).all()]
    return {"items": [_summary(store.get_meeting(m)) for m in ids]}


@router.get("/runs/{run_id}/meetings/{meeting_id}")
def meeting_detail(run_id: str, meeting_id: int) -> dict:
    meeting = _meeting_or_404(run_id, meeting_id)
    with session_scope() as s:
        node = s.get(Node, meeting["node_id"])
        node_status = node.status if node else None
        checkpoints = [gate_payload(s, g) for g in
                       s.query(Gate).filter(Gate.node_id == meeting["node_id"])
                       .order_by(Gate.id.asc()).all()]
    return {"meeting": _summary(meeting), "participants": store.list_participants(meeting_id, False),
            "decision_points": store.list_decision_points(meeting_id),
            "checkpoints": checkpoints, "turn_count": store.count_turns(meeting_id),
            "node_status": node_status}


@router.get("/runs/{run_id}/meetings/{meeting_id}/turns")
def meeting_turns(run_id: str, meeting_id: int, since_seq: int = 0, limit: int = 200) -> dict:
    _meeting_or_404(run_id, meeting_id)
    items = store.list_turns(meeting_id, since_seq=since_seq, limit=max(1, min(limit, 1000)))
    return {"items": items, "last_seq": items[-1]["seq"] if items else since_seq}


@router.put("/runs/{run_id}/meetings/{meeting_id}/participants")
async def replace_participants(run_id: str, meeting_id: int, payload: RosterIn) -> dict:
    meeting = _meeting_or_404(run_id, meeting_id)
    if meeting["phase"] not in ROSTER_PHASES:
        raise HTTPException(400, "名单只能在会前准备和名单确认两步修改")
    # 先把请求全部校验完，再动现有的参会者：任何一条不合法都不改库
    prompts: list[str] = []
    with session_scope() as s:
        for item in payload.participants:
            if item.mode not in EDITABLE_MODES:
                raise HTTPException(400, f"不能指定模式为 {item.mode} 的角色")
            prompt = item.persona
            if item.role_key:
                template = (s.query(AgentTemplate)
                            .filter(AgentTemplate.role_key == item.role_key).first())
                if template is None:
                    raise HTTPException(400, f"角色模板不存在：{item.role_key}")
                prompt = template.system_prompt
            prompts.append(prompt)
    for row in store.list_participants(meeting_id):
        if row["mode"] in EDITABLE_MODES:
            store.update_participant(row["id"], status="removed")
    for item, prompt in zip(payload.participants, prompts):
        store.add_participant(meeting_id, role_key=item.role_key, display_name=item.display_name,
                              mode=item.mode, system_prompt=prompt, model_id=item.model_id,
                              source="user")
    await emit(run_id, "meeting_ledger_updated", meeting["node_id"],
               {"meeting_id": meeting_id, "version": 0, "decision_points": [], "roster": True})
    return {"meeting_id": meeting_id, "participants": store.list_participants(meeting_id)}


@router.patch("/runs/{run_id}/meetings/{meeting_id}/decision-points/{point_id}")
def patch_decision_point(run_id: str, meeting_id: int, point_id: int,
                         payload: PointPatch) -> dict:
    meeting = _meeting_or_404(run_id, meeting_id)
    if meeting["status"] != "suspended":
        raise HTTPException(400, "决策点只能在会议挂起时修改")
    points = store.list_decision_points(meeting_id)
    if not any(p["id"] == point_id for p in points):
        raise HTTPException(404, "决策点不存在")
    if payload.status is not None:
        if payload.status not in EDITABLE_POINT_STATUSES:
            raise HTTPException(400, f"状态只能是 {EDITABLE_POINT_STATUSES}")
        store.update_decision_point(point_id, status=payload.status)
    if payload.conclusion is not None:
        store.update_decision_point(point_id, conclusion=payload.conclusion)
    return next(p for p in store.list_decision_points(meeting_id) if p["id"] == point_id)


@router.post("/runs/{run_id}/meetings/{meeting_id}/intervene", status_code=201)
async def intervene(run_id: str, meeting_id: int, payload: InterveneIn) -> dict:
    meeting = _meeting_or_404(run_id, meeting_id)
    if meeting["status"] in ("completed", "abandoned"):
        raise HTTPException(400, "会议已结束，不能再插话")
    with session_scope() as s:
        queued = s.query(func.count(MeetingIntervention.id)).filter(
            MeetingIntervention.meeting_id == meeting_id,
            MeetingIntervention.status == "queued").scalar() or 0
    if queued >= MAX_QUEUED_INTERVENTIONS:
        raise HTTPException(400, "排队中的插话过多，请等会议处理后再提交")
    intervention_id = store.queue_intervention(meeting_id, content=payload.content)
    await emit(run_id, "meeting_intervention_queued", meeting["node_id"],
               {"meeting_id": meeting_id, "intervention_id": intervention_id})
    return {"id": intervention_id, "status": "queued"}
```

`backend/app/main.py`：导入区的 `from app.api import agent_templates, artifacts, gates, memories, model_entries, nodes` 这一行并入 `meetings`；`include_router` 的循环元组里加 `meetings`。

## 阶段 C：讨论协议、角色库、参数与模板

产出新的角色库、替换后的模板、参数表与运行级配置、背景材料、讨论协议，以及按调用标识路由的 Mock 供应商。对应详设 §5、§7、§11、§12.6。

### Task C1: 角色库、模板替换与自定义角色

角色库和模板必须一起换：角色一改，旧模板里的四个讨论节点就没有可用的角色了。所以本任务同时完成角色库、模板、参数表与自定义角色接口；讨论、评审、确认三种协议在后面的任务里实现，在那之前会议节点还不能运行。

模板换掉之后，此前建的运行不能再续跑：它们的节点还指向已经退役的角色。产品还没有对外使用，不做迁移；开发库里如果有这样的旧运行，删掉或清库即可。

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/app/agents/templates/roles.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/engine/templates.py`（指纹覆盖元数据；清理退役的内置角色）
- Modify: `templates/sdlc_workflow.json`（前半段整体替换；加 `params`、`automation_levels`、`rubrics`）
- Create: `backend/app/engine/params.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/api/agent_templates.py`、`backend/app/api/schemas.py`
- Delete: `backend/tests/test_discussion.py`
- Modify: `backend/tests/test_seed_templates.py`、`backend/tests/test_core.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_agent_templates_api.py`、`backend/tests/test_params.py`、`backend/tests/test_template_front_half.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `AgentTemplate.meta_json`、`Run.config_json`（任务 B1）
- Produces: 内置角色键 `facilitator`、`review_chair`、`architect`、`ux`、`qa`、`security`、`ops_cost`、`business`、`requester_proxy`、`judge`、`requirement`，以及不变的 `design`、`task_breaker`、`pm`、`review`、`dev`、`delivery`。每个角色带 `meta: {"description": str, "tags": list[str], "meeting_roles": list[str]}`
- Produces: `RETIRED_ROLES = ("discuss_pro", "discuss_con", "discuss_mod", "proposal")`
- Produces: 模板顶层的三块数据：`params`（按协议分组的参数表）、`automation_levels`（等级到各确认点判定者的映射）、`rubrics`（各类确认点的判据文本）
- Produces（`app/engine/params.py`）：
  - `merge_params(schema: dict, overrides: dict | None) -> tuple[dict, list[str]]`（生效值，错误列表）
  - `effective_params(definition: dict, config: dict | None) -> dict[str, dict]`（每个分组的生效值）
  - `validate_run_config(definition: dict, config: dict | None) -> list[str]`
  - `apply_run_config(run_id: str, definition: dict, config: dict | None, *, only_pending: bool = False) -> list[str]`
  - `decider_for(run_config: dict | None, definition: dict | None, checkpoint: str, default: str = "human") -> str`
  - `VALID_DECIDERS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]]`
  - `definition_of(run_id: str) -> dict`（运行的模板定义；等级预设和判据文本从这里读）
- Produces: `GET /api/agent-templates` 的每一项多 `builtin: bool`、`meta: dict`；`POST /api/agent-templates`；`DELETE /api/agent-templates/{id}`；`PATCH` 可改 `name`、`meta`

- `test_seed_is_idempotent` 的最后一行 `assert keys.count("discuss_mod") == 1` 改成 `assert keys.count("facilitator") == 1`。

```python
def test_retired_unmodified_role_is_removed() -> None:
    with session_scope() as s:
        s.add(AgentTemplate(
            role_key="discuss_con", name="讨论者·质疑", system_prompt="旧内置提示词",
            tools_json=[], memory_scope="agent", max_steps=3, stop_condition=[],
            builtin_hash="seed-hash"))
        s.flush()
        row = s.query(AgentTemplate).filter(AgentTemplate.role_key == "discuss_con").one()
        row.builtin_hash = builtin_fingerprint(template_fields(row))   # 字段与指纹一致：没被用户改过

    seed_defaults()

    with session_scope() as s:
        assert s.query(AgentTemplate).filter(AgentTemplate.role_key == "discuss_con").count() == 0


def test_retired_user_edited_role_is_kept_as_custom() -> None:
    with session_scope() as s:
        s.add(AgentTemplate(
            role_key="discuss_pro", name="讨论者·主张", system_prompt="我自己改过的提示词",
            tools_json=[], memory_scope="agent", max_steps=3, stop_condition=[],
            builtin_hash="a-stale-hash-that-does-not-match-the-fields"))

    seed_defaults()

    with session_scope() as s:
        row = s.query(AgentTemplate).filter(AgentTemplate.role_key == "discuss_pro").one()
        assert row.system_prompt == "我自己改过的提示词"
        assert row.builtin_hash is None      # 保留为自定义角色，之后不再被内置同步触碰


def test_retired_legacy_row_without_hash_is_kept() -> None:
    """旧库里的行没有指纹，分不清改没改过：保留，不删。"""
    with session_scope() as s:
        s.add(AgentTemplate(
            role_key="discuss_mod", name="讨论主持人", system_prompt="旧库里的一行",
            tools_json=[], memory_scope="agent", max_steps=3, stop_condition=[],
            builtin_hash=None))
    seed_defaults()
    with session_scope() as s:
        assert s.query(AgentTemplate).filter(AgentTemplate.role_key == "discuss_mod").count() == 1


def test_new_roles_carry_meeting_metadata() -> None:
    seed_defaults()
    assert _row("review_chair").meta_json["meeting_roles"] == ["chair"]
    assert _row("requester_proxy").meta_json["meeting_roles"] == ["proxy"]
    architect = _row("architect")
    assert architect.meta_json["meeting_roles"] == ["discussion", "review"]
    assert architect.meta_json["description"]
    assert _row("judge").meta_json["tags"] == ["裁决"]
```

`backend/tests/test_agent_templates_api.py`：

```python
"""自定义角色：新建、删除；内置角色只能改不能删。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.db.base import init_db, session_scope
from app.db.models import AgentTemplate
from app.engine.templates import seed_defaults
from app.main import app


def _client(monkeypatch) -> TestClient:
    init_db()
    seed_defaults()
    return TestClient(app)


def test_create_and_delete_custom_role(monkeypatch) -> None:
    with _client(monkeypatch) as client:
        created = client.post("/api/agent-templates", json={
            "role_key": "data_expert", "name": "数据专家",
            "system_prompt": "你关注数据模型与查询成本。",
            "meta": {"description": "数据建模与查询成本", "tags": ["数据"],
                     "meeting_roles": ["discussion", "review"]},
        })
        assert created.status_code == 201, created.text
        body = created.json()
        assert body["builtin"] is False and body["meta"]["tags"] == ["数据"]
        duplicate = client.post("/api/agent-templates", json={
            "role_key": "data_expert", "name": "重复", "system_prompt": "x"})
        assert duplicate.status_code == 400
        patched = client.patch(f"/api/agent-templates/{body['id']}",
                               json={"name": "数据与算法", "meta": {"tags": ["数据", "算法"]}})
        assert patched.json()["name"] == "数据与算法"
        assert client.delete(f"/api/agent-templates/{body['id']}").status_code == 200
        with session_scope() as s:
            assert s.query(AgentTemplate).filter(
                AgentTemplate.role_key == "data_expert").count() == 0


def test_builtin_role_cannot_be_deleted(monkeypatch) -> None:
    with _client(monkeypatch) as client:
        rows = client.get("/api/agent-templates").json()["items"]
        chair = next(r for r in rows if r["role_key"] == "review_chair")
        assert chair["builtin"] is True
        assert client.delete(f"/api/agent-templates/{chair['id']}").status_code == 400


def test_bad_role_key_is_rejected(monkeypatch) -> None:
    with _client(monkeypatch) as client:
        response = client.post("/api/agent-templates", json={
            "role_key": "Bad Key", "name": "x", "system_prompt": "x"})
        assert response.status_code == 422
```

`backend/tests/test_params.py`：

```python
"""参数表：硬限校验、默认值合并、跨字段规则；判定者的解析。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from app.engine.params import decider_for, effective_params, merge_params, validate_run_config

SCHEMA = {
    "rounds_max": {"type": "int", "default": 20, "min": 1, "max": 99, "title": "讨论轮次上限"},
    "closure_rule": {"type": "str", "default": "unanimous",
                     "choices": ["unanimous", "no_objection", "facilitator_rules"],
                     "title": "闭合规则"},
    "participants_min": {"type": "int", "default": 3, "min": 1, "max": 99, "title": "参会人数下限"},
    "participants_max": {"type": "int", "default": 5, "min": 1, "max": 99, "title": "参会人数上限"},
}
DEFINITION = {
    "params": {"discussion": SCHEMA},
    "automation_levels": {
        "L1": {"consensus": "human", "proposal": "human"},
        "L2": {"consensus": "program", "proposal": "human"},
        "L3": {"consensus": "program_fallback", "proposal": "auto"},
    },
}


def test_defaults_when_no_override() -> None:
    values, errors = merge_params(SCHEMA, None)
    assert errors == []
    assert values == {"rounds_max": 20, "closure_rule": "unanimous",
                      "participants_min": 3, "participants_max": 5}


def test_override_within_bounds_and_unknown_keys_ignored() -> None:
    values, errors = merge_params(SCHEMA, {"rounds_max": "7", "not_a_param": 1})
    assert errors == [] and values["rounds_max"] == 7


def test_out_of_bounds_and_bad_choice_are_errors() -> None:
    _, errors = merge_params(SCHEMA, {"rounds_max": 0, "closure_rule": "whatever",
                                      "participants_max": "abc"})
    assert any("讨论轮次上限" in e and "1" in e for e in errors)
    assert any("闭合规则" in e for e in errors)
    assert any("参会人数上限" in e for e in errors)


def test_min_greater_than_max_is_an_error() -> None:
    _, errors = merge_params(SCHEMA, {"participants_min": 6, "participants_max": 4})
    assert errors == ["参会人数的下限不能大于上限"]


def test_effective_params_and_run_config_validation() -> None:
    effective = effective_params(DEFINITION, {"params": {"discussion": {"rounds_max": 9}}})
    assert effective["discussion"]["rounds_max"] == 9
    assert validate_run_config(DEFINITION, {"params": {"discussion": {"rounds_max": 9}}}) == []
    assert validate_run_config(DEFINITION, {"automation": {"level": "L9"}})
    assert validate_run_config(DEFINITION, {"params": {"nope": {}}})


def test_decider_resolution_prefers_override_then_level() -> None:
    assert decider_for(None, DEFINITION, "consensus") == "program"          # 默认 L2
    assert decider_for({"automation": {"level": "L1"}}, DEFINITION, "consensus") == "human"
    assert decider_for({"automation": {"checkpoints": {"consensus": "human"}}},
                       DEFINITION, "consensus") == "human"
    assert decider_for({"automation": {"level": "L3"}}, DEFINITION, "proposal") == "auto"
    assert decider_for({"automation": {"level": "L3"}}, DEFINITION, "unknown_point") == "human"


def test_gates_of_the_template_can_opt_into_model_judging() -> None:
    definition = {**DEFINITION, "nodes": [{"key": "gate_design", "type": "HUMAN_GATE"}]}
    assert validate_run_config(definition, {"automation": {"checkpoints": {"gate_design": "model"}}}) == []
    assert validate_run_config(definition, {"automation": {"checkpoints": {"gate_design": "auto"}}})
    assert validate_run_config(definition, {"automation": {"checkpoints": {"gate_tasks": "model"}}})
```

`backend/tests/test_template_front_half.py`：

```python
"""模板的前半段：节点、依赖、参数表、自动化等级预设；运行级配置写进会议节点。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from app.db.base import init_db, session_scope
from app.db.models import Node, Run, Workflow
from app.engine.params import apply_run_config
from app.engine.templates import instantiate_nodes, seed_defaults, workflow_definition


def test_front_half_nodes_and_dependencies() -> None:
    definition = workflow_definition("sdlc_v1")
    nodes = {node["key"]: node for node in definition["nodes"]}
    assert nodes["discuss"]["type"] == "MEETING"
    assert nodes["discuss"]["config"]["protocol"] == "discussion"
    assert nodes["review"]["deps"] == ["gate_proposal", "discuss"]
    assert nodes["review"]["config"]["protocol"] == "review"
    assert nodes["confirm"]["deps"] == ["requirement"]
    assert nodes["design"]["deps"] == ["gate_requirement"]
    for key in ("discuss_pro", "discuss_con", "discuss_response", "discuss_mod"):
        assert key not in nodes
    # 后半段的节点保持不变
    for key in ("design", "tasks", "taskgraph", "gate_tasks", "expand_exec", "verify",
                "gate_accept", "delivery"):
        assert key in nodes
    keys = [phase["key"] for phase in definition["phases"]]
    assert keys == ["idea", "discussion", "proposal", "review", "requirement",
                    "design", "tasks", "execution", "delivery"]


def test_params_and_levels_are_declared() -> None:
    definition = workflow_definition("sdlc_v1")
    discussion = definition["params"]["discussion"]
    assert discussion["rounds_max"]["default"] == 20
    assert discussion["participants_min"]["default"] == 3
    assert discussion["participants_max"]["max"] == 99
    assert definition["automation_levels"]["L2"]["consensus"] == "program"
    assert set(definition["rubrics"]) == {"proposal", "review", "requirement", "artifact"}


def test_run_config_is_applied_to_meeting_nodes() -> None:
    init_db()
    seed_defaults()
    config = {"params": {"discussion": {"rounds_max": 6}}}
    with session_scope() as s:
        workflow_id = s.query(Workflow).filter(Workflow.template_key == "sdlc_v1").one().id
        s.add(Run(id="params-run", workflow_id=workflow_id, status="created", config_json=config))
        s.flush()
    definition = workflow_definition("sdlc_v1")
    instantiate_nodes("params-run", definition)
    assert apply_run_config("params-run", definition, config) == []
    with session_scope() as s:
        nodes = {n.node_key: n for n in s.query(Node).filter(Node.run_id == "params-run").all()}
    assert nodes["discuss"].config_json["params"]["rounds_max"] == 6
    assert nodes["discuss"].config_json["params"]["closure_rule"] == "unanimous"
    assert nodes["review"].config_json["max_rounds"] == 3       # 复审轮次上限取自参数表
    assert "params" not in nodes["design"].config_json           # 非会议节点不受影响
```

```python
# 不再内置的角色：启动时清理没被用户改过的；改过的和旧库里没有指纹的保留为自定义角色
RETIRED_ROLES = ("discuss_pro", "discuss_con", "discuss_mod", "proposal")
```

5. `seed_defaults` 里，`existing = {r.role_key: r for r in s.query(AgentTemplate).all()}` 这一行换成下面的代码。只有"字段与指纹一致"的行才清理；用户改过的，以及旧库里没有指纹、无从判断的，都保留为自定义角色（之后可以用接口删除）：

```python
        for role_key in RETIRED_ROLES:
            retired = s.query(AgentTemplate).filter(AgentTemplate.role_key == role_key).first()
            if retired is None:
                continue
            unmodified = (retired.builtin_hash
                          and template_fingerprint(retired) == retired.builtin_hash)
            if unmodified:
                s.delete(retired)                # 没改过：清理
                continue
            retired.builtin_hash = None          # 改过，或旧库没有指纹：保留为自定义角色，之后不再触碰
        s.flush()
        existing = {r.role_key: r for r in s.query(AgentTemplate).all()}
```

```json
      "stall_rounds": {"type": "int", "default": 2, "min": 1, "max": 9, "title": "停滞判定轮数",
                       "hint": "连续几轮无人改变立场就进入共识确认点"},
      "proposal_check_rounds": {"type": "int", "default": 2, "min": 1, "max": 9, "title": "方案复核轮次上限"},
      "closure_rule": {"type": "str", "default": "unanimous", "choices": ["unanimous", "no_objection", "facilitator_rules"],
                       "title": "闭合规则"},
      "proxy_enabled": {"type": "bool", "default": false, "title": "启用需求方代理"}
    },
    "review": {
      "reviewers_min": {"type": "int", "default": 0, "min": 0, "max": 99, "title": "评审者人数下限",
                        "hint": "0 表示不限制"},
      "reviewers_max": {"type": "int", "default": 0, "min": 0, "max": 99, "title": "评审者人数上限",
                        "hint": "0 表示不限制；建议至少一名评审者绑定不同家族的模型"},
      "pre_brief": {"type": "bool", "default": true, "title": "评审前先写要点"},

      "min_blocking_severity": {"type": "str", "default": "major", "choices": ["blocker", "major"],
                                "title": "阻塞线"}

    "confirm": {

                   "title": "核对覆盖方式"},
      "expected_first": {"type": "bool", "default": true, "title": "先写预期再核对"},
      "rounds_max": {"type": "int", "default": 3, "min": 1, "max": 9, "title": "需求复核轮次上限"},
      "min_blocking_severity": {"type": "str", "default": "major", "choices": ["blocker", "major"],
                                "title": "阻塞线"}

    "gates": {

    "context": {
      "compact_at": {"type": "float", "default": 0.8, "min": 0.5, "max": 0.95, "title": "压缩触发阈值"},
      "compact_to": {"type": "float", "default": 0.5, "min": 0.2, "max": 0.6, "title": "压缩后目标占用",
                     "hint": "须比触发阈值至少低 20 个百分点"}
    },
    "meeting": {
      "token_budget": {"type": "int", "default": 0, "min": 0, "max": 100000000, "title": "会议 token 预算",
                       "hint": "0 表示不限，受运行总预算约束"}

  "rubrics": {

    "review": "判断方案是否可以确认：评审结论须为接受，或有条件接受且文字级修订已经过验证；逐条核对仍有争议的决策级意见是否已妥善处理。",

    "artifact": "判断这份工件是否完整、内部一致、可供下一环节直接使用。只依据工件本身与给定的检查单判断。"
  },
```

- [x] **Step 6: 写参数表模块**

`backend/app/engine/params.py`：

```python
"""参数表与运行级配置。

参数表、自动化等级预设、各类确认点的判据文本都写在模板 JSON 里：
- params：按协议分组，每项含 type、default，以及 min、max、choices、title、recommend、hint
- automation_levels：自动化等级 → 各确认点的判定者
- rubrics：确认点的判据文本
运行级配置（runs.config_json）：
- automation: {"level": "L2", "checkpoints": {"consensus": "human", ...}}
- params: {"discussion": {"rounds_max": 10}, ...}
- rosters: {"participants": {...}, "reviewers": [...]}
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from app.db.base import session_scope
from app.db.models import Node, Run, Workflow
from app.engine.templates import workflow_definition

DEFAULT_LEVEL = "L2"

# 每个确认点允许的判定者
VALID_DECIDERS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    "clarify": ("human", "proxy"),
    "roster": ("human", "auto"),
    "consensus": ("human", "program", "program_fallback"),
    "proposal": ("human", "auto"),
    "review": ("human", "model"),
    "requirement": ("human", "model"),
}

_KIND_LABEL = {"int": "整数", "float": "数字", "bool": "布尔值"}


def _coerce(key: str, spec: dict, raw) -> tuple[object | None, str | None]:
    kind = spec.get("type", "str")
    title = spec.get("title", key)
    try:
        if kind == "int":
            if isinstance(raw, bool):
                raise ValueError
            value: object = int(raw)
        elif kind == "float":
            value = float(raw)
        elif kind == "bool":
            value = (str(raw).strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes", "on")) if isinstance(raw, str) else bool(raw)
        else:
            value = str(raw)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None, f"{title}须是{_KIND_LABEL.get(kind, '文本')}"
    if kind in ("int", "float"):
        if "min" in spec and value < spec["min"]:
            return None, f"{title}不能小于 {spec['min']}"
        if "max" in spec and value > spec["max"]:
            return None, f"{title}不能大于 {spec['max']}"
    if spec.get("choices") and value not in spec["choices"]:
        return None, f"{title}只能是 {'、'.join(map(str, spec['choices']))} 之一"
    return value, None


def _cross_rules(values: dict) -> list[str]:
    errors: list[str] = []
    pairs = (("participants_min", "participants_max", "参会人数"),
             ("reviewers_min", "reviewers_max", "评审者人数"))
    for low, high, title in pairs:
        if low in values and high in values and values[low] > values[high]:
            errors.append(f"{title}的下限不能大于上限")
    if "compact_at" in values and "compact_to" in values:
        if values["compact_to"] > values["compact_at"] - 0.2:
            errors.append("压缩后目标占用须比触发阈值至少低 20 个百分点")
    return errors


def merge_params(schema: dict, overrides: dict | None) -> tuple[dict, list[str]]:
    """返回 (生效值, 错误列表)。不在参数表里的覆盖项被忽略。"""
    overrides = overrides or {}
    values: dict = {}
    errors: list[str] = []
    for key, spec in schema.items():
        value, error = _coerce(key, spec, overrides.get(key, spec.get("default")))
        if error:
            errors.append(error)
            value = spec.get("default")
        values[key] = value
    return values, errors + _cross_rules(values)


def effective_params(definition: dict, config: dict | None) -> dict[str, dict]:
    """每个参数分组的生效值：模板默认值叠加运行级配置。有错误的项回落到默认值。"""
    config = config or {}
    overrides = config.get("params") or {}
    return {section: merge_params(schema, overrides.get(section))[0]
            for section, schema in (definition.get("params") or {}).items()}


def _deciders_for(definition: dict, checkpoint: str) -> tuple[str, ...] | None:
    """某个确认点允许的判定者；不是确认点时返回 None。

    模板里的人工门节点也可以按节点键逐个配置（设计之后的门想用模型判定时这样做），
    这类确认点的判定者只能是人或模型。
    """
    if checkpoint in VALID_DECIDERS:
        return VALID_DECIDERS[checkpoint]
    is_gate = any(node.get("type") == "HUMAN_GATE" and node.get("key") == checkpoint
                  for node in definition.get("nodes") or [])
    return ("human", "model") if is_gate else None


def validate_run_config(definition: dict, config: dict | None) -> list[str]:
    config = config or {}
    errors: list[str] = []
    automation = dict(config.get("automation") or {})
    level = str(automation.get("level") or DEFAULT_LEVEL)
    if level not in (definition.get("automation_levels") or {}):
        errors.append(f"未知的自动化等级：{level}")
    for checkpoint, decider in dict(automation.get("checkpoints") or {}).items():
        allowed = _deciders_for(definition, checkpoint)
        if allowed is None:
            errors.append(f"未知的确认点：{checkpoint}")
        elif decider not in allowed:
            errors.append(f"确认点 {checkpoint} 的判定者只能是 {allowed} 之一")
    params = definition.get("params") or {}
    for section, values in dict(config.get("params") or {}).items():
        if section not in params:
            errors.append(f"未知的参数分组：{section}")
            continue
        errors += merge_params(params[section], values)[1]
    return errors


def decider_for(run_config: dict | None, definition: dict | None, checkpoint: str,
                default: str = "human") -> str:
    """逐确认点覆盖 > 自动化等级预设 > 默认值。"""
    automation = dict((run_config or {}).get("automation") or {})
    override = dict(automation.get("checkpoints") or {}).get(checkpoint)
    if override:
        return str(override)
    levels = dict((definition or {}).get("automation_levels") or {})
    preset = levels.get(str(automation.get("level") or DEFAULT_LEVEL)) or {}
    return str(preset.get(checkpoint) or default)


def apply_run_config(run_id: str, definition: dict, config: dict | None,
                     *, only_pending: bool = False) -> list[str]:
    """把生效参数写进会议节点的 config.params；复审与需求复核的轮次上限写进 max_rounds。

    only_pending 为真时只处理还没开始执行的会议节点。返回参数校验的错误列表。
    """
    params = effective_params(definition, config)
    errors = [error for section in params for error in merge_params(
        (definition.get("params") or {}).get(section, {}),
        ((config or {}).get("params") or {}).get(section))[1]]
    with session_scope() as s:
        for node in s.query(Node).filter(Node.run_id == run_id, Node.type == "MEETING").all():
            if only_pending and node.status not in ("pending", "ready"):
                continue
            protocol = (node.config_json or {}).get("protocol")
            if protocol not in params:
                continue
            config_json = dict(node.config_json or {})
            config_json["params"] = params[protocol]
            if protocol in ("review", "confirm"):
                config_json["max_rounds"] = params[protocol]["rounds_max"]
            node.config_json = config_json
    return errors


def run_of(run_id: str) -> dict:
    """取运行的模板键与运行级配置。"""
    with session_scope() as s:
        run = s.get(Run, run_id)
        if run is None:
            return {"template_key": "", "config": {}}
        workflow = s.get(Workflow, run.workflow_id)
        return {"template_key": workflow.template_key if workflow else "",
                "config": dict(run.config_json or {})}


def definition_of(run_id: str) -> dict:
    """运行的模板定义：自动化等级预设与判据文本从这里读。"""
    template_key = run_of(run_id)["template_key"]
    return workflow_definition(template_key) if template_key else {}
```


- [x] **Step 7: 加自定义角色的接口**

`backend/app/api/schemas.py` 加：

```python
class TemplateCreate(BaseModel):
    role_key: str = Field(min_length=2, max_length=40, pattern=r"^[a-z][a-z0-9_]+$")
    name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=120)
    system_prompt: str = Field(min_length=1)
    tools_json: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    memory_scope: str = "agent"
    max_steps: int = Field(default=3, ge=1, le=64)
    default_model_id: int | None = None
    meta: dict = Field(default_factory=dict)
```

`TemplatePatch` 加两个字段：

```python
    name: str | None = Field(default=None, min_length=1, max_length=120)
    meta: dict | None = None
```

`backend/app/api/agent_templates.py`：导入 `TemplateCreate`；`_serialize` 的返回字典里加两项 `"builtin": bool(row.builtin_hash),` 与 `"meta": dict(row.meta_json or {}),`；`update_template` 整个函数换成下面第一个函数（请求里的 `meta` 写到表的 `meta_json` 列）；文件末尾加后面两个函数：

```python
@router.patch("/agent-templates/{template_id}")
def update_template(template_id: int, payload: TemplatePatch) -> dict:
    with session_scope() as s:
        row = s.get(AgentTemplate, template_id)
        if row is None:
            raise HTTPException(404, "角色模板不存在")
        data = payload.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)
        meta = data.pop("meta", None)
        if data.get("default_model_id"):
            if s.get(ModelEntry, data["default_model_id"]) is None:
                raise HTTPException(400, "指定的模型不存在")
        if meta is not None:
            row.meta_json = dict(meta)
        for key, value in data.items():
            setattr(row, key, value)
        return _serialize(row)


@router.post("/agent-templates", status_code=201)
def create_template(payload: TemplateCreate) -> dict:
    with session_scope() as s:
        if s.query(AgentTemplate).filter(AgentTemplate.role_key == payload.role_key).first():
            raise HTTPException(400, f"角色键已存在：{payload.role_key}")
        if payload.default_model_id and s.get(ModelEntry, payload.default_model_id) is None:
            raise HTTPException(400, "指定的模型不存在")
        row = AgentTemplate(
            role_key=payload.role_key, name=payload.name, system_prompt=payload.system_prompt,
            tools_json=list(payload.tools_json), memory_scope=payload.memory_scope,
            max_steps=payload.max_steps, default_model_id=payload.default_model_id,
            stop_condition=[], meta_json=dict(payload.meta), builtin_hash=None)
        s.add(row)
        s.flush()
        return _serialize(row)


@router.delete("/agent-templates/{template_id}")
def delete_template(template_id: int) -> dict:
    with session_scope() as s:
        row = s.get(AgentTemplate, template_id)
        if row is None:
            raise HTTPException(404, "角色模板不存在")
        if row.builtin_hash:
            raise HTTPException(400, "内置角色只能编辑，不能删除")
        s.delete(row)
        s.flush()
    return {"deleted": template_id}
```

### Task C2: 运行级配置、背景材料与带反馈的修订

任务 C1 让模板带上了参数表，但还没有入口把用户的配置传进来。本任务补上这条线：新建运行时可以提交运行级配置和背景材料，运行中可以改配置；文档节点带着反馈重跑时在上一版的基础上修订，而不是从头重写。对应详设 §5.5、§12.3、§12.4。

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/app/engine/materials.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/config.py`（背景材料的两个上限）
- Modify: `backend/app/api/schemas.py`（`RunIn` 加配置与材料；新增 `MaterialIn`、`RunConfigPatch`）
- Modify: `backend/app/api/runs.py`（新建运行；背景材料、运行级配置、模板定义的端点）
- Modify: `backend/app/engine/params.py`（名单配置的结构校验）
- Modify: `backend/app/engine/templates.py`（读角色库的两个函数）
- Modify: `backend/app/meeting/session.py`（六个方法）
- Modify: `backend/app/conversation/policy.py`（`policy_for_run`、`reset_policy_cache`）
- Modify: `backend/app/engine/agent_loop.py`、`backend/app/meeting/calls.py`（压缩阈值取自运行级配置）
- Modify: `backend/app/engine/runners.py`（文档节点带反馈时修订上一版）
- Test: `backend/tests/test_run_config.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `apply_run_config`、`validate_run_config`、`run_of`、`definition_of`（任务 C1）
- Produces（`app/engine/materials.py`）：`check_materials(items: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> list[str]`；`save_material(run_id, name, content) -> dict`；`list_materials(run_id) -> list[dict]`；`total_chars(run_id) -> int`；`read_material(run_id, name) -> str`；`read_materials(run_id, budget: int) -> tuple[str, bool]`
- Produces: `settings.materials_max_chars`（默认 40000 字）、`settings.materials_context_tokens`（默认 6000）
- Produces: `POST /api/runs` 的请求体多 `config`（`automation`、`params`、`rosters`）和 `materials`（`[{"name", "content"}]`）；不合法时返回 400，`detail` 里是逐条的错误
- Produces: `GET /api/templates/{template_key}`；`GET`、`PATCH /api/runs/{run_id}/config`；`GET /api/runs/{run_id}/materials`、`POST`（201）、`GET /api/runs/{run_id}/materials/{name}`
- Produces: 运行快照的 `run` 里多 `config`
- Produces: `validate_rosters(rosters: dict | None) -> list[str]`；`role_library() -> list[dict]`；`role_template(role_key: str) -> dict | None`
- Produces（`MeetingSession`）：`meeting() -> dict`、`meeting_round() -> int`、`reset_builder() -> None`、`run_config() -> dict`、`decider(checkpoint: str) -> str`、`materials() -> tuple[str, bool]`
- Produces: `policy_for_run(run_id: str) -> ContextPolicy`；`reset_policy_cache(run_id: str) -> None`

运行级配置的结构（存在 `runs.config_json`）：

```json
{
  "automation": {"level": "L2", "checkpoints": {"consensus": "human"}},
  "params": {"discussion": {"rounds_max": 10}},
  "rosters": {
    "participants": {"role_keys": ["architect", "ux"]},
    "reviewers": {"count": 2}
  }
}
```

名单的每一组有三种写法：`role_keys` 是点名具体角色；`count` 是固定人数，由主持人在这个人数上挑角色；两个都不写就由主持人在参数表的上下限内自己定。

- [x] **Step 1: 写失败的测试**

`backend/tests/test_run_config.py`：

```python
"""运行级配置、背景材料、带反馈的修订。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from types import SimpleNamespace

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.artifacts.store import parse_front_matter, read_artifact, write_artifact
from app.config import settings
from app.conversation.policy import policy_for_run
from app.db.base import init_db, session_scope
from app.db.models import Node, Run, Workflow
from app.engine import runners
from app.engine.materials import read_materials
from app.engine.orchestrator import orchestrator
from app.engine.params import validate_rosters
from app.engine.templates import (
    instantiate_nodes, role_library, role_template, seed_defaults, workflow_definition,
)
from app.main import app

IDEA = "给小团队做一个反馈收集工具"
_counter = iter(range(1, 1000))
REVISED = (
    "# 需求规格\n\n## 1. 范围\n\n覆盖从收集到导出的最小闭环，不含实时协作。\n\n"
    "### R1 收集反馈\n\nWHEN 用户提交反馈 THE SYSTEM SHALL 保存并显示确认。\n\n"
    "验收标准：\n- Given 已登录，When 提交，Then 保存成功并提示。\n\n"
    "修改说明：按反馈补充了确认提示。\n\n"
    "### R2 导出\n\nWHEN 用户点击导出 THE SYSTEM SHALL 生成文件。\n\n"
    "验收标准：\n- Given 有数据，When 导出，Then 下载成功。\n\n"
    "### R3 失败提示\n\nWHEN 保存失败 THE SYSTEM SHALL 显示可读的错误。\n\n"
    "验收标准：\n- Given 服务不可用，When 提交，Then 提示稍后重试。\n"
)


def _client(monkeypatch) -> TestClient:
    init_db()
    seed_defaults()
    monkeypatch.setattr(orchestrator, "start", lambda run_id: None)     # 不真正开始调度
    return TestClient(app)


def _create(client: TestClient, **extra) -> dict:
    response = client.post("/api/runs", json={"idea": IDEA, **extra})
    assert response.status_code == 201, response.text
    return response.json()


def _node(run_id: str, key: str) -> Node:
    with session_scope() as s:
        return s.query(Node).filter(Node.run_id == run_id, Node.node_key == key).one()


def test_run_config_reaches_meeting_nodes(monkeypatch) -> None:
    with _client(monkeypatch) as client:
        snapshot = _create(client, config={
            "params": {"discussion": {"rounds_max": 6},
                       "context": {"compact_at": 0.6, "compact_to": 0.3}}})
    run_id = snapshot["run"]["id"]
    assert snapshot["run"]["config"]["params"]["discussion"]["rounds_max"] == 6
    assert _node(run_id, "discuss").config_json["params"]["rounds_max"] == 6
    assert _node(run_id, "review").config_json["max_rounds"] == 3
    assert policy_for_run(run_id).compact_at == 0.6


def test_bad_run_config_is_rejected_with_reasons(monkeypatch) -> None:
    with _client(monkeypatch) as client:
        bad_level = client.post("/api/runs", json={
            "idea": IDEA, "config": {"automation": {"level": "L9"}}})
        unknown_role = client.post("/api/runs", json={
            "idea": IDEA, "config": {"rosters": {"participants": {"role_keys": ["no_such_role"]}}}})
        bad_count = client.post("/api/runs", json={
            "idea": IDEA, "config": {"rosters": {"participants": {"count": 0}}}})
        out_of_range = client.post("/api/runs", json={
            "idea": IDEA, "config": {"params": {"discussion": {"rounds_max": 0}}}})
    assert bad_level.status_code == 400 and "未知的自动化等级" in bad_level.json()["detail"]
    assert unknown_role.status_code == 400
    assert "角色模板不存在：no_such_role" in unknown_role.json()["detail"]
    assert bad_count.status_code == 400 and "固定人数" in bad_count.json()["detail"]
    assert out_of_range.status_code == 400 and "讨论轮次上限" in out_of_range.json()["detail"]


def test_materials_are_saved_listed_and_bounded(monkeypatch) -> None:
    with _client(monkeypatch) as client:
        run_id = _create(client, materials=[{"name": "需求片段", "content": "要能导出周报"}])["run"]["id"]
        added = client.post(f"/api/runs/{run_id}/materials", json={"content": "预算有限"})
        listed = client.get(f"/api/runs/{run_id}/materials")
        fetched = client.get(f"/api/runs/{run_id}/materials/需求片段")
        monkeypatch.setattr(settings, "materials_max_chars", 30)
        oversized = client.post(f"/api/runs/{run_id}/materials", json={"content": "字" * 40})
        bad_name = client.post(f"/api/runs/{run_id}/materials",
                               json={"name": "a/b", "content": "x"})
    assert added.status_code == 201 and added.json()["name"] == "材料2"
    assert [item["name"] for item in listed.json()["items"]] == ["需求片段", "材料2"]
    assert fetched.json()["content"] == "要能导出周报"
    assert oversized.status_code == 400 and "总量" in oversized.json()["detail"]
    assert bad_name.status_code == 400
    text, truncated = read_materials(run_id, budget=5)
    assert truncated is True and "仅提供了部分材料" in text


def test_run_config_can_be_changed_before_or_between_checkpoints(monkeypatch) -> None:
    with _client(monkeypatch) as client:
        run_id = _create(client)["run"]["id"]
        patched = client.patch(f"/api/runs/{run_id}/config", json={
            "params": {"discussion": {"rounds_max": 4}}, "automation": {"level": "L1"}})
        with session_scope() as s:
            s.get(Run, run_id).status = "running"
        refused = client.patch(f"/api/runs/{run_id}/config", json={"automation": {"level": "L1"}})
        shown = client.get(f"/api/runs/{run_id}/config")
    assert patched.status_code == 200, patched.text
    assert _node(run_id, "discuss").config_json["params"]["rounds_max"] == 4
    assert refused.status_code == 400
    assert shown.json()["config"]["automation"] == {"level": "L1"}


def test_template_endpoint_exposes_params_and_levels(monkeypatch) -> None:
    with _client(monkeypatch) as client:
        body = client.get("/api/templates/sdlc_v1").json()
        missing = client.get("/api/templates/nope")
    assert body["params"]["discussion"]["rounds_max"]["max"] == 99
    assert body["automation_levels"]["L2"]["consensus"] == "program"
    assert set(body["rubrics"]) == {"proposal", "review", "requirement", "artifact"}
    assert missing.status_code == 404


def test_role_library_helpers() -> None:
    init_db()
    seed_defaults()
    assert {"facilitator", "architect", "judge"} <= {row["role_key"] for row in role_library()}
    assert role_template("architect")["meta"]["meeting_roles"] == ["discussion", "review"]
    assert role_template("nope") is None


def test_roster_structure_is_validated() -> None:
    assert validate_rosters({"participants": {"role_keys": ["architect"]},
                             "reviewers": {"count": 2}}) == []
    assert validate_rosters({"participants": {"role_keys": ["architect"], "count": 2}})
    assert validate_rosters({"reviewers": {"count": 100}})
    assert validate_rosters({"others": {}})


def _doc_node(run_id: str) -> runners.NodeContext:
    with session_scope() as s:
        node = s.query(Node).filter(Node.run_id == run_id, Node.node_key == "requirement").one()
        return runners.NodeContext(
            run_id=run_id, node_id=node.id, node_key="requirement", type="AGENT", name=node.name,
            phase=node.phase, config=dict(node.config_json), assignee=dict(node.assignee_json),
            attempt_id=0, attempt_no=1, idea=IDEA)


def _install_doc_agent(monkeypatch, seen: list[str]) -> None:
    model = SimpleNamespace(model_name="mock-fast", display_name="Mock Fast",
                            context_window=32768, max_output_tokens=4096)
    monkeypatch.setattr(runners.pool, "pick_role_model", lambda role: model)

    async def fake_agent(**kwargs):
        seen.append(kwargs["user_prompt"])
        return SimpleNamespace(content=REVISED)

    monkeypatch.setattr(runners, "run_agent", fake_agent)


def _template_run() -> str:
    init_db()
    seed_defaults()
    run_id = f"revise-run-{next(_counter)}"
    with session_scope() as s:
        workflow_id = s.query(Workflow).filter(Workflow.template_key == "sdlc_v1").one().id
        s.add(Run(id=run_id, workflow_id=workflow_id, status="running", idea_text=IDEA))
        s.flush()
    instantiate_nodes(run_id, workflow_definition("sdlc_v1"))
    write_artifact(run_id, "idea", "# 想法\n\n" + IDEA, created_by="user", inputs=[], status="approved")
    return run_id


def test_doc_agent_revises_previous_version_when_feedback_is_given(monkeypatch) -> None:
    plain_run, revise_run = _template_run(), _template_run()
    for run_id in (plain_run, revise_run):
        write_artifact(run_id, "requirement", "# 需求规格\n\n旧的一版。", created_by="test", inputs=[])
    seen: list[str] = []
    _install_doc_agent(monkeypatch, seen)

    asyncio.run(runners.execute_node(_doc_node(plain_run)))                 # 不带反馈：从头写
    ctx = _doc_node(revise_run)
    ctx.feedback = "补充失败时的提示"
    result = asyncio.run(runners.execute_node(ctx))                         # 带反馈：修订

    assert "上一版" not in seen[0]
    assert "上一版" in seen[1] and "旧的一版" in seen[1] and "修订" in seen[1]
    assert parse_front_matter(read_artifact(revise_run, "requirement"))[1].strip() == REVISED.strip()
    assert result["artifact"]["version"] == 2
```

- [x] **Step 3: 写背景材料模块**

`backend/app/engine/materials.py`：

```python
"""背景材料：一组文本，放在运行目录下的 materials 子目录。

背景材料不是工件，不进版本链：想法是用户的一句话，材料是用户补充的上下文
（需求文档片段、会议记录、约束清单等）。总量有硬上限，超出即拒绝提交；
拼进会议章程时受预算约束，放不下的部分截断，并写明只提供了部分材料。
文件名形如 001-需求片段：前缀是提交的顺序，读取时按它排。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from pathlib import Path

from app.config import settings
from app.models.budget import estimate_tokens, truncate_to_tokens

_BAD_CHARS = re.compile(r'[\\/:*?"<>|\r\n]')
_TRUNCATED_NOTE = "\n\n（仅提供了部分材料：背景材料超出预算，以上只是前面的一部分）"


def _dir(run_id: str) -> Path:
    return settings.runs_dir / run_id / "materials"


def _entries(run_id: str) -> list[tuple[int, str, Path]]:
    """已有的材料：(序号, 名称, 路径)，按提交顺序。"""
    directory = _dir(run_id)
    entries: list[tuple[int, str, Path]] = []
    for path in directory.glob("*.md") if directory.exists() else []:
        seq, _, name = path.stem.partition("-")
        if seq.isdigit():
            entries.append((int(seq), name, path))
    return sorted(entries)


def _name_error(name: str) -> str | None:
    if not 1 <= len(name) <= 80:
        return "材料名称长度须是 1 到 80 个字符"
    if _BAD_CHARS.search(name):
        return f'材料名称不能包含 \\ / : * ? " < > | 或换行：{name}'
    return None


def check_materials(items: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> list[str]:
    """新建运行时的一次性检查：名称合法、总量不超限。返回错误列表。"""
    errors = [error for error in (_name_error(name) for name, _ in items) if error]
    total = sum(len(content) for _, content in items)
    if total > settings.materials_max_chars:
        errors.append(f"背景材料总量不能超过 {settings.materials_max_chars} 字，当前 {total} 字")
    return errors


def list_materials(run_id: str) -> list[dict]:
    return [{"name": name, "chars": len(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))}
            for _, name, path in _entries(run_id)]


def total_chars(run_id: str) -> int:
    return sum(item["chars"] for item in list_materials(run_id))


def save_material(run_id: str, name: str, content: str) -> dict:
    """写入一份材料，重名则覆盖。名称不合法或总量超限时抛 ValueError。"""
    error = _name_error(name)
    if error:
        raise ValueError(error)
    entries = _entries(run_id)
    target = next((path for _, item_name, path in entries if item_name == name), None)
    replaced = len(target.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if target else 0
    if total_chars(run_id) - replaced + len(content) > settings.materials_max_chars:
        raise ValueError(f"背景材料总量不能超过 {settings.materials_max_chars} 字")
    _dir(run_id).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if target is None:
        seq = max((seq for seq, _, _ in entries), default=0) + 1
        target = _dir(run_id) / f"{seq:03d}-{name}.md"
    target.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
    return {"name": name, "chars": len(content)}


def read_material(run_id: str, name: str) -> str:
    if _name_error(name):
        raise FileNotFoundError(name)
    entry = next((path for _, item_name, path in _entries(run_id) if item_name == name), None)
    if entry is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(name)
    return entry.read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def read_materials(run_id: str, budget: int) -> tuple[str, bool]:
    """把全部材料拼成一段文本，不超过 budget 个 token（估算）。返回 (文本, 是否截断)。

    截断只取决于材料和预算：同一场会议里每次调用得到的文本相同，不破坏前缀缓存。
    """
    entries = _entries(run_id)
    if not entries:
        return "", False
    joined = "\n\n".join(f"### 材料：{name}\n{path.read_text(encoding='utf-8').strip()}"
                         for _, name, path in entries)
    if estimate_tokens(joined) <= budget:
        return joined, False
    return truncate_to_tokens(joined, budget) + _TRUNCATED_NOTE, True
```

```python
class MaterialIn(BaseModel):
    name: str = Field(default="", max_length=80)      # 留空时自动编号
    content: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200_000)


class RunIn(BaseModel):

    template_key: str = "sdlc_v1"
    budget_tokens: int | None = None
    config: dict | None = None                        # 运行级配置：automation / params / rosters
    materials: list[MaterialIn] = Field(default_factory=list)
```

```python
# 运行开始前，或有确认点挂起时，可以修改运行级配置
EDITABLE_RUN_STATUSES = ("created", "waiting_human")


def _run_or_404(run_id: str) -> dict:
    with session_scope() as s:
        run = s.get(Run, run_id)
        if run is None:
            raise HTTPException(404, "run 不存在")
        return {"id": run.id, "status": run.status, "config": dict(run.config_json or {})}


def _roster_errors(config: dict) -> list[str]:
    """名单里点名的角色必须存在。"""
    wanted = [key for group in (config.get("rosters") or {}).values() if isinstance(group, dict)
              for key in (group.get("role_keys") or [])]
    if not wanted:
        return []
    with session_scope() as s:
        known = {row[0] for row in s.query(AgentTemplate.role_key)
                 .filter(AgentTemplate.role_key.in_(wanted)).all()}
    return [f"角色模板不存在：{key}" for key in wanted if key not in known]
```

4. `create_run` 整个函数换成：

```python
@router.post("/runs", status_code=201)
async def create_run(payload: RunIn) -> dict:
    """新建运行。配置与背景材料先全部校验，有任何不合法都不建运行。"""
    try:
        definition = workflow_definition(payload.template_key)
    except (ValueError, FileNotFoundError) as exc:
        raise HTTPException(400, str(exc)) from exc
    config = payload.config or {}
    materials = [(item.name.strip() or f"材料{index + 1}", item.content)
                 for index, item in enumerate(payload.materials)]
    errors = (validate_run_config(definition, config) + _roster_errors(config)
              + check_materials(materials))
    if errors:
        raise HTTPException(400, "运行级配置不合法：" + "；".join(errors))
    run_id = new_run_id()
    with session_scope() as s:
        wf = s.query(Workflow).filter(Workflow.template_key == payload.template_key).one_or_none()
        if wf is None:
            raise HTTPException(400, "工作流模板尚未初始化")
        s.add(Run(id=run_id, workflow_id=wf.id, status="created", idea_text=payload.idea,
                  budget_tokens=payload.budget_tokens or settings.default_budget_tokens,
                  config_json=config))
    instantiate_nodes(run_id, definition)
    apply_run_config(run_id, definition, config)       # 生效参数写进会议节点
    for name, content in materials:
        save_material(run_id, name, content)
    record = write_artifact(run_id, "idea", payload.idea, created_by="user",
                            inputs=[], status="approved")
    await emit(run_id, "run_created", None,
               {"run_id": run_id, "template": payload.template_key,
                "idea": payload.idea[:500]})
    await emit(run_id, "artifact_written", None, record)
    orchestrator.start(run_id)
    return get_run(run_id)
```

5. 文件末尾加这些端点：

```python
@router.get("/templates/{template_key}")
def template_detail(template_key: str) -> dict:
    """模板定义：节点、参数表（硬限、建议范围、默认值）、自动化等级预设、判据文本。"""
    try:
        return workflow_definition(template_key)
    except (ValueError, FileNotFoundError) as exc:
        raise HTTPException(404, str(exc)) from exc


@router.get("/runs/{run_id}/config")
def get_run_config(run_id: str) -> dict:
    run = _run_or_404(run_id)
    return {"run_id": run_id, "status": run["status"], "config": run["config"],
            "editable": run["status"] in EDITABLE_RUN_STATUSES}


@router.patch("/runs/{run_id}/config")
def patch_run_config(run_id: str, payload: RunConfigPatch) -> dict:
    """修改运行级配置。参数只写进还没开始的会议节点；已经开过会的节点沿用它开始时的参数。"""
    run = _run_or_404(run_id)
    if run["status"] not in EDITABLE_RUN_STATUSES:
        raise HTTPException(400, "运行级配置只能在运行开始前或有确认点挂起时修改")
    config = {**run["config"], **payload.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)}
    definition = workflow_definition(run_of(run_id)["template_key"])
    errors = validate_run_config(definition, config) + _roster_errors(config)
    if errors:
        raise HTTPException(400, "运行级配置不合法：" + "；".join(errors))
    apply_run_config(run_id, definition, config, only_pending=True)
    with session_scope() as s:
        s.get(Run, run_id).config_json = config
    reset_policy_cache(run_id)
    return {"run_id": run_id, "config": config}


@router.get("/runs/{run_id}/materials")
def list_run_materials(run_id: str) -> dict:
    _run_or_404(run_id)
    return {"items": list_materials(run_id), "total_chars": total_chars(run_id),
            "max_chars": settings.materials_max_chars}


@router.post("/runs/{run_id}/materials", status_code=201)
def add_run_material(run_id: str, payload: MaterialIn) -> dict:
    _run_or_404(run_id)
    name = payload.name.strip() or f"材料{len(list_materials(run_id)) + 1}"
    try:
        return save_material(run_id, name, payload.content)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(400, str(exc)) from exc


@router.get("/runs/{run_id}/materials/{name}")
def read_run_material(run_id: str, name: str) -> dict:
    _run_or_404(run_id)
    try:
        return {"name": name, "content": read_material(run_id, name)}
    except FileNotFoundError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(404, "材料不存在") from exc
```

文件末尾加：

```python
_ROSTER_GROUPS = ("participants", "reviewers")


def validate_rosters(rosters: dict | None) -> list[str]:
    """名单配置的结构检查：每一组要么点名具体角色，要么给固定人数，要么不写。"""
    errors: list[str] = []
    for group, item in dict(rosters or {}).items():
        if group not in _ROSTER_GROUPS:
            errors.append(f"未知的名单分组：{group}")
            continue
        item = item if isinstance(item, dict) else {}
        label = "参会者" if group == "participants" else "评审者"
        keys, count = item.get("role_keys"), item.get("count")
        if keys is not None and count is not None:
            errors.append(f"{label}只能指定具体角色或固定人数之一")
        if keys is not None and not (isinstance(keys, list) and keys
                                     and all(isinstance(key, str) and key for key in keys)):
            errors.append(f"{label}的具体角色须是非空的字符串数组")
        if count is not None and (isinstance(count, bool) or not isinstance(count, int)
                                  or not 1 <= count <= 99):
            errors.append(f"{label}的固定人数须是 1 到 99 的整数")
    return errors
```

```python
def role_library() -> list[dict]:
    """角色库：每个角色的键、名称、提示词与元数据。主持人挑名单、前端列角色都用它。"""
    with session_scope() as s:
        return [
            {"role_key": r.role_key, "name": r.name, "system_prompt": r.system_prompt,
             "meta": dict(r.meta_json or {}), "builtin": bool(r.builtin_hash),
             "default_model_id": r.default_model_id}
            for r in s.query(AgentTemplate).order_by(AgentTemplate.id.asc()).all()
        ]


def role_template(role_key: str) -> dict | None:
    """取一个角色；不存在时返回 None。"""
    with session_scope() as s:
        row = s.query(AgentTemplate).filter(AgentTemplate.role_key == role_key).first()
        if row is None:
            return None
        return {"role_key": row.role_key, "name": row.name, "system_prompt": row.system_prompt,
                "meta": dict(row.meta_json or {}), "default_model_id": row.default_model_id}
```

```python
    # ---------- 会议自身、运行级配置与背景材料 ----------
    def meeting(self) -> dict:
        return store.get_meeting(self.meeting_id)

    def meeting_round(self) -> int:
        """会议内部的轮次，例如讨论的交锋轮次。它与节点级的回流轮次 round_no() 不是一回事。"""
        return int(self.meeting()["round"] or 1)

    def reset_builder(self) -> None:
        """新一轮开始时调用。账本快照每轮更新一次，这是预期内的变化，不该报冻结段被改。"""
        self.builder = ContextBuilder()

    def run_config(self) -> dict:
        from app.engine.params import run_of

        return dict(run_of(self.run_id)["config"] or {})

    def decider(self, checkpoint: str) -> str:
        """某个确认点由谁判定：human / program / program_fallback / proxy / auto / model。"""
        from app.engine.params import decider_for, definition_of

        return decider_for(self.run_config(), definition_of(self.run_id), checkpoint)

    def materials(self) -> tuple[str, bool]:
        """背景材料（已按预算截好的一段文本）和它是否被截断。"""
        from app.config import settings
        from app.engine.materials import read_materials

        return read_materials(self.run_id, settings.materials_context_tokens)
```

`backend/app/conversation/policy.py` 末尾加：

```python
_policy_cache: dict[str, ContextPolicy] = {}


def policy_for_run(run_id: str) -> ContextPolicy:
    """运行级配置里的 context 参数 → 策略。每个运行只读一次库，之后用缓存。"""
    cached = _policy_cache.get(run_id)
    if cached is None:
        from app.engine.params import definition_of, effective_params, run_of

        config = run_of(run_id)["config"]
        cached = policy_from_config(effective_params(definition_of(run_id), config).get("context"))
        _policy_cache[run_id] = cached
    return cached


def reset_policy_cache(run_id: str) -> None:
    """运行级配置改了之后调用。"""
    _policy_cache.pop(run_id, None)
```

### Task C3: 账本规则、渲染与纪要

讨论、评审、确认三种协议共用三样由程序完成的事：按规则结算账本、把会议状态渲染成发给模型的段、把会议写成人读得懂的纪要。它们都是纯函数，不调模型。先做出来，后面的协议只管流程。对应详设 §7.3、§7.4、§7.7、§7.8。

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/app/meeting/rules.py`
- Create: `backend/app/meeting/render.py`
- Create: `backend/app/meeting/minutes.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_meeting_ledger.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces（`rules.py`）：表态等级常量 `SUPPORT`、`RESERVED`、`ABSTAIN`、`NEED_INFO`、`OPPOSE`（1–5）；`LEVEL_NAMES`；状态常量 `OPEN_STATUSES = ("open", "reopened")`、`CLOSED_STATUSES = ("agreed", "ruled")`、`DEFERRED = "deferred"`
- Produces：`point_agreed(point, rule, voters) -> bool`；`settle(points, *, rule, voters, before) -> {"open", "agreed", "changed"}`；`to_rule(points, exchanges, exchanges_per_point) -> list[str]`；`relax(points, voters) -> {"agreed", "deferred"}`；`snapshot(points) -> dict`；`rank_speakers(point, voters) -> list[int]`
- Produces（`render.py`）：`charter_segments(charter: dict) -> list[Segment]`；`ledger_text(points, *, round_no, budget=LEDGER_LIMIT) -> str`；`ledger_segment(points, *, round_no) -> Segment`；`turn_text(turn: dict) -> str`；`turn_segment(turn: dict) -> Segment`；`table_cells(points, participants) -> list[str]`
- Produces（`minutes.py`）：`minutes_markdown(*, meeting, participants, points, turns, extra_sections=()) -> str`；`write_minutes(run_id, *, kind, created_by, inputs, meeting, participants, points, turns, extra_sections=()) -> dict`（写工件，并在同目录另存一份 `discussion.ledger.json`）

账本里的一条表态是 `{"level": 1–5, "reason": str, "round": int, "name": str, "history": [...]}`，键是参会者 id。`name` 在表态时写进去，之后参会者改名或被移除，历史发言仍然读得懂。

会议章程 `charter` 是协议写进 `meetings.charter_json` 的字典：

```json
{
  "title": "讨论会议章程",
  "sections": [{"title": "想法原文", "text": "……"}, {"title": "想法简报", "text": "……"}],
  "materials": "……"
}
```

`sections` 由协议按自己的需要拼；`materials` 有内容时单独成一段，带上限。

`backend/tests/test_meeting_ledger.py`：

```python
"""账本规则、渲染与纪要：全部由程序完成，不调模型。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json

from app.artifacts.store import artifacts_dir, parse_front_matter, read_artifact
from app.conversation.builder import Segment
from app.db.base import init_db, session_scope
from app.db.models import Node, Run, Workflow
from app.engine.templates import seed_defaults
from app.meeting import minutes, render
from app.meeting.rules import (ABSTAIN, NEED_INFO, OPPOSE, RESERVED, SUPPORT, point_agreed,
                               rank_speakers, relax, settle, snapshot, to_rule)
from app.models.budget import estimate_tokens


def _stance(level: int, reason: str = "理由") -> dict:
    return {"level": level, "reason": reason, "round": 1, "name": f"参会者", "history": []}


def _point(index: str, levels: dict[int, int], status: str = "open") -> dict:
    return {"id": len(index), "index": index, "status": status, "title": f"问题{index}",
            "question": "怎么做", "options": ["甲", "乙"], "conclusion": "", "rationale": "",
            "exchange_summary": "",
            "stances": {str(pid): _stance(level) for pid, level in levels.items()}}


# ---------------- 规则 ----------------
def test_unanimous_requires_every_voter_to_support() -> None:
    voters = [1, 2, 3]
    assert point_agreed(_point("D1", {1: SUPPORT, 2: RESERVED, 3: SUPPORT}), "unanimous", voters)
    assert not point_agreed(_point("D2", {1: SUPPORT, 2: ABSTAIN, 3: SUPPORT}), "unanimous", voters)
    assert not point_agreed(_point("D3", {1: SUPPORT, 2: SUPPORT}), "unanimous", voters)   # 有人没表态


def test_no_objection_lets_abstention_through() -> None:
    voters = [1, 2, 3]
    agreed = _point("D1", {1: SUPPORT, 2: ABSTAIN, 3: RESERVED})
    blocked = _point("D2", {1: SUPPORT, 2: NEED_INFO, 3: RESERVED})
    opposed = _point("D3", {1: SUPPORT, 2: OPPOSE, 3: RESERVED})
    assert point_agreed(agreed, "no_objection", voters)
    assert not point_agreed(blocked, "no_objection", voters)
    assert not point_agreed(opposed, "no_objection", voters)


def test_settle_reports_open_points_and_stance_changes() -> None:
    voters = [1, 2]
    points = [_point("D1", {1: SUPPORT, 2: SUPPORT}), _point("D2", {1: SUPPORT, 2: OPPOSE}),
              _point("D3", {1: RESERVED, 2: NEED_INFO}),
              _point("D4", {1: SUPPORT, 2: SUPPORT}, status="deferred")]
    before = {"D2": {"1": RESERVED, "2": OPPOSE}, "D3": {"1": RESERVED, "2": NEED_INFO}}

    result = settle(points, rule="unanimous", voters=voters, before=before)
    assert result == {"open": ["D2", "D3"], "agreed": ["D1"], "changed": True}

    again = settle(points, rule="unanimous", voters=voters, before=snapshot(points))
    assert again["changed"] is False


def test_to_rule_flags_points_whose_exchanges_are_used_up() -> None:
    points = [_point("D1", {1: SUPPORT, 2: OPPOSE}), _point("D2", {1: SUPPORT, 2: SUPPORT})]
    assert to_rule(points, {"D1": 2, "D2": 1}, exchanges_per_point=2) == ["D1"]


def test_relax_closes_points_nobody_opposes_and_defers_the_rest() -> None:
    voters = [1, 2]
    points = [_point("D1", {1: RESERVED, 2: ABSTAIN}), _point("D2", {1: SUPPORT, 2: OPPOSE}),
              _point("D3", {1: SUPPORT, 2: SUPPORT}, status="agreed")]
    assert relax(points, voters) == {"agreed": ["D1"], "deferred": ["D2"]}


def test_rank_speakers_puts_supporters_first() -> None:
    point = _point("D1", {1: OPPOSE, 2: SUPPORT, 3: NEED_INFO})
    assert rank_speakers(point, [1, 2, 3, 4]) == [2, 3, 1, 4]


# ---------------- 渲染 ----------------
def test_charter_segments_keep_materials_in_their_own_capped_segment() -> None:
    charter = {"title": "讨论会议章程",
               "sections": [{"title": "想法原文", "text": "做一个工具"}],
               "materials": "### 材料：片段\n" + "字" * 20_000}
    segments = render.charter_segments(charter)
    assert [(s.kind, s.name) for s in segments] == [("frozen", "章程"), ("frozen", "背景材料")]
    assert "## 讨论会议章程" in segments[0].text and "做一个工具" in segments[0].text
    assert estimate_tokens(segments[1].text) <= render.MATERIALS_LIMIT + 40
    assert render.charter_segments(charter)[1].text == segments[1].text     # 同样的输入，同样的文本


def test_ledger_degrades_in_steps_when_over_budget() -> None:
    closed = _point("D1", {1: SUPPORT, 2: SUPPORT}, status="agreed")
    closed.update(conclusion="选甲", rationale="很长" * 2000, exchange_summary="很长" * 2000)
    open_point = _point("D2", {1: OPPOSE, 2: SUPPORT})
    full = render.ledger_text([closed, open_point], round_no=3)
    slim = render.ledger_text([closed, open_point], round_no=3, budget=600)
    assert "理由：很长" in full                       # 预算够：已了结决策点带理由
    assert "### 各决策点的交锋摘要" in full and "- D1：很长" in full
    assert "理由：很长" not in slim and "交锋摘要" not in slim   # 预算不够：先降级这两样
    assert estimate_tokens(slim) <= 600 + 40
    assert "| D1 | 已一致 | 问题D1 | 甲 / 乙 | 选甲 |" in slim
    assert "| D2 | 讨论中 | 问题D2 | 甲 / 乙 | — |" in slim
    assert "反对" in slim                                        # 进行中决策点的表态仍在


def test_turn_segment_renders_a_stance_in_words() -> None:
    turn = {"seq": 4, "kind": "restance", "speaker_name": "架构师", "content": '{"stances": [...]}',
            "decision_point": None, "structured": {"stances": [
                {"index": "D1", "level": 5, "reason": "成本太高"},
                {"index": "D2", "level": 2, "reason": ""}]}}
    segment = render.turn_segment(turn)
    assert segment.kind == "append" and segment.name == "发言4"
    assert segment.text == ("### 架构师（重新表态）\n"
                            "D1：反对——成本太高\nD2：有保留支持")


def test_table_cells_show_levels_for_every_voter() -> None:
    points = [_point("D1", {1: SUPPORT, 2: OPPOSE})]
    voters = [{"id": 1, "display_name": "架构"}, {"id": 2, "display_name": "体验"},
              {"id": 3, "display_name": "测试"}]
    assert render.table_cells(points, voters) == [
        "| 编号 | 架构 | 体验 | 测试 |", "|---|---|---|---|", "| D1 | 支持 | 反对 | — |"]


# ---------------- 纪要 ----------------
def _run() -> str:
    init_db()
    seed_defaults()
    with session_scope() as s:
        workflow_id = s.query(Workflow).filter(Workflow.template_key == "sdlc_v1").one().id
        s.add(Run(id="minutes-run", workflow_id=workflow_id, status="running", idea_text="做一个工具"))
        s.flush()
        s.add(Node(node_key="discuss", type="MEETING", name="讨论会议", run_id="minutes-run",
                   status="running"))
    return "minutes-run"


def _fixture() -> dict:
    agreed = _point("D1", {1: SUPPORT, 2: SUPPORT}, status="agreed")
    agreed.update(conclusion="选甲", rationale="成本低", exchange_summary="体验担心工期，架构给出分批方案")
    deferred = _point("D2", {1: SUPPORT, 2: OPPOSE}, status="deferred")
    deferred.update(title="是否要登录", conclusion="待用户决定")
    return {
        "meeting": {"id": 1, "protocol": "discussion", "round": 2, "charter": {"title": "讨论会议章程",
                   "sections": [{"title": "想法原文", "text": "做一个反馈收集工具"}]}},
        "participants": [{"id": 1, "display_name": "架构", "mode": "participant"},
                         {"id": 2, "display_name": "体验", "mode": "participant"}],
        "points": [agreed, deferred],
        "turns": [{"seq": 1, "kind": "position", "speaker_name": "架构", "content": "必须可验证。",
                   "structured": None, "decision_point": None}],
    }


def test_minutes_lists_info_ledger_matrix_and_pending_points() -> None:
    body = minutes.minutes_markdown(**_fixture())
    for expected in ("## 会议信息", "想法原文：做一个反馈收集工具", "## 决策点账本",
                     "| D1 | 已一致 | 问题D1 | 甲 / 乙 | 选甲 |", "## 表态矩阵",
                     "| 编号 | 架构 | 体验 |", "| D1 | 支持 | 支持 |",
                     "## 各角色立场摘要", "必须可验证。", "## 各决策点的交锋记录摘要",
                     "体验担心工期", "## 未决议题", "D2 是否要登录：待用户决定"):

def test_write_minutes_writes_artifact_and_ledger_json() -> None:
```
