- [x] **Step 3: 写账本规则**

`backend/app/meeting/rules.py`：

```python
"""决策点账本的规则：表态等级、闭合规则、停滞判定。

全部是纯函数，由程序计算，不调模型（详设 §7.3、§7.4）。
一个决策点是存取层返回的字典，其中 stances 形如
{"<参会者 id>": {"level": 1–5, "reason": str, "round": int, "name": str, "history": [...]}}。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

SUPPORT, RESERVED, ABSTAIN, NEED_INFO, OPPOSE = 1, 2, 3, 4, 5
LEVEL_NAMES = {SUPPORT: "支持", RESERVED: "有保留支持", ABSTAIN: "弃权",
               NEED_INFO: "需更多信息", OPPOSE: "反对"}

OPEN_STATUSES = ("open", "reopened")       # 讨论中
CLOSED_STATUSES = ("agreed", "ruled")      # 了结
DEFERRED = "deferred"                      # 待定：带进方案的"待确认问题"，不丢


def level_of(point: dict, voter) -> int | None:
    return ((point.get("stances") or {}).get(str(voter)) or {}).get("level")


def point_agreed(point: dict, rule: str, voters: list[int]) -> bool:
    """按闭合规则，这个决策点在当前表态下算不算达成一致。"""
    levels = [level_of(point, voter) for voter in voters]
    if not voters or any(level is None for level in levels):
        return False                                  # 还有人没表态
    if rule == "no_objection":
        return all(level not in (NEED_INFO, OPPOSE) for level in levels)
    return all(level in (SUPPORT, RESERVED) for level in levels)


def settle(points: list[dict], *, rule: str, voters: list[int],
           before: dict | None) -> dict:
    """一轮全员表态之后结算一次。只计算，不改账本；落盘由协议做。

    before 是这一轮开始时的表态快照（snapshot() 的返回值）。
    返回 open（仍未了结的编号）、agreed（这一轮达成一致的编号）、
    changed（有没有人改动未了结决策点上的表态等级——停滞判定看它）。
    """
    working = [point for point in points if point["status"] in OPEN_STATUSES]
    agreed = [point["index"] for point in working if point_agreed(point, rule, voters)]
    changed = False
    for point in working:
        for voter, level in ((before or {}).get(point["index"]) or {}).items():
            if level_of(point, voter) != level:
                changed = True
    return {"open": [point["index"] for point in working if point["index"] not in agreed],
            "agreed": agreed, "changed": changed}


def to_rule(points: list[dict], exchanges: dict[str, int], exchanges_per_point: int) -> list[str]:
    """闭合规则是"主持人裁决"时：交锋次数已满、仍未一致的决策点要交给主持人裁决。"""
    return [point["index"] for point in points
            if point["status"] in OPEN_STATUSES
            and exchanges.get(point["index"], 0) >= exchanges_per_point]


def relax(points: list[dict], voters: list[int]) -> dict:
    """全自动模式下的退让：先按"无反对即可"了结，仍不行的记为待定。只计算，不落盘。"""
    agreed: list[str] = []
    deferred: list[str] = []
    for point in points:
        if point["status"] not in OPEN_STATUSES:
            continue
        levels = [level_of(point, voter) for voter in voters]
        if voters and all(level not in (NEED_INFO, OPPOSE) for level in levels):
            agreed.append(point["index"])
        else:
            deferred.append(point["index"])
    return {"agreed": agreed, "deferred": deferred}


def snapshot(points: list[dict]) -> dict:
    """未了结决策点当前的表态等级 {编号: {参会者 id: 等级}}，供下一轮判断有没有人改立场。"""
    return {point["index"]: {pid: (stance or {}).get("level")
                             for pid, stance in (point.get("stances") or {}).items()}
            for point in points if point["status"] in OPEN_STATUSES}


def rank_speakers(point: dict, voters: list[int]) -> list[int]:
    """把参会者按立场从最支持排到最反对；没表态的排在最后。"""
    return sorted(voters, key=lambda voter: (level_of(point, voter) is None,
                                             level_of(point, voter) or 0))
```

- [x] **Step 4: 写渲染**

`backend/app/meeting/render.py`：

```python
"""把会议状态渲染成文本：发给模型的段，以及给人看的表格。

两件事都只由程序做，模型不负责复述账本。发给模型的部分注意两点（详设 §10）：
要截断的内容在构造段的时候用 capped() 一次截好，同样的输入永远得到同样的文本；
措辞固定，不随调用变化。这样冻结段在两次调用之间逐字节不变，前缀缓存才命中。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from app.conversation.builder import Segment, capped
from app.meeting.rules import CLOSED_STATUSES, LEVEL_NAMES, OPEN_STATUSES
from app.models.budget import estimate_tokens

MATERIALS_LIMIT = 6000       # 背景材料段的 token 上限
CHARTER_LIMIT = 8000         # 章程正文段的 token 上限
LEDGER_LIMIT = 4000          # 账本快照段的 token 上限

STATUS_NAMES = {"open": "讨论中", "reopened": "复议中", "agreed": "已一致",
                "ruled": "主持人裁决", "deferred": "待定"}
TURN_LABELS = {"position": "立场", "stance": "表态", "restance": "重新表态", "claim": "主张",
               "challenge": "质疑", "respond": "回应", "exchange_summary": "交锋摘要",
               "question": "提问", "answer": "回答", "facilitator_note": "主持人",
               "user_intervention": "用户插话", "ruling": "裁决"}


def _cell(value) -> str:
    """表格单元：竖线会破坏 Markdown 表格，换成斜线；换行换成空格。"""
    text = "" if value is None else str(value)
    return (text.replace("|", "/").replace("\n", " ").strip()) or "—"


def charter_segments(charter: dict) -> list[Segment]:
    """会议章程：正文一段，背景材料另有上限的一段。都是冻结段，名字固定。"""
    parts = [f"## {charter.get('title') or '会议章程'}"]
    for section in charter.get("sections") or []:
        parts.append(f"### {section['title']}\n{section.get('text') or ''}".rstrip())
    segments = [Segment("frozen", capped("\n\n".join(parts) + "\n", CHARTER_LIMIT),
                        name="章程", limit=CHARTER_LIMIT)]
    materials = str(charter.get("materials") or "")
    if materials:
        segments.append(Segment("frozen", capped(materials, MATERIALS_LIMIT),
                                name="背景材料", limit=MATERIALS_LIMIT))
    return segments


def ledger_text(points: list[dict], *, round_no: int, budget: int = LEDGER_LIMIT) -> str:
    """账本快照（本轮开始时的状态）。超预算时分两级降级：
    先去掉已了结决策点的理由和交锋摘要，再把进行中决策点的表态压成只剩等级。"""
    full = _ledger(points, round_no, reasons=True)
    if estimate_tokens(full) <= budget:
        return full
    slimmer = _ledger(points, round_no, reasons=False)
    if estimate_tokens(slimmer) <= budget:
        return slimmer
    return capped(_ledger(points, round_no, reasons=False, levels_only=True), budget)


def ledger_segment(points: list[dict], *, round_no: int) -> Segment:
    return Segment("frozen", ledger_text(points, round_no=round_no), name="账本",
                   limit=LEDGER_LIMIT)


def _ledger(points: list[dict], round_no: int, *, reasons: bool,
            levels_only: bool = False) -> str:
    lines = [f"## 决策点账本（第 {round_no} 轮开始时的状态）"]
    if not points:
        return "\n".join(lines + ["", "（还没有决策点）"])
    lines += ["", "| 编号 | 状态 | 问题 | 候选项 | 结论 |", "|---|---|---|---|---|"]
    for point in points:
        options = " / ".join(str(option) for option in (point.get("options") or []))
        status = STATUS_NAMES.get(point["status"], point["status"])
        lines.append(f"| {point['index']} | {status} | {_cell(point.get('title'))} | "
                     f"{_cell(options)} | {_cell(point.get('conclusion'))} |")
    working = [point for point in points if point["status"] in OPEN_STATUSES]
    if working:
        lines += ["", "### 讨论中的决策点：各方最新的表态"]
        for point in working:
            lines += ["", f"**{point['index']} {point.get('title') or ''}**".rstrip()]
            stances = sorted((point.get("stances") or {}).items(), key=lambda item: int(item[0]))
            for pid, stance in stances:
                level = LEVEL_NAMES.get((stance or {}).get("level"), "未表态")
                reason = (stance or {}).get("reason")
                if levels_only or not reason:
                    lines.append(f"- {(stance or {}).get('name') or f'参会者{pid}'}：{level}")
                else:
                    lines.append(f"- {(stance or {}).get('name') or f'参会者{pid}'}：{level}——{reason}")
    if reasons:
        exchanged = [point for point in points if point.get("exchange_summary")]
        if exchanged:
            lines += ["", "### 各决策点的交锋摘要"]
            lines += [f"- {point['index']}：{point['exchange_summary']}" for point in exchanged]
    closed = [point for point in points if point["status"] in CLOSED_STATUSES]
    if closed:
        lines += ["", "### 已了结的决策点：结论与理由"]
        for point in closed:
            line = f"- {point['index']} {point.get('title') or ''}：{point.get('conclusion') or '—'}"
            if reasons and point.get("rationale"):
                line += f"（理由：{point['rationale']}）"
            lines.append(line)
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"


def turn_text(turn: dict) -> str:
    """一条发言的正文。结构化的发言（表态、交锋摘要）渲染成几行文字，不直接给 JSON。"""
    kind = turn.get("kind") or ""
    data = turn.get("structured") or {}
    if kind in ("stance", "restance") and data.get("stances"):
        lines = []
        for item in data["stances"]:
            reason = f"——{item['reason']}" if item.get("reason") else ""
            lines.append(f"{item.get('index')}：{LEVEL_NAMES.get(item.get('level'), '未表态')}{reason}")
        return "\n".join(lines)
    if kind == "exchange_summary" and data.get("summaries"):
        return "\n".join(f"{item.get('index')}：{item.get('summary')}" for item in data["summaries"])
    return str(turn.get("content") or "")


def turn_segment(turn: dict) -> Segment:
    """本轮的一条逐字发言，作为追加段。"""
    label = TURN_LABELS.get(turn.get("kind") or "", turn.get("kind") or "发言")
    about = f"，关于 {turn['decision_point']}" if turn.get("decision_point") else ""
    text = f"### {turn.get('speaker_name') or '？'}（{label}{about}）\n{turn_text(turn)}".rstrip()
    return Segment("append", text, name=f"发言{turn['seq']}")


def table_cells(points: list[dict], participants: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
    """表态矩阵（Markdown 表格的各行）：每个有表决权的角色一列。"""
    names = [participant["display_name"] for participant in participants]
    lines = ["| 编号 | " + " | ".join(names) + " |", "|" + "---|" * (len(names) + 1)]
    for point in points:
        stances = point.get("stances") or {}
        cells = []
        for participant in participants:
            stance = stances.get(str(participant["id"])) or {}
            cells.append(LEVEL_NAMES.get(stance.get("level"), "—") if stance else "—")
        lines.append(f"| {point['index']} | " + " | ".join(cells) + " |")
    return lines
```

- [x] **Step 5: 写纪要**

`backend/app/meeting/minutes.py`：

```python
"""会议纪要：由程序从账本和发言生成，不经过模型（详设 §7.8）。

写进工件（discussion 或 review）的同时，在同目录另存一份账本的 JSON，
供之后的节点和外部工具读取。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json

from app.artifacts.store import artifacts_dir, write_artifact
from app.meeting import render
from app.meeting.rules import DEFERRED, LEVEL_NAMES

PROTOCOL_NAMES = {"discussion": "讨论会议", "review": "评审会议", "confirm": "需求确认会议"}
LEDGER_FIELDS = ("id", "index", "title", "question", "options", "status", "conclusion",
                 "rationale", "exchange_summary", "stances", "origin_round", "closed_round")


def _one_line(text: str, limit: int) -> str:
    line = " ".join(str(text or "").split())
    return line if len(line) <= limit else line[:limit] + "…"


def minutes_markdown(*, meeting: dict, participants: list[dict], points: list[dict],
                     turns: list[dict], extra_sections: list[tuple[str, str]] = ()) -> str:
    """会议纪要正文：会议信息、账本、表态矩阵、立场摘要、交锋摘要、未决议题，再加协议自己要附的几节。"""
    charter = dict(meeting.get("charter") or {})
    protocol = meeting.get("protocol") or ""
    voters = [p for p in participants if p.get("mode") == "participant"]
    lines = [f"# 会议纪要（{PROTOCOL_NAMES.get(protocol, protocol)}）", "", "## 会议信息"]
    for section in charter.get("sections") or []:
        lines.append(f"- {section['title']}：{_one_line(section.get('text'), 300)}")
    lines.append("- 参会者：" + ("、".join(p["display_name"] for p in participants) or "（无）"))
    lines.append(f"- 轮次：{meeting.get('round') or 1}")
    lines += ["", "## 决策点账本", render.ledger_text(points, round_no=meeting.get("round") or 1,
                                                       budget=10 ** 9).rstrip()]
    if voters:
        lines += ["", "## 表态矩阵"] + render.table_cells(points, voters)
    positions = [t for t in turns if t.get("kind") == "position" and t.get("content")]
    if positions:
        lines += ["", "## 各角色立场摘要"]
        for turn in positions:
            lines += [f"### {turn['speaker_name']}", "", str(turn["content"]).strip(), ""]
    exchanged = [p for p in points if p.get("exchange_summary")]
    if exchanged:
        lines += ["", "## 各决策点的交锋记录摘要"]
        lines += [f"- {p['index']} {p.get('title') or ''}：{p['exchange_summary']}" for p in exchanged]
    pending = [p for p in points if p["status"] == DEFERRED]
    if pending:
        lines += ["", "## 未决议题（需要用户决定）"]
        lines += [f"- {p['index']} {p.get('title') or ''}：{p.get('conclusion') or '各方理由见账本'}"
                  for p in pending]
    for title, text in extra_sections:
        lines += ["", f"## {title}", str(text).rstrip()]
    return "\n".join(lines).strip() + "\n"


def write_minutes(run_id: str, *, kind: str, created_by: str, inputs: list[str],
                  meeting: dict, participants: list[dict], points: list[dict],
                  turns: list[dict], extra_sections: list[tuple[str, str]] = ()) -> dict:
    """写会议纪要工件（新版本），并另存账本的 JSON。返回工件的记录。"""
    body = minutes_markdown(meeting=meeting, participants=participants, points=points,
                            turns=turns, extra_sections=extra_sections)
    record = write_artifact(run_id, kind, body, created_by=created_by, inputs=inputs,
                            status="reviewed")
    ledger = [{field: point.get(field) for field in LEDGER_FIELDS} for point in points]
    path = artifacts_dir(run_id) / f"{kind}.ledger.json"
    path.write_text(json.dumps(ledger, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    return record
```

### Task C4: 讨论协议

把详设 §7 的讨论流程落到 `protocols/discussion.py`：会前准备、澄清、名单、独立立场、议题提炼、全员表态、交锋轮次、共识确认点、整合成稿、全员复核，以及带着反馈续开时的复议。谁在何时发言、何时算了结、何时停，全部由程序判定。对应详设 §7、§11.2。

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/app/meeting/protocols/discussion.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/meeting/store.py`（按轮取发言；写表态）
- Modify: `backend/app/meeting/runner.py`（协议模块惰性导入；带着反馈续开时作废旧确认点）
- Modify: `backend/app/engine/gates.py`（方案确认门的描述改为读账本里待定的决策点）
- Modify: `backend/tests/meeting_fakes.py`（加 `drive`）
- Test: `backend/tests/test_discussion_protocol.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `speak`、`speak_many`（任务 B6）；`open_checkpoint`、`record_auto_decision`（任务 B3）；`rules`、`render`、`minutes`（任务 C3）；`MeetingSession.decider`、`run_config`、`materials`（任务 C2）
- Produces: 协议名 `discussion`，步骤 `prepare`、`clarify`、`roster`、`positions`、`agenda`、`stances`、`exchange`、`summary`、`restance`、`consensus`、`reopen`、`draft`、`check`
- Produces: 会议内的确认点类型 `clarify`、`roster`、`consensus`；决定的 `data`：澄清是 `{"answers": ["第一条的回答", ...]}`，共识是 `{"option": "integrate" | "continue" | "delegate"}`
- Produces: `store.round_turns(meeting_id, *, round, kinds=None) -> list[dict]`；`store.set_stance(point_id, participant_id, stance: dict) -> None`
- Produces: `runner.PROTOCOL_MODULES = ("discussion", "review", "confirm")`；`get_protocol` 按名字惰性导入协议模块
- Produces（测试替身）：`drive(run_id, node_id, answer, *, idea="") -> dict`——反复执行会议节点并自动回答人工确认点，直到会议完成或发起回流；`answer(确认点的门载荷) -> (action, data, feedback)`
- Produces: 会议完成时节点结果里带 `artifact`（建设方案）、`minutes`（会议纪要）、`decision_points`、`deferred`、`rounds`
- Produces: 方案确认门（`gate_proposal`）的描述里列出账本中待定的决策点，取自最近一场讨论会议；标题是 `待确认问题（需要你决定）`

讨论协议用到的调用标识（测试和 Mock 供应商按它准备回复）：

| 步骤 | 调用标识 | 发言类型 | 输出 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 会前准备 | `brief` | `brief` | JSON：简报、澄清问题 |
| 澄清（需求方代理） | `answer` | `answer` | JSON：逐条回答与出处 |
| 名单 | `roster` | `roster_proposal` | JSON：提议的参会者 |
| 独立立场 | `position` | `position` | JSON：必须保证的事项、风险、路线、疑问 |
| 议题提炼 | `agenda` | `agenda` | JSON：决策点清单 |
| 表态、重新表态 | `stance`、`restance` | `stance`、`restance` | JSON：每个决策点的等级、理由、选择 |
| 交锋 | `claim`、`challenge`、`respond` | 同名 | 一段话 |
| 交锋摘要 | `exchange_summary` | `exchange_summary` | JSON：逐个决策点的摘要 |
| 主持人裁决 | `ruling` | `ruling` | JSON：结论、理由、保留的异议 |
| 复议 | `reopen` | `reopen_plan` | JSON：要重开或新建的决策点 |
| 成稿、修订 | `proposal`、`proposal_revise` | `proposal`、`proposal_revise` | Markdown |
| 全员复核 | `proposal_check` | `proposal_check` | JSON：是否如实反映、问题清单 |

- [x] **Step 1: 写失败的测试**

`backend/tests/meeting_fakes.py` 末尾加 `drive`（导入区加 `import asyncio`）：

```python
def drive(run_id: str, node_id: int, answer, *, idea: str = "") -> dict:
    """反复执行会议节点，自动回答路上遇到的人工确认点，直到会议完成或发起回流。

    answer(确认点的门载荷) -> (action, data, feedback)。每次执行都按节点当前的状态新建上下文，
    与编排器恢复节点时的做法一致。
    """
    from app.db.base import session_scope
    from app.db.models import Node
    from app.engine.gates import decide_gate
    from app.engine.runners import NodeContext
    from app.meeting.runner import run_meeting

    def context() -> NodeContext:
        with session_scope() as s:
            node = s.get(Node, node_id)
            return NodeContext(
                run_id=run_id, node_id=node_id, node_key=node.node_key, type=node.type,
                name=node.name, phase=node.phase, config=dict(node.config_json or {}),
                assignee=dict(node.assignee_json or {}), attempt_id=0, attempt_no=1,
                feedback=(node.config_json or {}).get("feedback"), idea=idea)

    while True:
        result = asyncio.run(run_meeting(context()))
        if result.get("done") or result.get("reroute"):
            return result
        action, data, feedback = answer(result["checkpoint"])
        decide_gate(gate_id=result["checkpoint"]["id"], action=action,
                    feedback=feedback, data=data)
```

```python
    "# 建设方案\n\n## 目标与成功标准\n\n两周内让小团队用上反馈收集：能收集、能整理、能导出。\n\n"
    "## 范围\n\n必做：收集、整理、导出周报。不做：实时协作、细粒度权限。\n\n"
    "## 总体方案\n\nD1：首版做网页版，部署最简单。D2：首版是否登录尚有分歧。\n\n"
    "## 关键流程\n\n提交反馈 → 整理 → 导出。\n\n## 里程碑与验收\n\n第一周：收集与导出可用。\n\n"
    "## 风险与缓解\n\n范围蔓延：首版功能冻结。\n")
PROPOSAL_V2 = "## 修改说明\n\n- D2：按复议结论改为首版需要登录。\n\n" + PROPOSAL


def _split(text: str) -> str:
    return ARCHITECT if "架构视角" in text else UX


def _agree(text: str) -> str:
    return ARCHITECT_YIELDS if "架构视角" in text else UX


def _by_round(*replies):
    """按轮次给出回复；用完以后一直用最后一个。"""
    queue = list(replies)

    def reply(text: str) -> str:
        current = queue.pop(0) if len(queue) > 1 else queue[0]
        return current(text)

    return reply


def _first_then(first: str, rest: str):
    calls: list[int] = []

    def reply(text: str) -> str:
        calls.append(1)
        return first if len(calls) == 1 else rest

    return reply


def _replies(**overrides) -> dict:
    """一套完整的脚本回复；按需覆盖其中几项。"""
    base = {
        "brief": BRIEF, "answer": ANSWER, "roster": ROSTER, "position": POSITION, "agenda": AGENDA,
        "stance": _split,
        "claim": "我支持首版不做登录：首版只有本机使用，登录是多余的门槛。",
        "challenge": "多人共用时无法区分反馈是谁提交的，后续跟进会乱。",
        "respond": "多人场景确实需要区分提交人，我改变立场：首版做最简登录。",
        "exchange_summary": SUMMARY, "restance": _by_round(_split, _agree), "ruling": RULING,
        "proposal": _first_then(PROPOSAL, PROPOSAL_V2), "proposal_check": CHECK_OK,
        "proposal_revise": PROPOSAL_V2, "reopen": REOPEN,
    }
    base.update(overrides)
    return base


def _meeting_run(config: dict | None = None) -> tuple[str, int]:
    """按模板建一个运行，返回 (运行 id, 讨论会议节点的 id)。生效参数已写进节点。"""
    init_db()
    seed_defaults()
    run_id = f"discuss-run-{next(_counter)}"
    with session_scope() as s:
        workflow_id = s.query(Workflow).filter(Workflow.template_key == "sdlc_v1").one().id
        s.add(Run(id=run_id, workflow_id=workflow_id, status="running", idea_text=IDEA,
                  config_json=config or {}))
        s.flush()
    definition = workflow_definition("sdlc_v1")
    instantiate_nodes(run_id, definition)
    apply_run_config(run_id, definition, config or {})
    write_artifact(run_id, "idea", "# 想法\n\n" + IDEA, created_by="user", inputs=[], status="approved")
    with session_scope() as s:
        return run_id, s.query(Node).filter(Node.run_id == run_id, Node.node_key == "discuss").one().id


def _approve(checkpoint: dict) -> tuple:
    return "approve", {}, ""


def _never(checkpoint: dict) -> tuple:
    raise AssertionError(f"全自动模式下不该出现人工确认点：{checkpoint['title']}")


def _ledger(node_id: int) -> dict[str, dict]:
    meeting_id = store.latest_meeting_for_node(node_id)["id"]
    return {point["index"]: point for point in store.list_decision_points(meeting_id)}


def _charter(node_id: int) -> dict[str, str]:
    charter = store.latest_meeting_for_node(node_id)["charter"]
    return {section["title"]: section["text"] for section in charter["sections"]}


def _proposal(run_id: str) -> tuple[dict, str]:
    meta, body = parse_front_matter(read_artifact(run_id, "proposal"))
    return meta, body


def test_meeting_reaches_a_proposal_with_two_human_checkpoints(monkeypatch) -> None:
    """默认等级：澄清和名单由人确认；共识由程序按闭合规则判定，不打扰人。"""
    seen: list[dict] = []
    install(monkeypatch, _replies(), seen)
    run_id, node_id = _meeting_run()
    asked: list[str] = []

    def answer(checkpoint: dict) -> tuple:
        asked.append(checkpoint["kind"])
        data = {"answers": ["Markdown 即可"]} if checkpoint["kind"] == "clarify" else {}
        return "approve", data, ""

    result = drive(run_id, node_id, answer, idea=IDEA)

    assert result["done"] is True
    assert (result["decision_points"], result["deferred"], result["rounds"]) == (2, 0, 2)
    assert asked == ["clarify", "roster"]
    ledger = _ledger(node_id)
    assert {index: point["status"] for index, point in ledger.items()} == {"D1": "agreed", "D2": "agreed"}
    assert ledger["D1"]["conclusion"] == "网页版" and ledger["D2"]["conclusion"] == "需要"
    assert ledger["D2"]["raised_by"] == "用户体验"
    architect = next(s for s in ledger["D2"]["stances"].values() if s["name"] == "架构师")
    assert architect["level"] == 1 and [item["level"] for item in architect["history"]] == [5]
    assert "用户回答" in _charter(node_id)["已澄清的问题"]
    assert "Markdown 即可" in _charter(node_id)["已澄清的问题"]
    meta, body = _proposal(run_id)
    assert meta["version"] == 1 and "## 待确认问题" in body and "## 决策点索引" in body
    assert "## 决策点账本" in parse_front_matter(read_artifact(run_id, "discussion"))[1]
    kinds = Counter(call["kind"] for call in seen)
    assert kinds["position"] == 2 and kinds["claim"] == 4 and kinds["exchange_summary"] == 2
    types = [event["type"] for event in load_events(run_id)]
    assert "meeting_ledger_updated" in types and "meeting_turn_finished" in types


def test_full_automation_falls_back_at_a_stall_without_asking_anyone(monkeypatch) -> None:
    """仅兜底的等级：需求方代理答澄清，名单自动生效，停滞后先按“无反对即可”了结，其余待定。"""
    install(monkeypatch, _replies(restance=_split))
    run_id, node_id = _meeting_run({"automation": {"level": "L3"}})

    result = drive(run_id, node_id, _never, idea=IDEA)

    assert result["done"] is True and result["deferred"] == 1 and result["rounds"] == 2
    ledger = _ledger(node_id)
    assert ledger["D1"]["status"] == "agreed" and ledger["D2"]["status"] == "deferred"
    _meta, body = _proposal(run_id)
    assert "## 待确认问题" in body and "D2 首版是否需要登录" in body
    answered = _charter(node_id)["已澄清的问题"]
    assert "需要导出周报" in answered and "按假设处理" in answered      # 没有出处的回答降级为假设
    with session_scope() as s:
        gates = {(g.kind, g.decided_by) for g in s.query(Gate).filter(Gate.run_id == run_id).all()}
    assert gates == {("clarify", "proxy"), ("roster", "program"), ("consensus", "program")}


def test_human_judges_consensus_each_round_and_can_send_it_back(monkeypatch) -> None:
    """全程人审：每轮结束都暂停由人判；人可以给指导语让它再讨论一轮。"""
    install(monkeypatch, _replies(restance=_split))
    run_id, node_id = _meeting_run({"automation": {"level": "L1"}})
    asked: list[dict] = []

    def answer(checkpoint: dict) -> tuple:
        asked.append(checkpoint)
        if checkpoint["kind"] != "consensus":
            return "approve", {}, ""
        if len(asked) == 3:                                    # 第一次到共识确认点
            return "reject", {"option": "continue"}, "请重点讨论多人共用的场景"
        return "approve", {"option": "integrate"}, ""

    result = drive(run_id, node_id, answer, idea=IDEA)

    assert [item["kind"] for item in asked] == ["clarify", "roster", "consensus", "consensus"]
    assert asked[2]["payload"]["reason"] == "round_end"
    assert [item["index"] for item in asked[2]["payload"]["open"]] == ["D2"]
    assert "带着分歧整合" in asked[2]["description"]
    assert result["done"] is True and result["deferred"] == 1 and result["rounds"] == 2
    meeting_id = store.latest_meeting_for_node(node_id)["id"]
    guidance = [t for t in store.list_turns(meeting_id) if t["kind"] == "user_guidance"]
    assert [t["content"] for t in guidance] == ["请重点讨论多人共用的场景"]


def test_delegating_lets_the_facilitator_rule_and_keeps_dissent(monkeypatch) -> None:
    install(monkeypatch, _replies(restance=_split))
    run_id, node_id = _meeting_run({"automation": {"level": "L1"}})

    def answer(checkpoint: dict) -> tuple:
        data = {"option": "delegate"} if checkpoint["kind"] == "consensus" else {}
        return "approve", data, ""

    drive(run_id, node_id, answer, idea=IDEA)

    point = _ledger(node_id)["D2"]
    assert point["status"] == "ruled" and point["conclusion"] == "需要"
    assert "多人共用是核心场景" in point["rationale"] and "保留的异议：架构师" in point["rationale"]


def test_facilitator_rules_closure_rules_once_exchanges_are_used_up(monkeypatch) -> None:
    seen: list[dict] = []
    install(monkeypatch, _replies(restance=_split), seen)
    run_id, node_id = _meeting_run({"params": {"discussion": {"closure_rule": "facilitator_rules"}}})

    result = drive(run_id, node_id, _approve, idea=IDEA)

    assert result["done"] is True and _ledger(node_id)["D2"]["status"] == "ruled"
    assert [call["kind"] for call in seen].count("ruling") == 1
    assert "主持人裁决" in _charter(node_id)["闭合规则"]


def test_a_single_participant_skips_stances_and_debate(monkeypatch) -> None:
    seen: list[dict] = []
    install(monkeypatch, _replies(roster=ROSTER_ONE), seen)
    run_id, node_id = _meeting_run({"rosters": {"participants": {"count": 1}}})

    result = drive(run_id, node_id, _approve, idea=IDEA)

    kinds = Counter(call["kind"] for call in seen)
    assert result["done"] is True and result["rounds"] == 1
    assert kinds["position"] == 1 and "stance" not in kinds and "claim" not in kinds
    assert {point["status"] for point in _ledger(node_id).values()} == {"agreed"}


def test_a_fixed_headcount_is_enforced(monkeypatch) -> None:
    install(monkeypatch, _replies())                         # 主持人提了 2 名，用户要的是 3 名
    run_id, node_id = _meeting_run({"rosters": {"participants": {"count": 3}}})
    with pytest.raises(NodeFailure, match="固定人数"):
        drive(run_id, node_id, _approve, idea=IDEA)


def test_feedback_reopens_points_and_produces_a_new_version(monkeypatch) -> None:
    seen: list[dict] = []
    install(monkeypatch, _replies(restance=_by_round(_agree, _agree)), seen)
    run_id, node_id = _meeting_run()
    drive(run_id, node_id, _approve, idea=IDEA)
    first = store.latest_meeting_for_node(node_id)
    with session_scope() as s:
        node = s.get(Node, node_id)
        node.config_json = with_feedback(node.config_json, "评审认为登录方案需要复议", "reroute")

    result = drive(run_id, node_id, _approve, idea=IDEA)

    assert result["done"] is True and store.latest_meeting_for_node(node_id)["id"] == first["id"]
    ledger = _ledger(node_id)
    assert ledger["D2"]["status"] == "agreed" and ledger["D2"]["closed_round"] == 2
    assert ledger["D3"]["raised_by"] == "评审意见"            # 反馈带出了账本里没有的问题
    meta, body = _proposal(run_id)
    assert meta["version"] == 2 and "## 修改说明" in body
    prompts = [call["text"] for call in seen if call["kind"] == "proposal"]
    assert len(prompts) == 2 and "上一版建设方案" in prompts[1]
    notes = [t for t in store.list_turns(first["id"]) if t["kind"] == "user_feedback"]
    assert [t["content"] for t in notes] == ["评审认为登录方案需要复议"]


def test_proposal_gate_lists_pending_points_from_the_ledger(monkeypatch) -> None:
    """待确认的问题由账本给出：方案确认门的卡片上直接看得到。"""
    install(monkeypatch, _replies(restance=_split))
    run_id, node_id = _meeting_run({"automation": {"level": "L3"}})
    drive(run_id, node_id, _never, idea=IDEA)
    with session_scope() as s:
        gate_node = s.query(Node).filter(Node.run_id == run_id,
                                         Node.node_key == "gate_proposal").one().id

    gate = request_gate(run_id, gate_node)

    assert "待确认问题（需要你决定）" in gate["description"]
    assert "D2 首版是否需要登录" in gate["description"]
    assert get_gate(gate["id"])["description"] == gate["description"]     # 冷读不重复追加


def test_resuming_does_not_repeat_finished_speech(monkeypatch) -> None:
    def crash(text: str) -> str:
        raise RuntimeError("模拟进程被杀")

    install(monkeypatch, _replies(restance=crash))
    run_id, node_id = _meeting_run()
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        drive(run_id, node_id, _approve, idea=IDEA)

    seen: list[dict] = []
    install(monkeypatch, _replies(restance=_by_round(_agree, _agree)), seen)
    drive(run_id, node_id, _approve, idea=IDEA)

    kinds = Counter(call["kind"] for call in seen)
    for finished in ("position", "agenda", "stance", "claim", "respond", "exchange_summary"):
        assert finished not in kinds
    assert kinds["restance"] == 2                            # 只重做了被中断的那一步


def test_check_flags_are_fixed_then_rechecked_only_where_flagged(monkeypatch) -> None:
    checks = iter([CHECK_ISSUE, CHECK_OK, CHECK_OK])
    seen: list[dict] = []
    install(monkeypatch, _replies(proposal_check=lambda text: next(checks)), seen)
    run_id, node_id = _meeting_run()

    result = drive(run_id, node_id, _approve, idea=IDEA)

    assert result["done"] is True
    kinds = Counter(call["kind"] for call in seen)
    assert kinds["proposal_check"] == 3 and kinds["proposal_revise"] == 1
    meta, body = _proposal(run_id)
    assert meta["version"] == 2 and "## 修改说明" in body


def test_unresolved_check_notes_are_recorded_instead_of_looping_forever(monkeypatch) -> None:
    install(monkeypatch, _replies(proposal_check=CHECK_ISSUE))
    run_id, node_id = _meeting_run()

    result = drive(run_id, node_id, _approve, idea=IDEA)

    assert result["done"] is True
    assert _proposal(run_id)[0]["version"] == 2               # 复核轮次上限是 2
    assert "## 复核遗留意见" in parse_front_matter(read_artifact(run_id, "discussion"))[1]
```

`backend/app/meeting/store.py`：

1. `list_turns` 整个函数换成下面两个函数（把每行的字典抽成 `_turn_dict`，供按轮取发言复用）：

```python
def _turn_dict(row: MeetingTurn) -> dict:
    return {"id": row.id, "seq": row.seq, "round": row.round, "phase": row.phase,
            "speaker_id": row.speaker_id, "speaker_name": row.speaker_name, "kind": row.kind,
            "decision_point_id": row.decision_point_id, "content": row.content,
            "structured": dict(row.structured_json or {}), "reply_to": row.reply_to,
            "tokens_in": row.tokens_in, "tokens_out": row.tokens_out,
            "cached_tokens": row.cached_tokens}


def list_turns(meeting_id: int, since_seq: int = 0, limit: int = 500) -> list[dict]:
    with session_scope() as s:
        rows = (
            s.query(MeetingTurn)
            .filter(MeetingTurn.meeting_id == meeting_id, MeetingTurn.seq > since_seq)
            .order_by(MeetingTurn.seq.asc())
            .limit(limit)
            .all()
        )
        return [_turn_dict(r) for r in rows]
```

2. 文件末尾加：

```python
def round_turns(meeting_id: int, *, round: int, kinds: list[str] | None = None) -> list[dict]:
    """某一轮里有内容的发言，按序号升序。kinds 给出时只取这些类型。

    进程被杀时留下的空白发言行不算数。
    """
    with session_scope() as s:
        query = s.query(MeetingTurn).filter(
            MeetingTurn.meeting_id == meeting_id, MeetingTurn.round == round,
            MeetingTurn.content != "")
        if kinds:
            query = query.filter(MeetingTurn.kind.in_(kinds))
        rows = query.order_by(MeetingTurn.seq.asc()).all()
        return [_turn_dict(r) for r in rows]


def set_stance(point_id: int, participant_id: int, stance: dict) -> None:
    """写入某个参会者在某个决策点上的最新表态；上一轮的等级与理由留进 history。"""
    with session_scope() as s:
        row = s.get(DecisionPoint, point_id)
        if row is None:
            return
        stances = dict(row.stances_json or {})
        previous = dict(stances.get(str(participant_id)) or {})
        history = list(previous.get("history") or [])
        if previous.get("level") is not None and previous.get("round") != stance.get("round"):
            history.append({"round": previous.get("round"), "level": previous.get("level"),
                            "reason": previous.get("reason")})
        stances[str(participant_id)] = {**stance, "history": history}
        row.stances_json = stances
```

`backend/app/meeting/runner.py`：

1. 导入区加 `import importlib`、`from app.db.base import session_scope`，`from app.db.models import (...)` 那组导入里加 `Gate`。
2. `get_protocol` 整个函数换成：

```python
# 协议名就是 protocols 包里的模块名
PROTOCOL_MODULES = ("discussion", "review", "confirm")


def get_protocol(name: str) -> MeetingProtocol:
    """取协议的实例。协议模块按名字惰性导入，模块里的类用 register_protocol 登记。"""
    cls = _PROTOCOLS.get(name or "")
    if cls is None and name in _PROTOCOL_MODULES:
        try:
            importlib.import_module(f"app.meeting.protocols.{name}")
        except ModuleNotFoundError as exc:
            raise NodeFailure(f"会议协议 {name} 还没有实现", fatal=True) from exc
        cls = _PROTOCOLS.get(name)
    if cls is None:
        raise NodeFailure(f"未知的会议协议：{name}", fatal=True)
    return cls()
```

3. `runner.py` 的 `_enter` 整个函数换成（多了一处：带着反馈续开时，节点上还开着、没有决定的旧确认点一并作废，否则调度器会一直等那扇门）：

```python
def _enter(ctx: NodeContext, protocol: str) -> tuple[int, str]:
    """返回 (会议 id, 进入原因)。进入原因取 new / feedback / resume / decided / restart。

    一条反馈算不算"新的"，要同时满足三点：来路属于 REOPEN_KINDS；节点配置里的序号
    （feedback_seq）与会议状态里已处理的序号不同；内容非空。
    """
    config = ctx.config or {}
    meeting_config = {k: v for k, v in config.items() if k not in RUNTIME_KEYS}
    existing = store.latest_meeting_for_node(ctx.node_id)

    def new() -> tuple[int, str]:
        meeting_id = store.create_meeting(
            run_id=ctx.run_id, node_id=ctx.node_id, node_key=ctx.node_key, protocol=protocol,
            charter={}, config=meeting_config)
        return meeting_id, "new"

    if existing is None:
        return new()
    feedback = str(config.get("feedback") or "").strip()
    seq = config.get("feedback_seq")
    reopen = (bool(feedback) and config.get("feedback_kind") in REOPEN_KINDS
              and seq != existing["state"].get("handled_feedback_seq"))
    if reopen:
        with session_scope() as s:
            for stale in s.query(Gate).filter(
                    Gate.node_id == ctx.node_id, Gate.status == "open").all():
                stale.status = "superseded"
        store.update_meeting(
            existing["id"], status=MEETING_ACTIVE,
            state_json={**existing["state"], "pending_feedback": feedback,
                        "feedback_kind": config.get("feedback_kind"),
                        "seen_feedback_seq": seq})
        return existing["id"], "feedback"
    if existing["status"] in (MEETING_ACTIVE, MEETING_WAITING_REWORK):
        return existing["id"], "resume"
    if existing["status"] == MEETING_SUSPENDED and _checkpoint_decided(existing["state"]):
        return existing["id"], "decided"
    store.update_meeting(existing["id"], status=MEETING_ABANDONED)
    return new()
```

`backend/app/engine/gates.py`：方案确认门原来从讨论稿的"未决项"小节里找待确认的问题。讨论稿现在是程序生成的纪要，待确认的问题在账本里。导入区去掉不再用到的 `Path`、`settings`、`parse_front_matter`、`Artifact`，加上 `DecisionPoint`、`Meeting`；`PENDING_TITLE` 换成下面这个常量；`_extract_pending_questions` 和 `_pending_questions` 两个函数换成下面这一个：

```python
PENDING_TITLE = "待确认问题（需要你决定）"


def _pending_questions(s, run_id: str) -> list[str]:
    """账本里待定的决策点，供方案确认门的卡片展示。

    必须复用调用方 session（连接池 size=1，另开 session 会在池上死等）。
    取最近一场讨论会议的账本；讨论会议还没开过时返回空列表。
    """
    meeting = (s.query(Meeting)
               .filter(Meeting.run_id == run_id, Meeting.protocol == "discussion")
               .order_by(Meeting.id.desc())
               .first())
    if meeting is None:
        return []
    rows = (s.query(DecisionPoint)
            .filter(DecisionPoint.meeting_id == meeting.id,
                    DecisionPoint.status == "deferred")
            .order_by(DecisionPoint.id.asc())
            .all())
    return [f"- [ ] {row.index} {row.title}：{row.conclusion or '各方理由见会议纪要'}"
            for row in rows]
```

- [x] **Step 4: 写讨论协议**

`backend/app/meeting/protocols/discussion.py`：

```python
"""讨论协议（详设 §7）。

步骤依次是：
  prepare     会前准备：主持人把想法与背景材料整理成简报，列出需要澄清的问题
  clarify     澄清：用户或需求方代理逐条回答
  roster      名单：用户指定的照用；否则主持人提议，用户确认或自动生效
  positions   独立立场：全体并行，互不可见
  agenda      议题提炼：主持人把立场合并成决策点清单，由程序落盘
  stances     全员表态：读全部立场和完整清单，对每个决策点表态
  exchange    交锋：逐个未了结的决策点"主张 → 质疑 → 回应"
  summary     交锋摘要：主持人为每个未了结的决策点写一段摘要
  restance    全员重新表态：改立场必须写理由；程序结算、判停滞、决定下一轮去哪
  consensus   轮次共识：处理未决议题（全部了结且由程序判定时直接往下走）
  reopen      复议：带着反馈续开时，把反馈转成要重开的决策点
  draft       整合成稿：主持人按账本写建设方案，程序附上决策点索引与待确认问题
  check       全员复核：读初稿，逐个确认已了结的决策点被如实反映

引擎掌控流程：谁在何时发言、何时算了结、何时停，全由程序按规则判定；模型只负责内容。
"""

from typing import Callable

from app.artifacts.store import (latest_artifact, parse_front_matter, read_artifact,

from app.engine.events import emit
```
