```python
# ---------------- 校验模型的结构化输出 ----------------
def _need(ok: bool, message: str) -> list[str]:
    return [] if ok else [message]


def _validate_brief(data: dict) -> list[str]:
    issues = _need(isinstance(data.get("brief"), str) and bool(data["brief"].strip()),
                   "brief 须是非空的文本")
    questions = data.get("questions", [])
    if not isinstance(questions, list) or not all(isinstance(item, str) for item in questions):
        issues.append("questions 须是字符串数组；没有要澄清的就给空数组")
    return issues


def _validate_answers(count: int) -> Validator:
    def validate(data: dict) -> list[str]:
        rows = data.get("answers")
        if not isinstance(rows, list) or len(rows) != count:
            return [f"answers 须恰好包含 {count} 项，逐条对应问题"]
        return [f"第 {position + 1} 条回答缺少 text"
                for position, row in enumerate(rows)
                if not isinstance(row, dict) or not str(row.get("text") or "").strip()]
    return validate


def _validate_roster(library: dict, low: int, high: int) -> Validator:
    def validate(data: dict) -> list[str]:
        rows = data.get("participants")
        if not isinstance(rows, list) or not rows:
            return ["participants 须是非空数组"]
        issues: list[str] = []
        for row in rows:
            if not isinstance(row, dict):
                issues.append("participants 的每一项须是对象")
                continue
            key = str(row.get("role_key") or "")
            if key and key not in library:
                issues.append(f"role_key 不存在：{key}")
            if not key and not (str(row.get("display_name") or "").strip()
                                and str(row.get("persona") or "").strip()):
                issues.append("临时角色必须同时给出 display_name 与 persona")
        if low == high:
            issues += _need(len(rows) == low, f"用户指定了固定人数：必须正好 {low} 名")
        else:
            issues += _need(low <= len(rows) <= high, f"人数须在 {low} 到 {high} 名之间")
        return issues
    return validate


def _validate_position(data: dict) -> list[str]:
    issues = []
    for key in ("must", "risks"):
        if not isinstance(data.get(key), list) or not all(isinstance(x, str) for x in data[key]):
            issues.append(f"{key} 须是字符串数组")
    issues += _need(isinstance(data.get("route"), str) and bool(data["route"].strip()),
                    "route 须是非空的文本")
    return issues


def _validate_agenda(data: dict) -> list[str]:
    rows = data.get("points")
    if not isinstance(rows, list) or not rows:
        return ["points 须是非空数组：至少提炼出一个决策点"]
    return ["每个决策点须有 title" for row in rows
            if not isinstance(row, dict) or not str(row.get("title") or "").strip()]


def _validate_stances(data: dict) -> list[str]:
    rows = data.get("stances")
    if not isinstance(rows, list) or not rows:
        return ["stances 须是非空数组"]
    issues: list[str] = []
    required = {2: "顾虑", 3: "弃权的原因", 4: "缺什么信息", 5: "反对的理由和替代建议"}
    for row in rows:
        if not isinstance(row, dict) or not str(row.get("index") or ""):
            issues.append("stances 的每一项须有 index")
            continue
        level = row.get("level")
        if isinstance(level, bool) or not isinstance(level, int) or not 1 <= level <= 5:
            issues.append(f"{row['index']}：level 须是 1 到 5 的整数")
        elif level >= 2 and not str(row.get("reason") or "").strip():
            issues.append(f"{row['index']}：{LEVEL_NAMES[level]}必须写明{required[level]}")
    for extra in data.get("new_points") or []:
        if not isinstance(extra, dict) or not str(extra.get("title") or "").strip():
            issues.append("new_points 的每一项须有 title")
    return issues


def _validate_summaries(open_indexes: list[str]) -> Validator:
    def validate(data: dict) -> list[str]:
        rows = data.get("summaries")
        rows = rows if isinstance(rows, list) else []
        covered = {str(row.get("index")) for row in rows if isinstance(row, dict)}
        if covered != set(open_indexes):
            return [f"summaries 须恰好覆盖这些决策点：{open_indexes}"]
        return [f"{row['index']} 的 summary 不能为空" for row in rows
                if not str(row.get("summary") or "").strip()]
    return validate


def _validate_rulings(open_indexes: list[str]) -> Validator:
    def validate(data: dict) -> list[str]:
        rows = data.get("rulings")
        rows = rows if isinstance(rows, list) else []
        covered = {str(row.get("index")) for row in rows if isinstance(row, dict)}
        if covered != set(open_indexes):
            return [f"rulings 须恰好覆盖这些决策点：{open_indexes}"]
        return [f"{row['index']} 的 {field} 不能为空" for row in rows
                for field in ("conclusion", "rationale")
                if not str(row.get(field) or "").strip()]
    return validate


def _validate_check(data: dict) -> list[str]:
    if not isinstance(data.get("ok"), bool):
        return ["ok 须是 true 或 false"]
    if data["ok"]:
        return []
    rows = data.get("issues")
    if not isinstance(rows, list) or not rows:
        return ["ok 为 false 时 issues 须列出至少一条意见"]
    return ["每条复核意见须有 problem" for row in rows
            if not isinstance(row, dict) or not str(row.get("problem") or "").strip()]


def _validate_reopen(data: dict) -> list[str]:
    rows = data.get("points")
    if not isinstance(rows, list) or not rows:
        return ["points 须是非空数组：至少重开或新建一个决策点"]
    return ["已有的决策点给 index；新的决策点给 title" for row in rows
            if not isinstance(row, dict)
            or not (str(row.get("index") or "").strip() or str(row.get("title") or "").strip())]


# ---------------- 读写账本与发言 ----------------
def _artifact_body(run_id: str, kind: str) -> str:
    raw = read_artifact(run_id, kind)
    return parse_front_matter(raw)[1].strip() if raw else ""


def _facilitator(mtg) -> dict | None:
    return next(iter(mtg.participants("facilitator")), None)


def _proxy(mtg) -> dict | None:
    return next(iter(mtg.participants("proxy")), None)


def _proxy_needed(mtg) -> bool:
    return bool(mtg.params().get("proxy_enabled")) or mtg.decider("clarify") == "proxy"


def _points(mtg) -> list[dict]:
    return store.list_decision_points(mtg.meeting_id)


def _open_points(points: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    return [point for point in points if point["status"] in OPEN_STATUSES]


def _voters(mtg) -> list[int]:
    return [member["id"] for member in mtg.participants("participant")]


def _rounds_max(mtg) -> int:
    return max(1, int(mtg.params().get("rounds_max") or 20))


def _charter(mtg) -> list[Segment]:
    return render.charter_segments(mtg.meeting()["charter"])


def _view(mtg, kinds: list[str] | None = None) -> list[Segment]:
    """人设之后发给模型的段：账本快照在前，本轮的逐字发言在后。"""
    points = _points(mtg)
    index_of = {point["id"]: point["index"] for point in points}
    segments = [render.ledger_segment(points, round_no=mtg.meeting_round())]
    for turn in store.round_turns(mtg.meeting_id, round=mtg.meeting_round(), kinds=kinds):
        turn["decision_point"] = index_of.get(turn["decision_point_id"])
        segments.append(render.turn_segment(turn))
    return segments


def _positions(mtg) -> list[Segment]:
    return [render.turn_segment(turn)
            for turn in store.round_turns(mtg.meeting_id, round=1, kinds=["position"])]


async def _ledger_updated(mtg) -> None:
    version = int(mtg.state().get("ledger_version") or 0) + 1
    mtg.set_state(ledger_version=version)
    await emit(mtg.run_id, "meeting_ledger_updated", mtg.node_id, {
        "meeting_id": mtg.meeting_id, "version": version,
        "decision_points": [{"index": point["index"], "status": point["status"]}
                            for point in _points(mtg)]})


def _mark(mtg, key: str) -> None:
    """记下交锋里已经完成的哪一次发言，重入时据此跳过。"""
    done = set(mtg.state().get("exchange_done") or [])
    mtg.set_state(exchange_done=sorted(done | {key}))


def _close(mtg, index: str, status: str, conclusion: str, rationale: str = "") -> None:
    for point in _points(mtg):
        if point["index"] == index:
            store.update_decision_point(point["id"], status=status, conclusion=conclusion,
                                        rationale=rationale or point["rationale"],
                                        closed_round=mtg.meeting_round())


def _conclusion_of(point: dict) -> str:
    """达成一致的决策点，结论取支持者里最常见的选择。"""
    choices = Counter((stance.get("choice") or "").strip()
                      for stance in (point.get("stances") or {}).values()
                      if stance.get("level") in (1, 2) and (stance.get("choice") or "").strip())
    return choices.most_common(1)[0][0] if choices else "见交锋摘要"


def _settle_round(mtg) -> dict:
    """一轮表态之后结算：达成一致的落盘。返回结算结果（open、agreed、changed）。"""
    points = _points(mtg)
    result = settle(points, rule=str(mtg.params().get("closure_rule") or "unanimous"),
                    voters=_voters(mtg), before=mtg.state().get("stance_snapshot"))
    by_index = {point["index"]: point for point in points}
    for index in result["agreed"]:
        point = by_index[index]
        supporters = [f"{stance.get('name')}：{stance.get('reason')}"
                      for stance in (point.get("stances") or {}).values()
                      if stance.get("level") in (1, 2) and stance.get("reason")]
        _close(mtg, index, "agreed", _conclusion_of(point), "；".join(supporters)[:600])
    return result


def _apply_stances(mtg, participant: dict, data: dict) -> None:
    """把一次表态写进账本。表态里补充的遗漏决策点先建成新的决策点。"""
    by_index = {point["index"]: point for point in _points(mtg)}
    number = len(by_index) + 1
    round_no = mtg.meeting_round()
    rows = list(data["stances"])
    for extra in data.get("new_points") or []:
        index = f"D{number}"
        number += 1
        point_id = store.upsert_decision_point(
            mtg.meeting_id, index, title=extra["title"].strip(),
            question=str(extra.get("question") or ""), options_json=list(extra.get("options") or []),
            raised_by=participant["display_name"], origin_round=round_no)
        by_index[index] = {"id": point_id, "index": index}
        rows.append({"index": index, "level": extra.get("level", 3),
                     "reason": extra.get("reason", ""), "choice": extra.get("choice", "")})
    for row in rows:
        point = by_index.get(str(row.get("index")))
        if point is None:
            continue                                   # 账本里没有的编号：不计入
        store.set_stance(point["id"], participant["id"], {
            "level": int(row["level"]), "reason": str(row.get("reason") or ""),
            "choice": str(row.get("choice") or ""), "round": round_no,
            "name": participant["display_name"]})


def _can_join_discussion(role: dict) -> bool:
    """能当讨论参会者的角色：元数据标了适合讨论，或者没有标会议角色（用户自建、没填）。"""
    roles = (role.get("meta") or {}).get("meeting_roles")
    return "discussion" in roles if roles is not None else True


def _write_charter(mtg) -> None:
    """会议章程：整场会议不变，所有角色共用。"""
    state = mtg.state()
    params = mtg.params()
    library = {row["role_key"]: row for row in role_library()}
    roster = []
    for row in mtg.participants():
        focus = ((library.get(row["role_key"] or "") or {}).get("meta") or {}).get("description")
        roster.append(f"- {row['display_name']}（{MODE_NAMES.get(row['mode'], row['mode'])}）："
                      f"{focus or row['system_prompt'][:60]}")
    clarifications = state.get("clarifications") or []
    answered = "\n".join(
        f"- 问：{item['question']}\n  答：{item['answer']}（{SOURCE_NAMES[item['source']]}）"
        for item in clarifications) or "（没有需要澄清的问题）"
    rule = str(params.get("closure_rule") or "unanimous")
    materials, _truncated = mtg.materials()
    charter = {
        "title": "讨论会议章程",
        "sections": [
            {"title": "想法原文", "text": (mtg.ctx.idea or "").strip()},
            {"title": "想法简报", "text": state.get("brief") or ""},
            {"title": "已澄清的问题", "text": answered},
            {"title": "参会名单", "text": "\n".join(roster)},
            {"title": "闭合规则", "text": CLOSURE_TEXT.get(rule, CLOSURE_TEXT["unanimous"])},
            {"title": "轮次", "text": (
                f"讨论最多进行 {_rounds_max(mtg)} 轮；"
                f"连续 {params.get('stall_rounds') or 2} 轮没有人改变立场时，进入共识确认。")},
        ],
        "materials": materials,
    }
    store.update_meeting(mtg.meeting_id, charter_json=charter)


def _roster_description(proposed: list[dict], low: int, high: int,
                        fixed: int | None) -> str:
    lines = ["主持人提议的参会名单："]
    lines += [f"- {row['display_name']}：{row.get('reason') or ''}" for row in proposed]
    lines += ["", "用户指定了固定人数。" if fixed else f"人数要求：{low} 到 {high} 名。",
              "可以直接通过；也可以先增删角色、改人设或模型，再通过；",
              "或者驳回并写明意见，让主持人重新提议。"]
    return "\n".join(lines)


def _open_brief(point: dict) -> dict:
    stances = sorted((point.get("stances") or {}).items(), key=lambda item: int(item[0]))
    return {"index": point["index"], "title": point["title"], "question": point["question"],
            "options": list(point.get("options") or []),
            "stances": [{"name": stance.get("name"),
                         "level": LEVEL_NAMES.get(stance.get("level"), "未表态"),
                         "reason": stance.get("reason") or ""} for _, stance in stances]}


def _consensus_description(mtg, open_points: list[dict], reason: str) -> str:
    rule = str(mtg.params().get("closure_rule") or "unanimous")
    lines = [REASON_TEXT.get(reason, ""),
             f"已进行 {mtg.meeting_round()} 轮。闭合规则：{CLOSURE_TEXT.get(rule, '')}"]
    if open_points:
        lines += ["", "未了结的决策点："]
        for point in open_points:
            lines.append(f"- {point['index']} {point['title']}")
            for stance in _open_brief(point)["stances"]:
                lines.append(f"  - {stance['name']}：{stance['level']}"
                             + (f"——{stance['reason']}" if stance["reason"] else ""))
    lines += ["", "请选择："]
    lines += [f"{number}. {option['label']}：{option['description']}"
              for number, option in enumerate(CONSENSUS_OPTIONS, start=1)]
    return "\n".join(lines)


def _index_section(points: list[dict]) -> str:
    lines = ["## 决策点索引", "", "| 编号 | 状态 | 结论 |", "|---|---|---|"]
    lines += [f"| {point['index']} | {render.STATUS_NAMES.get(point['status'], point['status'])} | "
              f"{point.get('conclusion') or '—'} |" for point in points]
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"


def _pending_section(mtg, points: list[dict]) -> str:
    """待确认问题由程序生成：待定的决策点带着各方理由，没有出处的澄清结果按假设列出。"""
    items = []
    for point in points:
        if point["status"] != DEFERRED:
            continue
        reasons = "；".join(f"{stance.get('name')}：{stance.get('reason')}"
                           for stance in (point.get("stances") or {}).values()
                           if stance.get("reason"))
        items.append(f"- [ ] {point['index']} {point['title']}：{reasons or '各方未达成一致'}")
    for item in mtg.state().get("clarifications") or []:
        if item["source"] == "assumption":
            items.append(f"- [ ] 假设：{item['question']}——{item['answer']}")
    return "\n".join(["## 待确认问题"] + (items or ["（无）"])) + "\n"


@register_protocol
class DiscussionProtocol(MeetingProtocol):
    name = "discussion"
    steps = ("prepare", "clarify", "roster", "positions", "agenda", "stances", "exchange",
             "summary", "restance", "consensus", "reopen", "draft", "check")

    async def on_enter(self, mtg) -> None:
        feedback = mtg.consume_feedback()
        if feedback:
            mtg.set_state(reopen_feedback=feedback)
            store.update_meeting(mtg.meeting_id, phase="reopen")

    # ---------- 会前准备与澄清 ----------
    async def step_prepare(self, mtg):
        if _facilitator(mtg) is None:
            template = role_template("facilitator")
            store.add_participant(mtg.meeting_id, role_key="facilitator",
                                  display_name=template["name"], mode="facilitator",
                                  system_prompt=template["system_prompt"])
        if _proxy_needed(mtg) and _proxy(mtg) is None:
            template = role_template("requester_proxy")
            store.add_participant(mtg.meeting_id, role_key="requester_proxy",
                                  display_name=template["name"], mode="proxy",
                                  system_prompt=template["system_prompt"])
        if mtg.state().get("brief"):
            return advance()                          # 简报已经写过（续开时）
        materials, truncated = mtg.materials()
        segments = []
        if materials:
            note = "\n（背景材料超出预算，以上只提供了前面的一部分）" if truncated else ""
            segments.append(Segment("append", f"## 背景材料\n{materials}{note}", name="背景材料"))
        result = await speak(mtg, _facilitator(mtg), kind="brief", turn_kind="brief",
                             segments=segments, structured=_validate_brief,
                             turn_round=mtg.meeting_round(),
                             instruction=f"## 想法原文\n{(mtg.ctx.idea or '').strip()}"
                                         f"{BRIEF_INSTRUCTION}")
        brief = result["data"]["brief"].strip()
        questions = [str(item).strip() for item in result["data"].get("questions") or []
                     if str(item).strip()]
        write_artifact(mtg.run_id, "idea",
                       f"# 想法\n\n{(mtg.ctx.idea or '').strip()}\n\n---\n\n# 想法简报\n\n{brief}\n",
                       created_by="facilitator", inputs=["idea"], status="approved")
        await emit(mtg.run_id, "artifact_written", mtg.node_id,
                   latest_artifact(mtg.run_id, "idea"))
        mtg.set_state(brief=brief, questions=questions)
        return advance()

    async def step_clarify(self, mtg):
        state = mtg.state()
        if "clarifications" in state:
            return advance()
        questions = state.get("questions") or []
        if not questions:
            mtg.set_state(clarifications=[])
            return advance()
        listing = "\n".join(f"{number}. {question}"
                            for number, question in enumerate(questions, start=1))
        if mtg.decider("clarify") == "proxy":
            proxy = _proxy(mtg)
            rows = [{"text": "", "source": ""} for _ in questions]   # 没有代理可用：全部记为假设
            if proxy is not None:
                materials, _truncated = mtg.materials()
                segments = ([Segment("append", f"## 背景材料\n{materials}", name="背景材料")]
                            if materials else [])
                rows = (await speak(
                    mtg, proxy, kind="answer", turn_kind="answer", segments=segments,
                    instruction=f"{ANSWER_INSTRUCTION}\n\n## 待澄清的问题\n{listing}",
                    structured=_validate_answers(len(questions)),
                    turn_round=mtg.meeting_round()))["data"]["answers"]
            clarifications = []
            for question, row in zip(questions, rows):
                text = str(row.get("text") or "").strip()
                evidence = str(row.get("source") or "").strip()
                if text and evidence:
                    clarifications.append({"question": question, "answer": text,
                                           "source": "proxy", "evidence": evidence})
                else:                                  # 没有出处的回答：标为假设
                    clarifications.append({
                        "question": question, "source": "assumption", "evidence": "",
                        "answer": f"{text}（没有出处，按假设处理）" if text
                        else "材料中没有说明，按假设处理"})
            await record_auto_decision(
                mtg, "clarify", decided_by="proxy" if proxy else "program",
                decision={"action": "approve", "answered": len(questions),
                          "assumptions": sum(1 for item in clarifications
                                             if item["source"] == "assumption")},
                title="澄清问题已回答")
            mtg.set_state(clarifications=clarifications)
            return advance()
        decision = mtg.decision()
        if decision is None:
            return suspend(await mtg.open_checkpoint(
                "clarify", title="请回答几个澄清问题", description=listing,
                payload={"questions": questions}))
        answers = [str(item).strip() for item in decision["data"].get("answers") or []]
        clarifications = []
        for number, question in enumerate(questions, start=1):
            text = answers[number - 1] if number <= len(answers) else ""
            if text:
                source = "user"
            else:
                text, source = "用户没有回答，按假设处理", "assumption"
            clarifications.append({"question": question, "answer": text,
                                   "source": source, "evidence": ""})
            store.add_turn(mtg.meeting_id, round=mtg.meeting_round(), phase="clarify",
                           speaker_id=None, speaker_name="用户", kind="answer",
                           content=f"问：{question}\n答：{text}")
        mtg.set_state(clarifications=clarifications)
        return advance()

    # ---------- 名单 ----------
    async def step_roster(self, mtg):
        state = mtg.state()
        if state.get("roster_done"):
            return advance()
        params = mtg.params()
        minimum = max(1, int(params.get("participants_min") or 1))
        maximum = max(minimum, int(params.get("participants_max") or minimum))
        spec = (mtg.run_config().get("rosters") or {}).get("participants") or {}
        fixed = int(spec["count"]) if spec.get("count") else None
        low, high = (minimum, maximum) if fixed is None else (fixed, fixed)
        if not state.get("roster"):
            await self._propose(mtg, spec, low, high)
        source = mtg.state()["roster_source"]
        decider = mtg.decider("roster")
        if source == "user" or decider == "auto":
            if source != "user":
                await record_auto_decision(
                    mtg, "roster", decided_by="program", title="参会名单自动生效",
                    decision={"action": "approve",
                              "participants": [row["display_name"]
                                               for row in mtg.participants("participant")]})
            return self._finish_roster(mtg)
        decision = mtg.decision()
        if decision is None:
            return suspend(await mtg.open_checkpoint(
                "roster", title="请确认参会名单",
                description=_roster_description(mtg.state()["roster"], low, high, fixed),
                payload={"proposed": mtg.state()["roster"], "min": low, "max": high,
                         "fixed": fixed}))
        if decision["action"] == "approve" and mtg.participants("participant"):
            return self._finish_roster(mtg)
        # 驳回，或者通过时名单是空的：带着意见重新提议
        for row in mtg.participants("participant"):
            store.update_participant(row["id"], status="removed")
        mtg.set_state(roster=None,
                      roster_feedback=decision.get("feedback") or "至少需要一名参会角色")
        return advance("roster")

    async def _propose(self, mtg, spec: dict, low: int, high: int) -> None:
        """名单的优先级：用户点名 > 主持人在人数要求内挑选。结果写成参会者。"""
        library = {row["role_key"]: row for row in role_library()}
        if spec.get("role_keys"):
            keys = [key for key in spec["role_keys"] if key in library]
            if not keys:
                raise NodeFailure("运行级配置里点名的参会角色都已不存在，请修改运行级配置")
            picked = [{"role_key": key, "display_name": library[key]["name"], "reason": "用户指定"}
                      for key in keys]
            source = "user"
        else:
            offered = [row for row in library.values() if _can_join_discussion(row)]
            listing = "\n".join(f"- {row['role_key']}｜{row['name']}｜"
                               f"{row['meta'].get('description') or ''}" for row in offered)
            count_text = f"请正好挑选 {low} 名" if low == high else f"请挑选 {low} 到 {high} 名"
            segments = [Segment("append", f"## 想法\n{mtg.ctx.idea or ''}\n\n"
                                          f"## 想法简报\n{mtg.state().get('brief') or ''}",
                                name="想法与简报"),
                        Segment("append", f"## 角色库（可以从中挑选）\n{listing}", name="角色库")]
            feedback = mtg.state().get("roster_feedback")
            if feedback:
                segments.append(Segment("append", f"## 用户对上一份名单的意见\n{feedback}",
                                        name="名单意见"))
            picked = (await speak(
                mtg, _facilitator(mtg), kind="roster", turn_kind="roster_proposal",
                segments=segments, instruction=f"{ROSTER_INSTRUCTION}\n{count_text}。",
                structured=_validate_roster(library, low, high),
                turn_round=mtg.meeting_round()))["data"]["participants"]
            source = "moderator"
        for row in picked:
            key = str(row.get("role_key") or "")
            template = library.get(key)
            store.add_participant(
                mtg.meeting_id, role_key=key or None,
                display_name=str(row.get("display_name") or (template["name"] if template
                                                             else "")).strip(),
                mode="participant",
                system_prompt=template["system_prompt"] if template
                else str(row.get("persona") or ""),
                source=source, expertise=list((template or {}).get("meta", {}).get("tags") or []))
        mtg.set_state(roster=picked, roster_source=source)

    def _finish_roster(self, mtg):
        _write_charter(mtg)
        mtg.set_state(roster_done=True)
        return advance()

    # ---------- 立场、议题、表态 ----------
    async def step_positions(self, mtg):
        members = mtg.participants("participant")
        if not members:
            raise NodeFailure("讨论会议没有参会者")
        charter = _charter(mtg)
        await speak_many(mtg, [
            {"participant": member, "kind": "position", "turn_kind": "position",
             "charter": charter, "segments": [], "instruction": POSITION_INSTRUCTION,
             "structured": _validate_position, "turn_round": mtg.meeting_round()}
            for member in members])
        return advance()

    async def step_agenda(self, mtg):
        if mtg.state().get("agenda_done"):
            return advance()
        result = await speak(mtg, _facilitator(mtg), kind="agenda", turn_kind="agenda",
                             charter=_charter(mtg), segments=_positions(mtg),
                             instruction=AGENDA_INSTRUCTION, structured=_validate_agenda,
                             turn_round=mtg.meeting_round())
        existing = len(_points(mtg))
        for offset, item in enumerate(result["data"]["points"]):
            store.upsert_decision_point(
                mtg.meeting_id, f"D{existing + offset + 1}", title=item["title"].strip(),
                question=str(item.get("question") or ""),
                options_json=[str(option) for option in (item.get("options") or [])],
                raised_by=str(item.get("raised_by") or ""), origin_round=mtg.meeting_round())
        mtg.set_state(agenda_done=True)
        await _ledger_updated(mtg)
        return advance()

    async def step_stances(self, mtg):
        members = mtg.participants("participant")
        if len(members) <= 1:
            # 只有一名参会者：立场即共识，不经过表态和交锋
            for point in _open_points(_points(mtg)):
                _close(mtg, point["index"], "agreed", "唯一参会者的立场", "只有一名参会者")
            await _ledger_updated(mtg)
            return advance("reopen")
        charter, view = _charter(mtg), _view(mtg, ["position"])
        results = await speak_many(mtg, [
            {"participant": member, "kind": "stance", "turn_kind": "stance", "charter": charter,
             "segments": view, "instruction": STANCE_INSTRUCTION, "structured": _validate_stances,
             "turn_round": mtg.meeting_round()} for member in members])
        for member, result in zip(members, results):
            _apply_stances(mtg, member, result["data"])
        _settle_round(mtg)
        remaining = _open_points(_points(mtg))
        await _ledger_updated(mtg)
        if not remaining:
            mtg.set_state(consensus_reason="all_closed")
            return advance("consensus")
        mtg.set_state(stance_snapshot=snapshot(remaining), stall=0, exchange_done=[], exchanges={})
        return advance()

    # ---------- 交锋轮次 ----------
    async def step_exchange(self, mtg):
        members = mtg.participants("participant")
        limit = max(1, int(mtg.params().get("exchanges_per_point") or 2))
        round_no = mtg.meeting_round()
        charter = _charter(mtg)
        by_id = {member["id"]: member for member in members}
        done = set(mtg.state().get("exchange_done") or [])
        exchanges: dict[str, int] = {}
        for point in _open_points(_points(mtg)):
            ranked = rank_speakers(point, [member["id"] for member in members])
            claimer = by_id[ranked[0]]
            subject = f"决策点 {point['index']}「{point['title']}」：{point['question']}"
            exchanges[point["index"]] = 0
            for number in range(1, limit + 1):
                mark = f"{point['index']}:{number}"
                if mark in done:                       # 这一次交锋重入前已经完成
                    exchanges[point["index"]] += 1
                    continue
                opponent = ranked[-number] if ranked[-number] != ranked[0] else ranked[-1]
                challenger = by_id[opponent]
                instructions = {
                    "claim": f"关于{subject}，请主张你支持的立场，给出理由和依据。只写一段话。",
                    "challenge": (f"关于{subject}，请质疑刚才的主张：指出它站不住的地方并给出理由；"
                                  "如果你的立场不同，说明你支持什么。只写一段话。"),
                    "respond": (f"关于{subject}，请回应刚才的质疑：采纳并说明你如何调整立场，"
                                "或不采纳并说明理由。只写一段话。"),
                }
                for kind, speaker in (("claim", claimer), ("challenge", challenger),
                                      ("respond", claimer)):
                    if f"{mark}:{kind}" in done:
                        continue
                    await speak(mtg, speaker, kind=kind, turn_kind=kind, charter=charter,
                                segments=_view(mtg, _EXCHANGE_KINDS),
                                decision_point_id=point["id"], turn_round=round_no,
                                instruction=instructions[kind])
                    _mark(mtg, f"{mark}:{kind}")
                exchanges[point["index"]] += 1
        mtg.set_state(exchanges={**(mtg.state().get("exchanges") or {}), **exchanges})
        return advance()

    async def step_summary(self, mtg):
        round_no = mtg.meeting_round()
        facilitator = _facilitator(mtg)
        open_indexes = [point["index"] for point in _open_points(_points(mtg))]
        found = store.find_turn(mtg.meeting_id, phase="summary", round=round_no,
                                kind="exchange_summary", speaker_id=facilitator["id"])
        data = found["data"] if found else (await speak(
            mtg, facilitator, kind="exchange_summary", turn_kind="exchange_summary",
            charter=_charter(mtg), segments=_view(mtg, _EXCHANGE_KINDS),
            instruction=SUMMARY_INSTRUCTION, structured=_validate_summaries(open_indexes),
            turn_round=round_no))["data"]
        index_of = {point["index"]: point["id"] for point in _points(mtg)}
        for row in data["summaries"]:
            if str(row["index"]) in index_of:
                store.update_decision_point(index_of[str(row["index"])],
                                            exchange_summary=str(row["summary"]).strip())
        return advance()

    async def _rule(self, mtg, indexes: list[str]) -> None:
        """请主持人为这些决策点裁决：写结论、理由，并记下保留的异议。"""
        facilitator = _facilitator(mtg)
        found = store.find_turn(mtg.meeting_id, phase=mtg.meeting()["phase"],
                                round=mtg.meeting_round(), kind="ruling",
                                speaker_id=facilitator["id"])
        data = found["data"] if found else (await speak(
            mtg, facilitator, kind="ruling", turn_kind="ruling", charter=_charter(mtg),
            segments=[render.ledger_segment(_points(mtg), round_no=mtg.meeting_round())],
            instruction=RULING_INSTRUCTION, structured=_validate_rulings(indexes),
            turn_round=mtg.meeting_round()))["data"]
        rulings = {str(row["index"]): row for row in data["rulings"]}
        for index in indexes:
            row = rulings.get(index)
            if row is None:
                continue
            rationale = str(row["rationale"]).strip()
            dissent = str(row.get("dissent") or "").strip()
            if dissent:
                rationale = f"{rationale}（保留的异议：{dissent}）"
            _close(mtg, index, "ruled", str(row["conclusion"]).strip(), rationale)
        await _ledger_updated(mtg)

    async def step_restance(self, mtg):
        members = mtg.participants("participant")
        round_no = mtg.meeting_round()
        params = mtg.params()
        # 闭合规则是"主持人裁决"：交锋次数已满、仍未一致的交给主持人裁决
        used_up = to_rule(_points(mtg), mtg.state().get("exchanges") or {},
                          int(params.get("exchanges_per_point") or 2))
        if used_up:
            await self._rule(mtg, used_up)
        charter, view = _charter(mtg), _view(mtg, _ROUND_KINDS)
        results = await speak_many(mtg, [
            {"participant": member, "kind": "restance", "turn_kind": "restance",
             "charter": charter, "segments": view, "instruction": RESTANCE_INSTRUCTION,
             "structured": _validate_stances, "turn_round": round_no} for member in members])
        for member, result in zip(members, results):
            _apply_stances(mtg, member, result["data"])
        outcome = _settle_round(mtg)
        remaining = _open_points(_points(mtg))
        await _ledger_updated(mtg)
        if not remaining:
            mtg.set_state(consensus_reason="all_closed")
            return advance("consensus")
        stall = 0 if outcome["changed"] else int(mtg.state().get("stall") or 0) + 1
        mtg.set_state(stall=stall)
        if mtg.decider("consensus") == "human":       # 全程人审：每轮结束都由人判
            mtg.set_state(consensus_reason="round_end")
            return advance("consensus")
        if stall >= int(params.get("stall_rounds") or 2):
            mtg.set_state(consensus_reason="stall")
            return advance("consensus")
        if round_no >= int(mtg.state().get("rounds_limit") or _rounds_max(mtg)):
            mtg.set_state(consensus_reason="rounds_max")
            return advance("consensus")
        # 进入下一轮：账本快照会更新，这是预期内的变化，不该报冻结段被改
        store.update_meeting(mtg.meeting_id, round=round_no + 1)
        mtg.set_state(stance_snapshot=snapshot(remaining), exchange_done=[], exchanges={})
        mtg.reset_builder()
        return advance("exchange")

    # ---------- 轮次共识 ----------
    async def step_consensus(self, mtg):
        points = _points(mtg)
        open_points = _open_points(points)
        decider = mtg.decider("consensus")
        reason = str(mtg.state().get("consensus_reason")
                     or ("all_closed" if not open_points else "stall"))
        if not open_points and decider != "human":
            return advance()                          # 程序判定：全部了结，直接成稿
        if open_points and decider == "program_fallback":
            # 全自动模式下的退让：先按"无反对即可"了结，仍不行的记为待定
            outcome = relax(points, _voters(mtg))
            by_index = {point["index"]: point for point in points}
            for index in outcome["agreed"]:
                _close(mtg, index, "agreed", _conclusion_of(by_index[index]),
                       "全自动模式下按“无反对即可”了结")
            for index in outcome["deferred"]:
                _close(mtg, index, DEFERRED, "各方未达成一致，待用户决定")
            await record_auto_decision(
                mtg, "consensus", decided_by="program", title="轮次共识由程序判定",
                decision={"action": "approve", "option": "relax", **outcome},
                payload={"reason": reason})
            await _ledger_updated(mtg)
            return advance()
        decision = mtg.decision()
        if decision is None:
            return suspend(await mtg.open_checkpoint(
                "consensus", title=CONSENSUS_TITLES.get(reason, "轮次共识"),
                description=_consensus_description(mtg, open_points, reason),
                payload={"reason": reason, "round": mtg.meeting_round(),
                         "options": CONSENSUS_OPTIONS,
                         "open": [_open_brief(point) for point in open_points]}))
        data = decision["data"] or {}
        option = str(data.get("option") or ("integrate" if decision["action"] == "approve"
                                            else "continue"))
        if option == "continue":
            # 再讨论一轮：指导语作为一条用户发言进入纪要，算在下一轮里
            following = mtg.meeting_round() + 1
            store.add_turn(mtg.meeting_id, round=following, phase="consensus", speaker_id=None,
                           speaker_name="用户", kind="user_guidance",
                           content=decision.get("feedback") or "")
            store.update_meeting(mtg.meeting_id, round=following)
            mtg.set_state(consensus_reason=None, stall=0, exchange_done=[], exchanges={},
                          stance_snapshot=snapshot(open_points),
                          rounds_limit=max(_rounds_max(mtg), following))
            mtg.reset_builder()
            return advance("exchange")
        if option == "delegate" and open_points:
            await self._rule(mtg, [point["index"] for point in open_points])
        else:
            for point in open_points:                 # 带着分歧整合：未了结的记为待定
                _close(mtg, point["index"], DEFERRED, "各方未达成一致，待用户决定")
            await _ledger_updated(mtg)
        mtg.set_state(consensus_reason=None)
        return advance()

    # ---------- 复议 ----------
    async def step_reopen(self, mtg):
        feedback = str(mtg.state().get("reopen_feedback") or "").strip()
        if not feedback:
            return advance()
        mtg.set_state(reopen_feedback="")
        speaker = "评审意见" if mtg.state().get("feedback_kind") == "reroute" else "用户"
        store.add_turn(mtg.meeting_id, round=mtg.meeting_round(), phase="reopen", speaker_id=None,
                       speaker_name=speaker, kind="user_feedback", content=feedback)
        result = await speak(
            mtg, _facilitator(mtg), kind="reopen", turn_kind="reopen_plan", charter=_charter(mtg),
            segments=[render.ledger_segment(_points(mtg), round_no=mtg.meeting_round()),
                      Segment("append", f"## 需要处理的反馈\n{feedback}", name="反馈")],
            instruction=REOPEN_INSTRUCTION, structured=_validate_reopen,
            turn_round=mtg.meeting_round())
        by_index = {point["index"]: point for point in _points(mtg)}
        number = len(by_index) + 1
        for row in result["data"]["points"]:
            index = str(row.get("index") or "").strip()
            if index in by_index:                     # 已有的决策点：置为复议中
                store.update_decision_point(by_index[index]["id"], status="reopened",
                                            conclusion="", closed_round=None)
            elif index:
                continue                              # 账本里没有的编号：不计入
            else:                                     # 反馈带出了账本里没有的问题：新建
                store.upsert_decision_point(
                    mtg.meeting_id, f"D{number}", title=str(row.get("title") or "").strip(),
                    question=str(row.get("question") or ""),
                    options_json=[str(option) for option in (row.get("options") or [])],
                    raised_by=speaker, origin_round=mtg.meeting_round() + 1)
                number += 1
        following = mtg.meeting_round() + 1
        store.update_meeting(mtg.meeting_id, round=following)
        mtg.set_state(stance_snapshot={}, exchange_done=[], exchanges={}, stall=0,
                      consensus_reason=None, rounds_limit=None)
        mtg.reset_builder()
        await _ledger_updated(mtg)
        return advance("stances")

    # ---------- 成稿与复核 ----------
    async def step_draft(self, mtg):
        previous = _artifact_body(mtg.run_id, "proposal")
        segments = [render.ledger_segment(_points(mtg), round_no=mtg.meeting_round()),
                    *_positions(mtg)]
        if previous:
            segments.append(Segment("append", f"## 上一版建设方案\n{previous}", name="上一版方案"))
        result = await speak(
            mtg, _facilitator(mtg), kind="proposal", turn_kind="proposal", charter=_charter(mtg),
            segments=segments, turn_round=mtg.meeting_round(),
            instruction=REVISION_INSTRUCTION if previous else PROPOSAL_INSTRUCTION)
        self._save_proposal(mtg, strip_outer_fence(result["content"]))
        return advance()

    def _save_proposal(self, mtg, draft: str, notes: str = "") -> dict:
        """程序在方案末尾附上决策点索引与待确认问题，然后落盘并更新纪要。"""
        points = _points(mtg)
        body = (draft.strip() + "\n\n" + _index_section(points) + "\n"
                + _pending_section(mtg, points))
        issues = validate_artifact("proposal", body)
        if issues:
            raise NodeFailure("建设方案校验未通过：" + "；".join(issues))
        record = write_artifact(mtg.run_id, "proposal", body, created_by="facilitator",
                                inputs=["idea", "discussion"], status="draft")
        self._write_minutes(mtg, notes)
        mtg.set_state(proposal_version=record["version"])
        return record

    def _write_minutes(self, mtg, notes: str = "") -> dict:
        extra = [("复核遗留意见", notes)] if notes else []
        return minutes.write_minutes(
            mtg.run_id, kind="discussion", created_by="facilitator", inputs=["idea"],
            meeting=mtg.meeting(), participants=mtg.participants(), points=_points(mtg),
            turns=store.list_turns(mtg.meeting_id, limit=100_000), extra_sections=extra)

    async def step_check(self, mtg):
        state = mtg.state()
        check_round = int(state.get("check_round") or 1)
        limit = max(1, int(mtg.params().get("proposal_check_rounds") or 2))
        wanted = set(state.get("check_pending") or [])
        members = [member for member in mtg.participants("participant")
                   if not wanted or member["id"] in wanted]
        view = [render.ledger_segment(_points(mtg), round_no=mtg.meeting_round()),
                Segment("append", "## 建设方案（待复核）\n"
                        + _artifact_body(mtg.run_id, "proposal"), name="建设方案")]
        results = await speak_many(mtg, [
            {"participant": member, "kind": "proposal_check", "turn_kind": "proposal_check",
             "charter": _charter(mtg), "segments": view, "instruction": CHECK_INSTRUCTION,
             "structured": _validate_check, "turn_round": check_round} for member in members])
        flagged = [(member, result["data"]) for member, result in zip(members, results)
                   if result["data"]["ok"] is False]
        if not flagged:
            return self._finish(mtg)
        notes = "\n".join(
            f"- {member['display_name']}（{issue.get('index') or '未标注决策点'}）："
            f"{issue.get('problem') or ''}"
            for member, data in flagged for issue in data.get("issues") or [])
        if check_round >= limit:                      # 复核轮次用完：遗留意见记进纪要，交给方案确认
            self._write_minutes(mtg, notes)
            mtg.set_state(review_notes=notes, check_pending=[])
            return self._finish(mtg)
        result = await speak(
            mtg, _facilitator(mtg), kind="proposal_revise", turn_kind="proposal_revise",
            charter=_charter(mtg), turn_round=check_round,
            segments=view + [Segment("append", f"## 复核意见\n{notes}", name="复核意见")],
            instruction=REVISE_INSTRUCTION)
        self._save_proposal(mtg, strip_outer_fence(result["content"]))
        mtg.set_state(check_round=check_round + 1,
                      check_pending=[member["id"] for member, _data in flagged])
        return advance("check")                      # 只让提出意见的人再复核一遍

    def _finish(self, mtg):
        points = _points(mtg)
        deferred = [point for point in points if point["status"] == DEFERRED]
        summary = (f"讨论会议完成：{len(points)} 个决策点，{len(deferred)} 个待确认，"
                   f"共 {mtg.meeting_round()} 轮")
        return done(summary, artifact=latest_artifact(mtg.run_id, "proposal"),
                    minutes=latest_artifact(mtg.run_id, "discussion"),
                    decision_points=len(points), deferred=len(deferred),
                    rounds=mtg.meeting_round())
```

说明几处设计上的取舍：

- **每轮结算在程序里做**（`_settle_round`）。模型只表态，了结与否由 `rules.settle` 按闭合规则算。
- **交锋的发言人是程序点的**：最支持的人主张，最反对的人质疑（`rank_speakers`），每个决策点每轮最多交锋 `exchanges_per_point` 次。
- **`advance("exchange")`、`advance("check")`、`advance("roster")` 这类写法是"下一步还是这一步"**：`MeetingProtocol.run` 按返回的 `next` 推进，写成同名就回到本步骤的开头，从而在一个步骤里循环。
- **每换一轮都调用 `mtg.reset_builder()`**：账本快照每轮更新，这是预期内的变化。
- **方案末尾的两节由程序生成**：`决策点索引` 与 `待确认问题`（含待定的决策点和没有出处的假设）。提示词里明确要求模型不要写这两节，避免重复。

### Task C5: Mock 供应商按调用标识路由

离线的 Mock 供应商原来靠系统提示词里的角色关键词猜该回什么，讨论流程一改就失效。现在会议里的每一次调用都带调用标识，Mock 改成按标识查表；文档类节点仍按关键词路由，并给需求规格补上追踪矩阵，供需求确认会议核对。对应详设 §14.1、§15.1。

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/scripts/mock_provider.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_mock_provider.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `_pick(messages, tools)` 先找提示里的调用标识 `【调用类型：<标识>】`，找到就交给 `_meeting_reply(标识, 提示全文)`；找不到再按原有的关键词规则路由
- Produces: `_ledger_rows(text: str) -> list[dict]`——从提示文本里读出决策点账本表格的各行（编号、状态、标题、候选项、结论）
- Produces: 需求规格的 Mock 回复末尾带 `## 需求追踪矩阵` 与 `## 非需求类决策` 两张表，来源决策点取自提示里的账本
- 不变：`_pm_plan`、开发角色的工具调用序列、设计、任务清单、changelog 的回复

评审、确认两类会议和裁决模型的调用标识，在实现它们的任务里往 `_meeting_reply` 里补。

- [x] **Step 1: 写失败的测试**

`backend/tests/test_mock_provider.py`：

```python
"""Mock 供应商：会议调用按调用标识路由；文档节点仍按系统提示关键词路由。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "scripts"))

from mock_provider import _ledger_rows, _pick  # noqa: E402

LEDGER = """## 决策点账本（第 1 轮开始时的状态）

| 编号 | 状态 | 问题 | 候选项 | 结论 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| D1 | 已一致 | 首版的范围与入口 | 网页版 / 桌面版 | 网页版 |
| D2 | 讨论中 | 首版是否需要登录 | 需要 / 不需要 | — |
"""
UX = "## 你的角色\n你是讨论与评审中的用户体验视角。你关心：目标用户是谁。"


def _reply(kind: str, body: str = "", system: str = "") -> str:
    messages = [{"role": "system", "content": system},
                {"role": "user", "content": f"{body}\n\n【调用类型：{kind}】"}]
    text, call = _pick(messages, None)
    assert call is None
    return text


def test_stances_follow_the_ledger_and_yield_after_the_exchange() -> None:
    first = json.loads(_reply("stance", LEDGER + "\n" + UX))
    assert [item["index"] for item in first["stances"]] == ["D2"]      # 只对未了结的决策点表态
    assert first["stances"][0]["level"] == 5
    assert first["stances"][0]["choice"] == "不需要"
    again = json.loads(_reply("restance", LEDGER + "\n" + UX))
    assert again["stances"][0]["level"] == 1                           # 交锋之后让步
    summary = json.loads(_reply("exchange_summary", LEDGER))
    assert summary["summaries"][0]["index"] == "D2"


def test_roster_follows_the_headcount_and_picks_offered_roles() -> None:
    listing = ("## 角色库（可以从中挑选）\n- architect｜架构师｜技术可行性\n"
               "- ux｜用户体验｜任务路径\n")
    roster = json.loads(_reply("roster", listing + "\n请正好挑选 2 名。"))
    assert [row["role_key"] for row in roster["participants"]] == ["architect", "ux"]


def test_answers_match_the_number_of_questions() -> None:
    listing = "## 待澄清的问题\n1. 首版支持哪些平台？\n2. 是否需要登录？\n"
    answer = json.loads(_reply("answer", listing))
    assert len(answer["answers"]) == 2                                  # 不受输出纪律里的编号条目干扰


def test_proposal_is_written_from_the_ledger() -> None:
    text = _reply("proposal", LEDGER)
    assert "## 目标与成功标准" in text and "D1 首版的范围与入口：网页版" in text
    assert "待确认问题" not in text                                     # 这一节由程序生成


def test_untagged_document_nodes_still_route_by_keywords() -> None:
    messages = [{"role": "system", "content": "你是需求分析师。条目使用 EARS 句式。"},
                {"role": "user", "content": "方案与纪要\n" + LEDGER}]
    text, call = _pick(messages, None)
    assert call is None and "【调用类型：" not in text
    assert "## 需求追踪矩阵" in text and "| R1 | D1 |" in text
    assert "## 非需求类决策" in text


def test_ledger_rows_keep_the_latest_status_of_each_point() -> None:
    later = "\n（之后的纪要）\n| D2 | 已一致 | 首版是否需要登录 | 需要 / 不需要 | 需要 |\n"
    rows = _ledger_rows(LEDGER + later)
    assert [row["index"] for row in rows] == ["D1", "D2"]
    assert rows[1]["status"] == "已一致" and rows[1]["options"] == ["需要", "不需要"]
```

`backend/scripts/mock_provider.py`：

1. 文件头的文档字符串换成：

```python
r"""离线 OpenAI 兼容 Mock 服务（仅用于本地演示与端到端测试）。

启动：
    .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\mock_provider.py --port 8100

路由规则：
- 会议里的每一次调用，提示末尾都带一个调用标识（形如【调用类型：stance】），
  按标识查表回复，不靠提示词里的关键词猜；
- 文档节点按系统提示词里的角色关键词路由；
- 开发者角色模拟 write_file → write_file → run_command → finish 的工具调用序列，
  让平台无需真实 API Key 也能跑通完整的 SDLC 流程。

生产使用真实供应商时不需要启动本服务。
"""
```

2. 删除 `_idea_of`、`_discussion`、`_proposal`、`_discussion_response`、`_discussion_mod` 五个函数（它们服务的三方讨论和方案架构师节点已经不在模板里）。

3. `_requirement` 整个函数换成：

```python
def _requirement(messages: list[dict]) -> str:
    text = """# 需求规格：AM-Workflow W1

## 1. 范围

覆盖想法到交付的最小闭环，不含向量记忆、多 run 并行与容器化执行。

## 2. 功能需求

### R1 供应商配置

WHEN 用户打开供应商设置页 THE SYSTEM SHALL 展示已配置的供应商、协议、脱敏密钥与模型数量。

验收标准：
- Given 已存在一个 openai_chat 供应商，When 打开设置页，Then 密钥仅显示末四位。
- Given 填写非法 base_url，When 点击测试连接，Then 返回可读错误且不写入数据库。

### R2 创建运行

WHEN 用户提交想法并选择模板 THE SYSTEM SHALL 创建 run、固化节点并写入 idea 工件。

验收标准：
- Given 想法非空，When 提交，Then 返回 run 快照且状态为 running。
- Given 模板不存在，When 提交，Then 返回 400 与明确提示。

### R3 阶段推进与人工门

WHEN 节点产出工件且到达人工确认点 THE SYSTEM SHALL 暂停运行并等待决定。

验收标准：
- Given 方案确认点待处理，When 通过，Then 运行继续且门状态为 approved。
- Given 需求确认点待处理，When 填写反馈并驳回，Then 目标节点带着反馈重跑。

### R4 任务图与并行执行

WHEN 任务分解完成 THE SYSTEM SHALL 解析任务清单、校验依赖并按波次并行执行。

验收标准：
- Given 两个无依赖任务，When 进入执行阶段，Then 两个开发节点同时处于 running。
- Given 任务依赖成环，When 保存任务图，Then 返回 400 且不落库。

### R5 验证与交付

WHEN 开发节点全部完成 THE SYSTEM SHALL 运行验证命令并据退出码判定结果。

验收标准：
- Given 单元测试通过，When 执行验证节点，Then 节点 done 且记录输出。
- Given 测试失败，When 执行验证节点，Then 节点失败并进入回流或转人工。

## 3. 非功能需求

- 事件写入必须 append-only，SSE 断线可按 seq 补发；
- SQLite 使用 WAL 与单连接，SSE 生成器不得持有 session；
- 所有模型调用受 run 级 token 预算约束。
"""
    joined = "\n".join(str(m.get("content") or "") for m in messages)
    if "R6" in joined or "周报" in joined or "导出" in joined:
        text = text.replace("## 3. 非功能需求", """### R6 周报导出

WHEN 成员在反馈列表点击「导出周报」THE SYSTEM SHALL 生成当前筛选结果的 Markdown 周报并下载。

验收标准：
- Given 已筛选出至少一条反馈，When 点击导出周报，Then 下载文件包含主题聚类与优先级建议。
- Given 筛选结果为空，When 点击导出周报，Then 提示没有可导出的内容且不生成文件。

## 3. 非功能需求""")
    points = _ledger_rows(joined)
    if points:
        count = 6 if "### R6" in text else 5
        text += ("\n\n## 需求追踪矩阵\n\n| 需求编号 | 来源决策点 | 方案章节 | 优先级 |\n"
                 "|---|---|---|---|\n")
        text += "".join(f"| R{number} | {points[(number - 1) % len(points)]['index']} | 总体方案 | P1 |\n"
                        for number in range(1, count + 1))
        text += ("\n## 非需求类决策\n\n| 决策点 | 不产出需求的理由 |\n|---|---|\n"
                 "| （无） | 每个已了结的决策点都有对应的需求 |\n")
    return text
```

4. 在 `# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 开发工具序列` 这条分隔线之前插入：

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 会议调用
_CALL_TAG = re.compile(r"【调用类型：([a-z_]+)】")
_LEDGER_ROW = re.compile(
    r"^\|\s*(D\d+)\s*\|\s*([^|]+?)\s*\|\s*([^|]+?)\s*\|\s*([^|]*?)\s*\|\s*([^|]*?)\s*\|\s*$",
    re.M,
)
_OPEN_STATUSES = ("讨论中", "复议中")


def _ledger_rows(text: str) -> list[dict]:
    """从提示文本里读出决策点账本表格的各行：编号、状态、标题、候选项、结论。

    同一个决策点可能出现多次（账本快照、纪要）；以最后一次为准。
    """
    rows: dict[str, dict] = {}
    for match in _LEDGER_ROW.finditer(text):
        conclusion = match.group(5).strip()
        rows[match.group(1)] = {
            "index": match.group(1), "status": match.group(2).strip(),
            "title": match.group(3).strip(),
            "options": [option.strip() for option in match.group(4).split("/")
                        if option.strip()],
            "conclusion": "" if conclusion in ("—", "") else conclusion,
        }
    return list(rows.values())


def _persona(text: str) -> str:
    """取"## 你的角色"那一段的第一句，用来区分发言人。"""
    match = re.search(r"## 你的角色\n(.+)", text)
    return match.group(1).strip()[:40] if match else ""


def _stances(text: str, *, yielding: bool) -> str:
    """对每个未了结的决策点表态。用户体验视角对最后一个未了结的决策点持反对意见，交锋之后让步。"""
    rows = [row for row in _ledger_rows(text) if row["status"] in _OPEN_STATUSES]
    items = []
    for position, row in enumerate(rows):
        opposed = position == len(rows) - 1 and "体验" in _persona(text) and not yielding
        choice = (row["options"][-1] if opposed else row["options"][0]) if row["options"] else ""
        items.append({"index": row["index"], "level": 5 if opposed else 1,
                      "reason": "多人共用时需要能区分反馈是谁的" if opposed else "同意目前的结论",
                      "choice": choice})
    return json.dumps({"stances": items}, ensure_ascii=False)


def _proposal_from_ledger(rows: list[dict], *, revised: bool) -> str:
    lines = ["# 建设方案"]
    if revised:
        lines += ["", "## 修改说明", "", "- 按反馈重开的决策点已重新讨论，结论以账本为准。"]
    lines += ["", "## 目标与成功标准", "",
              "把想法落成首版可用的最小闭环；每个阶段都有可验证的产出。",
              "", "## 范围", "", "必做：反馈的提交、归类与导出。不做：实时协作、细粒度权限。",
              "", "## 总体方案", ""]
    lines += [f"- {row['index']} {row['title']}：{row['conclusion'] or '尚有分歧，待用户决定'}"
              f"（{row['status']}）" for row in rows] or ["- （账本里还没有决策点）"]
    lines += ["", "## 关键流程", "", "提交反馈 → 归类 → 导出。",
              "", "## 里程碑与验收", "", "第一周：最小闭环可用，验收以可复现的检查为准。",
              "", "## 风险与缓解", "", "- 范围蔓延：首版功能冻结。"]
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"


def _fallback(text: str) -> str:
    """没有专门准备内容的调用：给一份格式正确的通用文档。"""
    return (
        "# 说明\n\n## 背景\n\n这是一份由离线 Mock 生成的通用文档，"
        "用来让流程在没有真实模型时也能走下去。它不包含针对当前想法的具体判断。\n\n"
        "## 要点\n\n- 范围：只覆盖最小闭环，不做的部分明确列出；\n"
        "- 验收：每一步都有可复现的检查；\n"
        "- 风险：范围蔓延，以及关键决策没有回退方案；对应的缓解是首版功能冻结、关键决策写明回退路径。\n\n"
        "## 后续\n\n接入真实模型后，这份文档会由模型按节点要求撰写，内容以实际输入的工件与反馈为准。\n"
    )


def _meeting_reply(kind: str, text: str) -> str:
    """会议里的一次调用。按调用标识给出格式正确的回复，内容尽量贴着提示里的账本与问题。"""
    rows = _ledger_rows(text)
    open_rows = [row for row in rows if row["status"] in _OPEN_STATUSES]
    if kind == "brief":
        return json.dumps({
            "brief": "目标：把想法落成首版可用的最小闭环。用户：提出想法的人及其团队。"
                     "约束：首版范围要小、每一步可验证。假设：小团队内部使用。",
            "questions": ["首版必须支持哪些平台或入口？"]}, ensure_ascii=False)
    if kind == "answer":
        listing = text.split("## 待澄清的问题", 1)[-1]
        count = len(re.findall(r"^\d+\.\s", listing, re.M))
        return json.dumps({"answers": [{"text": "材料中没有说明", "source": ""}
                                       for _ in range(count)]}, ensure_ascii=False)
    if kind == "roster":
        wanted = re.search(r"挑选 (\d+)", text)
        offered = re.findall(r"^- ([a-z_]+)｜", text, re.M)
        preferred = [key for key in ("architect", "ux", "qa") if key in offered]
        picked = (preferred + [key for key in offered if key not in preferred])
        picked = picked[:int(wanted.group(1)) if wanted else 2]
        return json.dumps({"participants": [
            {"role_key": key, "reason": "覆盖想法涉及的一类主要风险"} for key in picked]},
            ensure_ascii=False)
    if kind == "position":
        return json.dumps({"must": ["每一步都可验证"], "risks": ["范围蔓延", "关键决策没有回退方案"],
                           "route": "先做能跑通的最小闭环", "questions": []}, ensure_ascii=False)
    if kind == "agenda":
        return json.dumps({"points": [
            {"title": "首版的范围与入口", "question": "首版做哪些功能、从哪里进入",
             "options": ["网页版最小闭环", "桌面版最小闭环"], "raised_by": "架构师"},
            {"title": "多人共用时如何区分反馈", "question": "首版是否需要登录",
             "options": ["需要", "不需要"], "raised_by": "用户体验"}]}, ensure_ascii=False)
    if kind in ("stance", "restance"):
        return _stances(text, yielding=kind == "restance")
    subject = re.search(r"决策点 (D\d+)「([^」]*)」", text)
    label = f"{subject.group(1)}「{subject.group(2)}」" if subject else "这个决策点"
    if kind == "claim":
        return f"我支持{label}目前的结论：它覆盖了最主要的场景，实现成本最低。"
    if kind == "challenge":
        return f"关于{label}，我担心它忽略了多人共用时的区分问题，建议补上最小的区分手段。"
    if kind == "respond":
        return f"这个质疑有道理。我调整立场：{label}按质疑方的建议补上最小区分手段。"
    if kind == "exchange_summary":
        return json.dumps({"summaries": [
            {"index": row["index"],
             "summary": f"围绕{row['title']}，一方主张成本最低的做法，另一方担心多人共用时的区分问题，"
                        "主张方同意补上最小区分手段。"} for row in open_rows]}, ensure_ascii=False)
    if kind == "ruling":
        return json.dumps({"rulings": [
            {"index": row["index"], "conclusion": row["options"][0] if row["options"] else "按最小闭环处理",
             "rationale": "在成本与风险之间取折中", "dissent": "有参会者认为应当采用另一种做法"}
            for row in open_rows]}, ensure_ascii=False)
    if kind == "reopen":
        last = rows[-1] if rows else None
        point = ({"index": last["index"], "title": "", "question": "", "options": []} if last
                 else {"index": "", "title": "反馈提出的新问题", "question": "", "options": []})
        return json.dumps({"points": [point], "note": "按反馈重开最近的一个决策点"}, ensure_ascii=False)
    if kind in ("proposal", "proposal_revise"):
        return _proposal_from_ledger(rows, revised=kind == "proposal_revise")
    if kind == "proposal_check":
        return '{"ok": true, "issues": []}'
    if kind == "summarize":
        return ("较早的发言围绕首版范围与是否登录展开：一方强调成本最低的最小闭环，"
                "另一方强调多人共用时要能区分反馈；结论以账本为准。")
    if kind == "intervention_ack":
        return "收到。我会在接下来的讨论里先处理这一点。"
    return _fallback(text)


```

5. `_pick` 整个函数换成：

```python
def _pick(messages: list[dict], tools: list[dict] | None) -> tuple[str, dict | None]:
    if tools:
        return "", _dev_tool_call(messages)
    text = "\n".join(str(m.get("content") or "") for m in messages)
    tagged = _CALL_TAG.search(text)
    if tagged:                                   # 会议里的调用：只按调用标识路由
        return _meeting_reply(tagged.group(1), text), None
    system = next((m.get("content") or "" for m in messages if m.get("role") == "system"), "")
    if "计划分解器" in system or "只输出一个 JSON 对象" in system:
        return _pm_plan(messages), None
    if "EARS" in system:
        return _requirement(messages), None
    if "任务清单必须使用规定行格式" in system:
        return _task_doc(messages), None
    if "编号章节" in system:
        return _design(messages), None
    if "changelog" in system.lower():
        return _changelog(messages), None
    return _fallback(text), None
```

## 阶段 D：评审协议

产出评审会议：评审者先写要点再独立评审，主席归类，执笔者回应，程序得出整体结论；文字级问题由执笔者修订并验证，决策级问题定点回流讨论会议。对应详设 §8。

### Task D1: 评审意见的规范形态与裁决规则

评审协议里由程序完成的两件事先做出来：把模型给的评审意见整理成规范形态并按规则降级；根据各条意见的分类和级别得出整体结论。顺便把建设方案末尾由程序生成的两节（决策点索引、待确认问题）从讨论协议里抽成共用模块——评审会议的修订环节也要用。

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/app/meeting/review_rules.py`
- Create: `backend/app/meeting/proposal.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/meeting/protocols/discussion.py`（改用共用的 `proposal.compose`）
- Test: `backend/tests/test_review_rules.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces（`review_rules.py`）：`SEVERITIES = ("blocker", "major", "minor")`、`CATEGORIES = ("decision", "wording")`；`normalize_issue(raw: dict) -> dict`；`blocking(issues, *, category=None, line="major") -> list[dict]`；`verdict(issues, *, blocking_line="major") -> str`（`accept`、`conditional`、`return`）；`feedback_text(issues, *, blocking_line="major") -> str`；`issue_lines(issues) -> list[str]`
- Produces（`proposal.py`）：`compose(draft, *, points, clarifications) -> str`、`index_section(points) -> str`、`pending_section(clarifications, points) -> str`

一条规范化的评审意见是下面这样的字典；`id`、`standing`、`response`、`disposition` 等字段由评审协议在流程中补上：

```json
{"severity": "blocker", "category": "decision", "location": "D2",
 "problem": "结论与账本不符", "suggestion": "按账本的结论改写"}
```

`backend/tests/test_review_rules.py`：

```python
"""评审意见的规范形态与裁决规则：整体结论由程序得出。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from app.meeting.proposal import compose
from app.meeting.review_rules import feedback_text, normalize_issue, verdict

DECISION = {"severity": "blocker", "category": "decision", "location": "D2",
            "problem": "结论与账本不符", "suggestion": "按账本的结论改写"}
WORDING = {"severity": "major", "category": "wording", "location": "4.2",
           "problem": "术语不统一", "suggestion": "统一为“反馈”"}


def test_issues_without_location_or_suggestion_are_downgraded() -> None:
    assert normalize_issue(DECISION) == DECISION
    vague = normalize_issue({**WORDING, "location": "", "suggestion": ""})
    assert vague["severity"] == "minor" and vague["category"] == "wording"
    unknown = normalize_issue({**WORDING, "severity": "critical", "category": "style"})
    assert unknown["severity"] == "minor" and unknown["category"] == "wording"


def test_verdict_follows_categories_and_severities() -> None:
    assert verdict([]) == "accept"
    assert verdict([normalize_issue(WORDING)]) == "conditional"
    assert verdict([normalize_issue(DECISION)]) == "return"
    assert verdict([normalize_issue({**WORDING, "severity": "minor"})]) == "accept"
    assert verdict([normalize_issue(WORDING)], blocking_line="blocker") == "accept"


def test_overruled_issues_do_not_count() -> None:
    overruled = {**normalize_issue(DECISION), "standing": False}
    assert verdict([overruled]) == "accept"


def test_feedback_for_the_discussion_lists_blocking_decision_issues() -> None:
    issues = [{**normalize_issue(DECISION), "id": "I1", "response": "同意复议"},
              {**normalize_issue(WORDING), "id": "I2"}]
    text = feedback_text(issues)
    assert "I1" in text and "[D2]" in text and "按账本的结论改写" in text
    assert "执笔者回应：同意复议" in text and "I2" not in text


def test_compose_appends_index_and_pending_questions() -> None:
    points = [{"index": "D1", "status": "agreed", "title": "范围", "conclusion": "网页版"},
              {"index": "D2", "status": "deferred", "title": "登录", "conclusion": "",
               "stances": {"1": {"name": "架构", "reason": "成本太高", "level": 5}}}]
    clarifications = [{"question": "支持哪些平台", "answer": "未知", "source": "assumption"}]

    body = compose("# 建设方案\n\n正文。", points=points, clarifications=clarifications)

    assert body.startswith("# 建设方案\n\n正文。\n\n## 决策点索引")
    assert "| D1 | 已一致 | 网页版 |" in body
    assert "- [ ] D2 登录：架构：成本太高" in body
    assert "- [ ] 假设：支持哪些平台——未知" in body
```

- [x] **Step 3: 写规则与共用的方案拼装**

`backend/app/meeting/review_rules.py`：

```python
"""评审意见的规范形态与裁决规则（详设 §8.3、§8.4）。

整体结论由程序根据各条意见的分类和级别得出；评审主席只负责归类，
以及对有分歧条目的定夺。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

SEVERITIES = ("blocker", "major", "minor")
CATEGORIES = ("decision", "wording")
SEVERITY_NAMES = {"blocker": "阻塞", "major": "重要", "minor": "提示"}
CATEGORY_NAMES = {"decision": "决策级", "wording": "文字级"}
VERDICT_NAMES = {"accept": "接受", "conditional": "有条件接受", "return": "退回"}
_RANK = {"blocker": 0, "major": 1, "minor": 2}


def normalize_issue(raw: dict) -> dict:
    """一条评审意见的规范形态。

    级别或分类不合法的，按最轻、最不动决策的方式处理（提示级、文字级）；
    缺位置或缺建议的意见降为提示级（详设 §8.3）。
    """
    severity = raw.get("severity") if raw.get("severity") in SEVERITIES else "minor"
    category = raw.get("category") if raw.get("category") in CATEGORIES else "wording"
    location = str(raw.get("location") or "").strip()
    suggestion = str(raw.get("suggestion") or "").strip()
    if not location or not suggestion:
        severity = "minor"
    return {"severity": severity, "category": category, "location": location,
            "problem": str(raw.get("problem") or "").strip(), "suggestion": suggestion}


def blocking(issues: list[dict], *, category: str | None = None,
             line: str = "major") -> list[dict]:
    """达到阻塞线、并且仍然成立的意见。category 给出时只看这一类。"""
    return [issue for issue in issues
            if issue.get("standing", True)
            and (category is None or issue["category"] == category)
            and _RANK[issue["severity"]] <= _RANK[line]]


def verdict(issues: list[dict], *, blocking_line: str = "major") -> str:
    """整体结论：退回、有条件接受、接受（详设 §8.4）。"""
    if blocking(issues, category="decision", line=blocking_line):
        return "return"
    if blocking(issues, category="wording", line=blocking_line):
        return "conditional"
    return "accept"


def feedback_text(issues: list[dict], *, blocking_line: str = "major") -> str:
    """给讨论会议的回流反馈：达到阻塞线的决策级意见，连同执笔者的回应。"""
    lines = []
    for issue in blocking(issues, category="decision", line=blocking_line):
        line = (f"- {issue.get('id', '')} [{issue['location']}] "
                f"{SEVERITY_NAMES[issue['severity']]}：{issue['problem']}。建议：{issue['suggestion']}")
        if issue.get("response"):
            line += f"（执笔者回应：{issue['response']}）"
        lines.append(line)
    return "\n".join(lines) or "（没有需要复议的决策级意见）"


def issue_lines(issues: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
    """报告里的"问题清单及处置状态"（Markdown 表格的各行）。"""
    lines = ["| 编号 | 级别 | 分类 | 位置 | 问题 | 处置 |", "|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]
    for issue in issues:
        state = "已否决" if not issue.get("standing", True) else issue.get("disposition", "—")
        lines.append(f"| {issue.get('id', '')} | {SEVERITY_NAMES[issue['severity']]} | "
                     f"{CATEGORY_NAMES[issue['category']]} | {issue['location'] or '—'} | "
                     f"{_cell(issue['problem'])} | {_cell(state)} |")
    return lines


def _cell(value) -> str:
    text = str(value or "").replace("|", "/").replace("\n", " ").strip()
    return text or "—"
```

`backend/app/meeting/proposal.py`：

```python
"""建设方案的程序部分：决策点索引与待确认问题由程序附在模型写的正文后面。

模型不写这两节（提示词里明确要求），所以它们永远完整、与账本一致。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from app.meeting import render
from app.meeting.rules import DEFERRED


def index_section(points: list[dict]) -> str:
    lines = ["## 决策点索引", "", "| 编号 | 状态 | 结论 |", "|---|---|---|"]
    lines += [f"| {point['index']} | {render.STATUS_NAMES.get(point['status'], point['status'])} | "
              f"{point.get('conclusion') or '—'} |" for point in points]
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"


def pending_section(clarifications: list[dict], points: list[dict]) -> str:
    """待确认问题：待定的决策点带着各方理由；没有出处的澄清结果按假设列出。"""
    items = []
    for point in points:
        if point["status"] != DEFERRED:
            continue
        reasons = "；".join(f"{stance.get('name')}：{stance.get('reason')}"
                           for stance in (point.get("stances") or {}).values()
                           if stance.get("reason"))
        items.append(f"- [ ] {point['index']} {point['title']}：{reasons or '各方未达成一致'}")
    for item in clarifications or []:
        if item.get("source") == "assumption":
            items.append(f"- [ ] 假设：{item['question']}——{item['answer']}")
    return "\n".join(["## 待确认问题"] + (items or ["（无）"])) + "\n"


def compose(draft: str, *, points: list[dict], clarifications: list[dict]) -> str:
    """模型写的正文 + 决策点索引 + 待确认问题。"""
    return (draft.strip() + "\n\n" + index_section(points) + "\n"
            + pending_section(clarifications, points))
```

`backend/app/meeting/protocols/discussion.py`：

1. 导入区的 `from app.meeting import minutes, render, store` 改成 `from app.meeting import minutes, proposal, render, store`。
2. 删除 `_index_section` 和 `_pending_section` 两个函数（它们搬进了 `proposal.py`）。
3. `_save_proposal` 整个方法换成：

```python
    def _save_proposal(self, mtg, draft: str, notes: str = "") -> dict:
        """程序在方案末尾附上决策点索引与待确认问题，然后落盘并更新纪要。"""
        points = _points(mtg)
        body = proposal.compose(draft, points=points,
                                clarifications=mtg.state().get("clarifications") or [])
        issues = validate_artifact("proposal", body)
        if issues:
            raise NodeFailure("建设方案校验未通过：" + "；".join(issues))
        record = write_artifact(mtg.run_id, "proposal", body, created_by="facilitator",
                                inputs=["idea", "discussion"], status="draft")
        self._write_minutes(mtg, notes)
        mtg.set_state(proposal_version=record["version"])
        return record
```

### Task D2: 评审协议

从这一任务起，计划不再给出完整代码：会议运行时、调用通道、讨论协议已经立好了范式，照 `protocols/discussion.py` 的写法实现即可。这里写清步骤、数据、规则和必须覆盖的测试场景，代码与测试由执行本任务的会话完成。对应详设 §8。

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/app/meeting/protocols/review.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/meeting/store.py`（加 `latest_meeting(run_id: str, protocol: str) -> dict | None`：一个运行里最近的一场某类会议）
- Modify: `backend/app/meeting/render.py`（`TURN_LABELS` 补齐评审类发言的显示名；`turn_text` 把 `review`、`review_delta` 渲染成"优点 / 问题清单 / 上一轮意见的处理结果"，其余带 `structured` 的发言输出缩进的 JSON）
- Modify: `backend/scripts/mock_provider.py`（`_meeting_reply` 补本任务的调用标识）
- Test: `backend/tests/test_review_protocol.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `reroute_or_escalate`（任务 B3）；`review_rules`、`proposal.compose`（任务 D1）
- Produces: 协议名 `review`；参会者模式 `chair`（评审主席）、`reviewer`（评审者）、`author`（执笔者）、`proxy`（需求方代理，可选）
- Produces: 会议状态里的字段 `issues`、`verdict`、`precheck_ok` 所需的 `accepted_by_user` 与 `unfixed`、`awaiting_rework`、`ledger_snapshot`
- Produces: 事件 `review_round_started`（会议编号、轮次、是否增量复审、轮次上限）、`review_verdict`（会议编号、轮次、结论、阻塞数、意见数）
- Produces: 节点完成时结果里带 `verdict`、`precheck_ok`、`precheck_reason`、`accepted_by_user`、`report`（评审工件的记录）、`issues`、`blocking`
- Produces: 评审报告作为 `review` 工件的新版本；方案被修订时 `proposal` 工件出新版本

**步骤**：`steps = ("intake", "prepare", "points", "review", "merge", "respond", "discuss", "verdict", "revise", "verify", "report")`

| 步骤 | 执行者 | 做什么 | 调用标识 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `intake` | 评审主席 | 没有反馈就跳过。带着反馈续开时，把反馈写成一条用户发言，再整理成评审意见，然后直接去 `respond` | `review_intake` |
| `prepare` | 主席 | 方案工件不存在则节点失败。建参会者、定检查单、写章程、发 `review_round_started` | `review_brief` |
| `points` | 评审者并行 | 参数 `pre_brief` 为真时，只读想法、简报、账本（这次调用不带方案），写"我会重点查什么" | `review_points` |
| `review` | 评审者并行 | 第 1 轮独立评审；第 2 轮起增量复审 | `review`、`review_delta` |
| `merge` | 主席 | 去重，定级别与分类，标注提出者与是否有分歧 | `review_merge` |
| `respond` | 执笔者 | 对每条仍然成立、还没回应的意见表态；可以向需求方代理提问 | `author_response`、`answer` |
| `discuss` | 提出者、主席 | 参数 `conference` 为真时，只处理有分歧的和被执笔者反驳的：提出者辩护一段，主席逐条定夺 | `review_defend`、`review_rule` |
| `verdict` | 程序 | 用 `review_rules.verdict` 得出结论并发事件；退回时发起回流 | — |
| `revise` | 执笔者 | 按仍然成立且被采纳的文字级意见修订方案，出新版本 | `author_revise` |
| `verify` | 主席 | 逐条确认改好没有；没改好的退回修订，至多再一轮 | `revision_verify` |
| `report` | 程序 | 写评审报告 | — |

**规则：**

1. **参会者。** `chair` 用角色 `review_chair`。`author` 取讨论会议里模式为 `facilitator` 的那一行，没有就用角色 `facilitator`。评审者：运行级配置 `rosters.reviewers.role_keys` 点名的照用；否则沿用讨论会议的参会角色，`rosters.reviewers.count` 或参数 `reviewers_max` 给了上限就截取；讨论会议没有留下参会角色时，从角色库里请元数据标了适合评审的前三个。一个都没有，或少于 `reviewers_min`，节点失败并写明原因。讨论会议有需求方代理时一并请来。
2. **每一步在一轮里只做一次。** 做完在会议状态里记 `done[步骤名] = flow`，进入一步前先看这个标记。带着反馈续开（`on_enter` 取到反馈）或从等待返工恢复（`awaiting_rework` 为真）时 `flow` 加一，并清空 `reviews`、`verdict`、`accepted_by_user`、`unfixed`、`discussed`；评审意见跨轮保留。后一种情况把会议的当前步骤设回 `prepare`。
3. **意见。** 每条是一个字典：`id`（I1、I2……接着已有的最大编号往下排）、`severity`、`category`、`location`、`problem`、`suggestion`（后五个来自 `review_rules.normalize_issue`），以及 `raised_by`、`disputed`、`standing`、`accepted`、`response`、`disposition`、`origin`（`reviewer` 或 `user`）、`round`、`dissent`。全部存在会议状态的 `issues` 里。
4. **汇总。** 主席给出的意见规范化后入库，此时 `standing=True`、`accepted=None`。增量复审的输出带 `resolved`：上一轮的决策级意见据此标为"已处理"（`standing=False`）或"未处理"（仍然成立）。
5. **回应。** 只处理 `accepted is None` 且 `standing` 的意见，输出必须恰好覆盖这些编号，不采纳必须写理由。回应里带了 `ask_requester` 且会议里有需求方代理时，把问题交给代理（出处规则同讨论协议），再让执笔者只对提过问题的那几条回应一次。
6. **讨论。** 目标是有分歧的（`disputed`）和被执笔者反驳的（`accepted is False`）意见，每条只讨论一次。主席定夺后：`standing` 取 `keep`；成立的意见 `accepted=True`、处置记"主席定夺：成立"，被否的记"主席定夺：否决"；级别与分类按主席给的重新规范化；`dissent` 原样保留。
7. **裁决。** `verdict(issues, blocking_line=params.min_blocking_severity)`。结论不是退回就往下走。是退回时调用 `reroute_or_escalate(mtg, ["discuss"], 反馈, title=..., description=...)`，反馈是"评审会议要求复议以下决策级问题："加 `review_rules.feedback_text(...)`。返回 `None` 表示人在确认点上接受了现状：记 `accepted_by_user=True`，往下走。返回回流指令：记 `awaiting_rework=True` 和当时的账本快照 `ledger_snapshot`，把指令原样返回。
8. **修订。** 退回且没有被人工接受时不修订（方案由讨论会议复议之后修订）。其余情况收集"仍然成立、被采纳、文字级、处置不是已修订并验证"的意见；有就让执笔者修订，方案用 `proposal.compose` 拼好、校验、落盘（`created_by="author"`），给这些意见记下 `revised_round`，然后重写章程并 `mtg.reset_builder()`（章程里换成修订后的方案）。
9. **验证。** 对这一轮修订过的意见调用主席，输出必须恰好覆盖这些编号。改好的处置记"已修订并验证"，没改好的记"未改好"。还有没改好的且这是第一次验证，就让执笔者只对这几条再修订一次、再验证一次；之后不再重试，把没改好的编号记进 `unfixed`。
10. **前置检查的结论。** `precheck_ok` 为真的条件：结论是接受或有条件接受（或者人接受了现状），并且没有处置为"未改好"的意见。不为真时 `precheck_reason` 写明是哪一种原因。
11. **章程。** 每一轮的 `prepare` 重写一次，章节依次是：被评审的建设方案（当前版本）、决策点账本、（第 2 轮起）上一轮的决策级意见与处理情况、改动的决策点（与 `ledger_snapshot` 比较，结论变了的）、检查单、参与者。背景材料同讨论会议。
12. **受理。** 用户的驳回意见整理成 `origin="user"`、`raised_by=["用户"]` 的意见，走后面的回应、讨论、裁决。

**调用的输出格式**（只输出一个 JSON 对象；不合格的按结构化输出错误处理，见任务 B6）：

| 调用标识 | 输出 | 校验 |
|---|---|---|
| `review_intake` | `{"issues": [{"severity", "category", "location", "problem", "suggestion"}]}` | 至少一条；每条有 `problem` |
| `review_brief` | `{"checklist": ["检查项"]}` | 非空字符串数组 |
| `review_points` | `{"focus": ["要点"]}` | 非空字符串数组 |
| `review` | `{"strengths": ["优点"], "issues": [同上，每条另含 `severity`、`category`]}` | `strengths` 是字符串数组；意见每条有 `problem` |
| `review_delta` | 同 `review`，另有 `"resolved": [{"id": "I1", "handled": true, "note": ""}]` | 同上 |
| `review_merge` | `{"issues": [{…同上…, "raised_by": ["评审者"], "disputed": false}]}` | 可以是空数组；每条有 `problem` |
| `author_response` | `{"responses": [{"id", "accepted": true, "reason", "ask_requester": ["问题"]}]}` | 恰好覆盖给它的编号；`accepted` 是布尔值；不采纳必须有 `reason` |
| `answer` | `{"answers": [{"text", "source"}]}` | 与问题逐条对应 |
| `review_defend` | 一段话 | — |
| `review_rule` | `{"rulings": [{"id", "keep": true, "category", "severity", "note", "dissent"}]}` | 恰好覆盖；`keep` 是布尔值；`note` 非空 |
| `author_revise` | 完整的新版方案（Markdown） | 作为工件校验 |
| `revision_verify` | `{"verified": [{"id", "fixed": true, "note"}]}` | 恰好覆盖；`fixed` 是布尔值 |

`severity` 取 `blocker`、`major`、`minor`，`category` 取 `decision`、`wording`；不合法的由 `normalize_issue` 处理。

**报告**：用 `minutes.write_minutes(kind="review", created_by="chair", inputs=["proposal"], ...)` 写，附加几节：评了什么、谁评的、优点、结论（"结论：接受。"这样的句式；人工接受了现状、有没改好的意见时各加一句说明）、问题清单及处置状态（`review_rules.issue_lines`）、保留的异议。

**测试**（照 `tests/test_discussion_protocol.py` 的写法：`_replies()` 给一套脚本回复并按需覆盖，`drive()` 驱动；准备一个已经开完讨论会议的运行，手写方案 v1，讨论会议里放主持人和两名参会者、两个已了一致的决策点）：

| 场景 | 要断言的 |
|---|---|
| 评审者没有提问题 | 结论 `accept`；`precheck_ok` 为真；执笔者一次都没被调用；报告里有"结论：接受。"和评审者列的优点 |
| 两条文字级重要意见，执笔者采纳 | 结论 `conditional`；方案出了第 2 版且带"## 修改说明"；意见的处置是"已修订并验证"；`author_revise`、`revision_verify` 各调用一次 |
| 决策级阻塞意见 | 返回的回流指令目标是 `discuss`，反馈里带着修改建议；会议状态是 `waiting_rework`。用编排器的 `_handle_reroute` 走一遍，再改掉账本里对应的结论、写出方案第 2 版，然后重新驱动：结论 `accept`，还是同一场会议，增量复审被调用了两次，章程里有"上一轮的决策级意见与处理情况"和"改动的决策点"两节 |
| 执笔者反驳，主席判成立 | 意见仍然成立且被采纳，处置是"主席定夺：成立"，`dissent` 保留；结论 `conditional`；报告的"保留的异议"里有这条 |
| 执笔者反驳，主席判否决 | 意见不再成立，处置是"主席定夺：否决"；结论 `accept` |
| 修订一直没改好 | 结论 `conditional` 但 `precheck_ok` 为假，`precheck_reason` 说明有意见没改好；`author_revise` 恰好两次 |
| 轮次已用完仍有决策级意见 | 只出现一个 `escalation` 确认点；人通过后结论仍是 `return`，但 `precheck_ok` 为真、`accepted_by_user` 为真，报告里写明接受了现状 |
| 用户在评审裁决确认点上驳回 | 驳回意见成为一条 `origin` 是 `user` 的意见，被执笔者采纳并修订；报告出了第 2 版 |
| 执笔者向需求方代理提问 | 代理被调用一次，发言人是需求方代理；执笔者回应两次 |

**Mock**：`review_brief` 回三条检查项；`review_points` 回一条要点；`review` 回"有优点、没有问题"；`review_delta` 回"没有问题、`resolved` 为空"；`review_merge`、`author_response`、`review_rule`、`revision_verify` 回空数组；`review_defend` 回一段话；`author_revise` 回带修改说明的方案（复用 `_proposal_from_ledger`）；`review_intake` 回一条文字级意见。

## 阶段 E：需求确认协议

产出需求确认会议：程序核对追踪矩阵，语义核对者先凭账本写预期再对照实际条目，质量评审者按需求质量特性检查，程序汇总裁决；不通过时定点回流需求分析师。对应详设 §9。

### Task E1: 追踪矩阵的解析与机械核对

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/app/meeting/confirm_rules.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_confirm_rules.py`

**Interfaces（全是纯函数）：**
- `parse_requirements(text) -> list[dict]`：需求条目 `{"id": "R1", "title": "…", "body": "…"}`。条目的标题形如 `### R1 标题`（2 到 4 级标题），正文到下一条需求或下一个同级及以上的标题为止。
- `parse_matrix(text) -> list[dict]`：`## 需求追踪矩阵` 下的表格行 `{"requirement": "R1", "points": ["D1"], "section": "…", "priority": "P1"}`。只读前四列；列的顺序以表头为准。
- `parse_non_requirements(text) -> list[str]`：`## 非需求类决策` 的表格里出现的决策点编号。
- `closed_points(points: list[dict]) -> list[dict]`：状态是已一致或主持人裁决的决策点。
- `mechanical_check(text: str, points: list[dict]) -> list[str]`：下面七条规则，返回逐条的说明。
- `confirm_verdict(items: list[dict], *, blocking_line: str = "major") -> str`：`pass` 或 `fail`。
- `changed_requirements(before: dict, after: dict) -> list[str]`：正文变了的需求数字编号。入参是 `{编号: 正文}`。

**机械核对的七条规则**（详设 §9.2）：

1. 没有解析出需求条目；或者需求编号重复。
2. 有需求不在追踪矩阵里。
3. 矩阵里的行没有来源决策点；来源决策点不存在，或者还没有了结；优先级不是 P1、P2、P3 之一。
4. 已了结的决策点既没有任何需求引用，也没有列进非需求类决策。
5. 某条需求的正文里出现"待定"。
6. 某条需求没有 EARS 句式（`WHEN … THE SYSTEM SHALL`）。
7. 某条需求没有 Given/When/Then 验收标准。

每条说明都要能定位：写明需求编号或决策点编号，例如"R4 没有出现在追踪矩阵里"、"决策点 D2 已了结，但既没有需求引用它，也没有列入非需求类决策"。

**汇总裁决**：清单里的一条是 `{"id": "C1", "kind": "mechanical | distorted | missing | added | quality", "requirement": "R3", "index": "D2", "severity": "blocker | major | minor", "problem": "…", "suggestion": "…"}`。以下任一情况为不通过：有机械核对的条目；有走样、遗漏或凭空新增的条目；有达到阻塞线的质量评审条目。其余通过。
